H160. ahabah

H212. ophan

=== H0319 ===
H319. acharith

אַחֲרִית noun feminine after-part, end; —

a. of place, only Psalm Eccl 139:9 (late) ׳א יָם.

b. of time, latter part or actual close (according to context), opposed to רֵאשִׁית; — of year Deut 11:12; of a man's life Num 23:10; Prov 5:11; Job 8:7; 42:12; of a people's existence Num 24:20; = final lot Deut 32:20, 29; Jer 12:4; 31:17; Psa 73:17; a future, i.e. a happy close of life, suggesting sometimes the idea of a posterity, promised to the righteous Prov 23:18 (|| תִּקְוָה hope) 24:14; Jer 29:11 (א לָכֶם ׳לָתֵת וְתִקְוָה), withheld from the wicked Prov 24:20 (|| ׃ יִדְעָֽח נֵר ׃ see below); the end or ultimate issue of a course of action Jer 5:31; Prov 14:12; 23:32 (of wine, i.e. of indulgence in it) 25:8; Isa 46:10 (absolute, but implicitly of a phase of history) 47:7 (of the conduct described 47:6 b-7 a) Dan 12:8; Eccl 7:8; of a prediction = the event Isa 41:22. הַיָּמִים בְּאַחֲרִית in the end of the days, a prophetic phrase denoting the final period of the history so far as the speaker's perspective reaches; the sense thus varies with the context, but it often = the ideal or Messianic future; Gen 49:1 (of the period of Israel's possession of Canaan) Num 24:14; Deut 4:30 (of the period of Israel's return to God after adversity) 31:29 (of the period of Israel's rebellion) Hosea 3:5; Isa 2:2 ( = Micah 4:1) Jer 23:20 (see Graf) = 30:24; 48:47; 49:39; Ezek 38:16 (of the period of Gog's attack upon restored Israel) Dan 2:28 (Aramaic) ⟦N100⟧ 10:14 (of the age of Antiochus Epiphanes): compare ׳כְּא הַשָּׁנִים Ezek 38:8.

c. ׳א הַגּוֺיִם Jer 50:12 the last, hindermost of the nations (of Babylon), opposed to הגוים ראשׁית Amos 6:1 (Israel) compare Num 24:20 (Amalek) chief of the nations.

H343. ed

1 national calam. of Israel (apostate) Jer 18:17; Ezek 35:5; Obadiah 13 (3 t. in verse); of Egypt Jer 46:21; Moab 48:16; Edom (׳א עֵשָׂו) 49:8; Hazor 49:32.

2 of righteous sufferer 2Sam 22:19 = Psa 18:19 compare ׳א אֵל i.e. from God Job 31:23 & אֵידָם אָרְחוֺת 30:12 their calamitous paths (suffix reference to bereavement, pain, etc.)

3 often of wicked Job 18:12; 21:17; 31:3; Prov 1:26-27, (wisd. mocks at; || מַּחַד) 6:15; 24:22; also א ׳יוֺם Deut 32:35; Job 21:30 (compare above ⟦N146⟧ 2Sam 22:19 = Psa 18:19; Prov 27:10; Jer 18:17; 46:21; Obadiah 13 (3 t. in verse) where || צָרה יוֺם אֹבֶד יוֺם 46:12; 46:14, compare יהוה יוֺם 46:15).

=== H0403 ===
H403. aken

אָכֵן (perhaps from אַח; compare Aramaic דֵּח & דִּכֵּן)

adverb with strong asseverative force:

a. surely, truly, especially at beginning of a speech (stronger & more decided than אַח) ⟦N026⟧ Gen 28:16; Exod 2:14; 1Sam 15:32; Isa 40:7; 45:15; Jer 3:23 (twice in verse); 4:10; 8:8. In 1Kin 11:2 אָבֵן stands unusually; and מֶּןֿ (compare ᵑ7 ᵑ6 ᵐ5) should probably be read (so Klo).

b. emphasizing a contrast, but indeed, but in fact, especially after אָמַרְתִּי I said or thought, expressing the reality, in opposed to what had been wrongly imagined, Isa 49:4b (opposed to v^a) 53:4 (opposed to 53:3 end) Jer 3:20 (opposed to the expectation 3:19 b) Zeph 3:7b Psa 31:23b (opposed to 31:23a) 66:19; 82:7 (opposed to 82:6) Job 32:8 (opposed to 32:7).

H430. elohim

אֱלֹהִים noun masculine plural (feminine 1Kin 11:33; on number of occurrences of אֱלֹהִים אֱלוֺהַּ, אֵל, compare also Nes^l. c,)

1 plural in number.

a. rulers, judges, either as divine representatives at sacred places or as reflecting divine majesty and power: האלהים Exod 21:6 (Onk ᵑ6, but τὸ κριτήριον τοῦ Θεοῦ ᵐ5) 22:7; 22:8; אלהים 22:8; 22:27 (ᵑ7 Ra AE Ew RVm; but gods, ᵐ5 Josephus Philo AV; God, Di RV; all Covt. code of E) compare 1Sam 2:25 see Dr.; Judg 5:8 (Ew, but gods ᵐ5; God ᵑ6 BarHebr.; ᵑ9 יהוה Be) Psa 82:1; 82:6 (De Ew Pe; but angels Bl Hup) 138:1 (ᵑ7 ᵑ6 Rab Ki De; but angels ᵐ5 Calv; God, Ew; gods, Hup Pe Che).

b. divine ones, superhuman beings including God and angels Psa 8:6 (De Che Br; but angels ᵑ7 ᵑ6 ᵐ5 Ew; God, RV and most moderns) Gen 1:27 (if with Philo ᵑ7 Jerome De Che we interpret נעשה as God's consultation with angels; compare Job 38:7).

c. angels Psalm Job 97:7 (ᵑ6 ᵐ5 Calv; but gods, Hup De Pe Che); compare (ה)אלהים בני = (the) sons of God, or sons of gods = angels Job 1:6; 2:1; 38:7; Gen 6:2, 4 (J; so ᵐ5 Books of Enoch & Jubilees Philo Jude Judg 1:6 2Pet 2:4 Jos^Ant. i. 3. 1, most ancient fathers and modern critics; against usage are sons of princes, mighty men, Onk and Rab.; sons of God, the pious, Theod Chrys Jerome Augustine Luther Calv Hengst; ᵐ5^L read οἱ υιὁὶ τοῦ Θεοῦ), compare אלים בני.

d. gods האלהים Exod 18:11; 22:19 (E) 1Sam 4:8; 2Chr 2:4; Psa 86:8; האלהים אלהי the God of gods, supreme God Deut 10:17; Psa 136:2; אלהים Exod 32:1, 23 (JE) Judg 9:13; אחרים אלהים other gods Exod 20:3; 23:13; Josh 24:2, 16 (E) Deut 31:18, 20 (JE) 5:7 + (17 t. in D, not P) Judg 2:12, 17, 19; 10:13; 1Sam 8:8; 26:19; 1Kin 9:6, 9 (= 2Chr 7:19, 22) 11:4, 10; 14:9; 2Kin 5:17; 17:35, 37, 38; 22:17 (= 2Chr 34:25) 28:25; Jer 1:16 + (18 t. Jeremiah) Hosea 3:1; (ה)נכר אלהי foreign gods Gen 35:2, 4; Josh 24:20, 23 (E) Deut 31:16 (JE) Judg 10:16; 1Sam 7:3; 2Chr 33:15; Jer 5:19; ׳א נחור Gen 31:53 (E); ׳א מצרים Exod 12:12 (P) Jer 43:12-13, ׳א האמרי Josh 24:15 (E) Judg 6:10; ׳א ארם etc. 10:6; ידי מעשה אלהים Deut 4:28; ׳א הגוים gods of the nations 2Kin 18:33; 19:12; Deut 29:17; 2Chr 32:17, 19; Isa 36:18; 37:12; ׳א העמים Deut 6:14; 13:8; Judg 2:12; Psa 96:5; 1Chr 5:25; 16:26; 2Chr 32:13-14, ׳א כסף Exod 20:23 (E); ׳א זהב 20:23 (E) ⟦N052⟧ 32:31 (JE); ׳א מַסֵּכָה 34:17 (J) Lev 19:4 (H).

2 Plural intensive.

a. god or goddess, always with suffix 1Sam 5:7 (Dagon), Judg 11:24 (Chemosh), 1Kin 18:24 (Baal), Judg 9:27; Dan 1:2 (twice in verse); or construct ׳א א לכמוש ׳צדנים מואב לעשתרת to Ashtoreth goddess of the Zidonians, Chemosh god of Moab, etc. 1Kin 11:33; ׳א הארץ god of the land 2Kin 17:26 (twice in verse); ⟦N148⟧ 17:27, and so the Syrians suppose that Yahweh is a mountain-god and not a god of valleys 1Kin 20:28.

b. godlike one Exod 4:16 (J; Moses in relation to Aaron), 7:1 (P; in relation to Pharaoh), 1Sam 28:13 (the shade of Samuel), Psa 45:7 (the Messianic king, O God, ᵑ6 ᵐ5 Jerome, most scholars ancient and modern, but thy throne is God's = God's throne AE Ki Thea Ew Hup, compare 1Chr 28:5). c. works of God, or things specially belonging to him (see אֵל 5) אלהים הר Psa 68:16; Ezek 28:14, 16; אלהים אש Job 1:16; (ה)אלהים גן Ezek 28:13; 31:8-9,. d. God (see 3 & 4).

H517. em

H520. ammah

II. אַמָּה noun feminine ell, cubit (SI אמה; so Sabean DHM^ZMG 1865, 613; Aramaic , אַמָּא; Assyrian ammatu Nor^280; Ethiopic etymology dubious; Thes and others mater brachii, i.e. length of fore-arm; others derive from √ אמם, precede, be in front, & hence fore-arm compare Di Isa 6:4; Dl^Pr 109 MV derive immediately from √אמם be wide (see above), אַמָּה = distance, & hence a particular distance, ell, cubit) — ׳א absolute Gen 6:15 +; construct אַמַּת Deut 3:11; Jer 51:13; dual אַמָּתַיִם Exod 25:10 +; plural אַמּוֺת 26:16 +; —

1 cubit, so אַמַּתאִֿישׁ i.e. ordinary cubit, Deut 3:11 (compare Isa 8:1); in Ezek 40:5; 43:13 is a cubit one hand-breadth longer, compare 2Chr 3:3 ׳א הָרִאשׁוֺנָה בַּמִּחָה; absolute length dubious, compare Smith^Dict. Bib., article Weights and Measures; Lepsius^Längenmaasse der Alten, SBAk 1883, 1195 f. identification with Egyptian, longer cubit. 525m., shorter .450m.; see also Id.^Babylonian-Assyr. Längenmaasse 1877; MBAk 1877, 741 Oppert^GGA 1878, 1055; Rev. d'Assyr. i. 124 (also on the Babylonian 'half-cubit' = .270 m. Hpt^AJPh 1888, 419 Hom^Semiten i. 501); on ׳א אַצִּילָה Ezek 41:8 compare Sm & Co (Co strike out); קָנִים אמות חֲמֵשׁ 42:16 Kt, read מֵאוֺת so Qr Co; chiefly in Exodus 25-27. 36-38 (56 t.) 1Kings 6.. 7 (45 t.) 2 Chronicles 3.4 (21 t.) Ezekiel 40-43 (86 t.); a (one) cubit = אַמָּה Gen 6:16; Exod 25:10, 23 +; הָאַמָּה Ezek 43:14; ׳א אַחַת 40:12 (twice in verse); 40:42 (3 t. in verse); 42:4; 43:14; two cubits = אַמָּתַיִם Exod 25:10, 17, 23; 30:2; 37:1, 6, 10, 25; Num 11:31; אַמּוֺת שְׁתַּיִם Ezek 40:9; 41:3, 22; 43:14; with Numbers 1-10 ׳א mostly follows number in plural Exod 26:16; 27:1 (3 t. in verse); 1Kin 6:10; Ezek 40:5, 7, ⟦N046⟧ 9 +; with numbers 11-1000 +, it mostly following number in singular Gen 6:15 (3 t. in verse); Exod 27:12-13, 38:13-14, 15; Num 35:4; Josh 3:4; 1Kin 6:2 (twice in verse); 6:3; 7:15 (twice in verse); Jer 52:21-22, Ezek 40:49 (twice in verse) +; seldom following, in plural 40:11 (strike out Co) 40:27 (Co singular) 42:2; so also אַמּוֺת וְחָמֵשׁ עֶשְׂרִים 40:13, 29 but אַמָּה וְעֶשְׂרִים חָמֵשׁ 40:25, 30, 33, 36; (late) also in plural precedes all Numbers 2Chr 3:3 (twice in verse); 3:4, 8 (twice in verse) +; 6:13; Ezek 42:2; also often בָּאַמָּה following all numbers Exod 26:2 (twice in verse); 26:8 (twice in verse); 27:9, 18; 36:9 (twice in verse); 36:15 (twice in verse); 36:21; Num 35:5 (4 t. in verse); 1Kin 6:6 (3 t. in verse); 7:23 (3 t. in verse); 2Chr 4:2 (3 t. in verse); Ezek 40:21; 47:3; Zech 5:2 (twice in verse) +, compare חָמֵשׁ לְאַמּוֺת2Chr 3:11; often ׳א וַחֲצִי Exod 25:10 (3 t. in verse); 25:17 (twice in verse) +, also ׳א הָא ׳וַחֲצִי 26:16; 36:21; 1 Kings 17:31; 1 Kings 17:32; 1 Kings 17:35 etc.; compare וָזָרֶ֑ת אַמּוֺת שֵׁשׁ 1Sam 17:4; followed by בַּמִּדָּה in measurement Josh 3:4; 2Chr 3:3.

=== H0541 ===
H541. aman

[אָמֵץ] verb be stout, strong, bold, alert (according to Lag^BN 28 f. from earlier אָמֹץ compare אֹמֶץ) — Qal. Perfect 3 plural אָֽמְצוּ 2Sam 22:18 2t.; Imperfect יֶאֱמָ֑ץ Gen 25:23; וַיֶּאֶמְצוּ2Chr 13:18; Imperative אֱמַץ Josh 1:7; 1Chr 28:20; אֱמַ֑ץ Josh 1:6; אֱמָ֑ץ Deut 31:7 3t.; אִמְצוּ 31:6 2t. —

1 be strong, of a people Gen 25:23 (J); of personal enemies 2Sam 22:18 = Psa 18:18; 142:7 (all followed by מִן of comparison); also (without מן) prevail2Chr 13:18.

2 Imperative be bold (always || חִזְקוּ חֲזַק,), Deut 31:7, 23; Josh 1:6-7, 18; (|| id. + וְאַלתַּֿעַרְצוּ אַלתִּֿירְאוּ) Deut 31:6; || id. + תֵּחָת וְאַל אַלתַּֿעֲרֹץ) Josh 1:9; (|| id. + תֵּחָ֑תּוּ וְאַל תִּירְאוּ אַל) 10:25; 1Chr 22:13; 28:20; 2Chr 32:7.

Pi`el Perfect אִמֵּץ Deut 2:30 etc.; Imperfect יְאַמֵּץ Amos 2:14 etc.; וַיְאַמֶּץלֿוֺ Isa 44:14; 3feminine singular suffix 3 masculine singular תְּאַמְּצֶנּוּ Psa 89:22; 1singular suffix אֲאַמִּצְכֶם Job 16:5 etc.; Imperative אַמֵּץ Nahum 2:2; אַמֵּ֑צוּ Isa 35:3; אַמְּצֵ֑הוּ Deut 3:28; Infinitive suffix אַמְּצוֺ Prov 8:28; Participle מְאַמֶּץֿ 24:5;

1 make firm, strengthen, followed by accusative, of giving clouds their place Prov 8:28 (subject ׳י); of repairing temple 2Chr 24:13; of physical vigour Prov 31:17 ׃ זְרוֺעֶֹתיהָ וַתְּא מָתְנֶי֑הָ בְעוֺז ׳חָֽגְרָה (subject חַיִל אֵשֶׁת); of strength for war ׳א כֹּחַ Nahum 2:2 (|| מָתְנַיִם חִזֵּק Amos 2:14 (subject חָזָק); compare Prov 24:5; of royal power 2Chr 11:17 || חִזֵּק; compare ׳א כּשְׁלוֺת בִּרְכַּיִם Isa 35:3; ׳א ׳בּ כֹּרְעוֺת Job 4:4 (both || רָפוֺת יָדַיִם חִזֵּק fig, of encouragement; so ׳א alone Deut 3:28 (|| חִזֵּק) Job 16:5 (|| חשׂך q. v.); Psa 89:22 (subject י ׳זְרוֺעַ); of support Isa 41:10 (subject ׳י; || תמך עזר,; — De Che below 2).

2 assure, secure for one's self, always followed by ל + suffix; of carpenter appropriating tree ⟦N117⟧ Isa 44:14; compare of ׳י appropriating Israel under figure of bough. Psa 80:16; under figure of chosen man 80:18.

3 followed by לֵבָב harden, make obstinate Deut 2:30 (subject ׳י; || רוּחוֺ הִקְשָׁה) 15:7 (of unkind man); 2Chr 36:13 (of king Zedekiah, || אֶתעָֿדְמּוֺ הִקְשָׁה).

=== H0637 ===
H637. aph

II. אַף conjunction denoting addition, especially of something greater, also, yea (so Phoenician, Aramaic , אוֺף אַף,; compare ).

1 very rare in plain prose (in which גַּם is more usual): Gen 40:16* (*with pronoun, as rather often) Ialso in my dream, Num 16:14; Deut 2:11*; 2:20* 2Sam 20:14 (see Dr) 2Kin 2:14 * Est 5:12; more frequently in poetry, especially as introducing emphatically a new thought Deut 33:3, 20, 28; 1Sam 2:7; Psa 16:6; 16:7; 16:9; 18:49; ⟦N198⟧ Psalm 65:14 they shout for joy, yea, they sing! 68:9; 68:17; 74:16; 89:28Psalm 1; Prov 22:19* 23:28* +; or in more elevated prose style, Lev 26:16*; 26:24*; 26:28*; 26:41*; and 25 t. in the impassioned rhetoric of Isa^2 (Isaiah 40:24-48:15), e.g. Isa 40:24; 41:10, 26; 42:13; 43:7, 19; 46:11 yea, I have spoken, I will also bring it to pass; I have purposed, I will also do it! 48:12, 15. Implying something surprising or unexpected, even, indeed Job 14:3; 15:4*. וְאַף and also Lev 26:39-40, 42, 44; Deut 15:17; Hab 2:15; Psa 68:19; 1Chr 8:32* = 9:38* 2Chr 12:5* Neh 2:18; 13:15; and even Job 19:4 שָׁגִיתִי וְאַףאָֿמְנָם and even indeed (if) I have erred . . . With הַאַף הֲ, indeed . . .. really . . .. Gen 18:13, 23 wilt thou indeed sweep away the righteous with the wicked? 18:24; Amos 2:11; Job 34:17; 40:8. In contrast to a preceding thought (expressed or implied) but, nay (imo) Psa 44:10; 58:3; compare Judg 5:29*.

2 (Equally in prose and poetry) with reference to a preceding sentence, yea, à fortiori, the more so (= how much more! after an affirm. clause; = how much less! after a negative one): 2Sam 4:10f. when one told me, Saul is dead . . . I took hold of him and slew him . . . וג הָרְגוּ רְשָׁעִים כִּיאֲֿנָשִׁים ׳אַף à fortiori, how much more (should I do so when wicked men have slain a righteous person, etc.! Ezek 14:21 (Ew Hi) 15:5; Prov 21:27 (in all these passages כִּי = when) Job 4:19. So וְאַף 1Sam 23:3; 2Kin 5:13 . . . אֵלֶיךָ כִּיאָֿמַר וְאַף and the more (= and how much rather), when he hath said to thee, etc. More commonly in this sense strengthened by כִּי (q. v.), see below

H775. Arpakshad

=== H0834 ===
H834. asher

אֲשֶׁר particle of relation (Moabite id.; origin dubious:

1 according to Tsepreghi^Diss. Lugd. p. 171 Mühlau^Bö. Lb. ii. 79 n. Sta^Morg. Forsch. 1875, 188; Lb. § 167 Hom^ZMG 1878, 708 ff. Müll^§ 153 Sayce^Hebraica. ii. 51 Lag^M. i. 255 & especially Krae^Hebraica. vi. 298 ff, originally a substantive 'place' = footstep, mark, (do.), אֲתַר, place, Assyrian ašru, used (see Kraetz.) both as a substantive 'there, where,' and as a relative of place 'where': in Hebrew this development has advanced further, and it has become a relative sign Generally. The chief objection to this explanation is that it would isolate Hebrew from the other Semitic languages, in which pronouns are formed regularly from demonstrative roots (compare also Nö^ZMG 1886, 738).

2 according to Phi^St. C. 73 Sperling^Nota Rel. im Hebr. 1876, 15-22 for אֲשֶׁל, developed from the relative שׁ (q. v.) by (1) the prefixing of either a merely prosthetic א, or, better, a pronominal א (giving rise to אש, the form of the relative in Phoenician), and (2) the addition of the demonstrative root ל [found also in הַלָּזֶה אֵלָּה, אֵל, (q. v.), he who, who (plural)]: the main objection to this explanation is the change of ל to ר, which is hardly rendered probable by the compare of Syriac by side of הָלְכָּא ᵑ7. 1 seems preferable, the primitive root having acquired different significations in the different Semitic languages, and having been weakened in Hebrew to a mere particle of relation). A

sign of relation, bringing the clause introduced by it into relation with an antecedent clause. As a rule אֲשֶׁר is a mere

connecting link, and requires to be supplemented (see the grammars) by a pronominal affix, or other word, such as שָׁם, defining the nature of the relation more precisely: e.g. Gen 1:11 זַרֵעוֺבֿוֺ אֲשֶׁר literally as to which, its seed is in it = in which is its seed, Psa 1:4 like the chaff רוּחַ אֲשֶׁרתִּֿדְּפֶנּוּ as to which, the wind drives it = which the wind drives, etc.; & so אֲשֶׁרשָׁ֗֗֗ם = where, אֲשֶׁרמִ֗֗֗שָּׁם = whence, Gen 2:11; 3:23; 20:13 etc. Sometimes also (see below) the relation expressed by it is specifically temporal, local causal, etc. More particularly

1 it includes its pronominal antecedent, whether in the nominative or oblique cases, as Num 22:6 יוּאָר תָּאֹר וַאֲשֶׁר and he whom thou cursest is cursed, Exod 4:12 and I will teach thee תְּדַבֵּר אֲשֶׁר that which thou shalt say; and with particles or prepositions, as אֲשֶׁר אֵת (according to the context) him who . . ., those who . . ., that which . . .; לַאֲשֶׁר to him who . . . Gen 43:16, to those who . . . 47:24, to that which 27:8; מֵאֲשֶׁר Judg 16:30; 2Sam 18:18 than those whom; Lev 27:24 מֵאִתּוֺ קָנָהוּ לַאֲשֶׁר to him from whom he bought it, Num 5:7; Isa 24:2 בוֺ נשֶׁא כַּאֲשֶׁר like him against whom there is a creditor.

2 instances of אֲשֶׁר followed by a pronominal affix, or by מִשָּׁם שָֽׁמָּה, שָׁם,, are so common that the examples cited above will be sufficient. Very rarely there occurs the anomalous construction אֲשֶׁר עִם Gen 31:32 for עִמּוֺ אֲשֶׁר (see 44:9), בַּאֲשֶׁר Isa 47:12 for לַאֲשֶׁר בָּהֶם, אֲשֶׁר for אֲשֶׁרלָ֗֗֗הֶם Ezek 23:40: Psa 119:49 see under אשׁר על. It is followed by the pronoun in the nominative, in the following cases: — (a) immediately, mostly before an adjective or participle, Gen 9:3 all moving things הוּאחַֿי אֲשֶׁר which are living, Lev 11:26; Num 9:13; 14:8, 27; 35:31; Deut 20:20; 1Sam 10:19 (see Dr) 2Kin 25:19 (|| Jer 52:25 היה) 27:9; Ezek 43:19; Hag 1:9; Ruth 4:15; Neh 2:18; Eccl 7:26; before a verb 2Kin 22:13 (omitted 2Chr 34:21). (b) in a negative sentence, at the end: Gen 7:2; 17:12; Num 17:5; Deut 17:15 הוּא אָחִיךָ לֹא אֲשֶׁר who is not thy brother, 20:15; Judg 19:12; 1Kin 8:41 || 9:20 ||. N.B. Psa 16:3 הֵ֑מָּה בָּאָרֶץ אֲשֶׁר is an unparalleled expression for 'who are in the land'; read וג אַדִּירֵי הֵמָּה בָּאָ֑רֶץ ׳אֲשֶׁר 'the saints that are in the land, they (המה) are the nobles, in whom,' etc.

3 sometimes (though rarely) the defining adjunct is a pronoun of 1 or 2 person as well as of 3 person. In such cases it is strictly to be rendered I who . . ., thou who, etc.; Hosea 14:4 יָתוֺם יְרֻחַם אֲשֶׁרבְּֿךָ thou by whom the fatherless is compassionated ! Jer 31:32 I, whose covenant they brake, 32:19; Isa 49:23; Job 37:17f. thou whose garments are warm . . ., canst thou ? etc., Psa 71:19; 71:20; 144:12 we whose sons, etc., 139:15 my frame was not hidden from thee, בַסֵּתֶר אֲ֯שֶׁרעֻֿשֵּׂיתִי I who was wrought in secret (= though I was wrought in secret), Exod 14:13 for ye who have seen the Egyptians to-day, — ye shall not see them again for ever! (compare Psa 41:9).

4 the defining pron. adjunct is dispensed with —

a. when אֲשֶׁר represents the simple subject of a sentence, or the direct object of a verb: so constantly, as Gen 2:1 the work עָשָׂה אֲשֶׁר which he made, 3:3 the tree הַגָּן בְּתוֺךְ אֲשֶׁר which is in the midst of the garden, etc.

b. after words denoting time, place, or manner, so that אֲשֶׁר then becomes equivalent to when, where, why: (a) Gen 6:4 אֲשֶׁר כֵן אַחֲרֵי afterwards, when, etc. (compare 2Chr 35:20) Gen 45:6 there are still 5 years חָרִישׁ אֵין אֲשֶׁר when there shall be no plowing, Josh 14:10; 1Kin 22:25; after יוֺם or הַיּוֺם Deut 4:10; Judg 4:14; 1Sam 24:5 (see Dr) 2Sam 19:25; Jer 20:14 and elsewhere; similarly Gen 40:13. (β) 35:13 אִתּוֺ דִּבֶּר אֲשֶׁר בַּמָּקוֺם in the place where he spake with him, 35:14; 39:20; Num 13:27; 22:26; Deut 1:31 in the desert which thou sawest, where (accents Ke Di), 8:15; 1Kin 8:9 (unless הַבְּרִית לוּחוֺת has here fallen out: see ᵐ5 & Deut 9:9) Isa 55:11; 64:10; Psa 84:4. So (γ) in אֲשֶׁר אֶל to (the place) which (or whither) Exod 32:34; Ruth 1:16; אֶלכָּֿלאֲֿשֶׁר to every (place) whither Josh 1:16; Prov 17:8; בַּאֲשֶׁר in (the place) where Judg 5:27; 17:8-9, 1Sam 23:13; 2Kin 8:1; Ruth 1:16-17, Job 39:30, once only with שָׁם Gen 21:17; אֲשֶׁר בְּכֹל wheresoever Josh 1:7, 9; Judg 2:15; 1Sam 14:47; 18:5; 2Sam 7:7; 2Kin 18:7; מֵאֲשֶׁר from (the place) where = whencesoever Exod 5:11; Ruth 2:9; עַלאֲֿשֶׁר to (the place) whither (or which) 2Sam 15:20; 1Kin 18:12; עַלכָּֿלאֲֿשֶׁר Jer 1:7. (δ) ֗֗֗ אֲשֶׁר הַדָּבָר זֶה this is the reason that or why . . . Josh 5:4; 1Kin 11:27.

c. more extreme instances Lev 14:22, 30, 31; Num 6:21; Deut 7:19 (wherewith), 28:20; 1Sam 2:32 (wherein), 1Kin 2:26; ⟦N050⟧ Judg 8:15 (about whom), Isa 8:12 (where יאמר would be followed normally by לוֺ), 31:6 turn ye to (him as to) whom they have deeply rebelled, 47:15; Zeph 3:11; Eccl 3:9; 1Kin 14:19 (= how).

d. it is dispensed with only in appearance after וג (אָמַרְתִּי אָמַר ׅ׳אֲשֶׁר followed by the words used, its place being really taken by a pronoun in the speech which follows, as Gen 3:17 the tree as to which I commanded thee saying, Thou shalt not eat from it, Exod 22:8; Deut 28:68; Judg 7:4 (זֶה) 8:15 (where the noun repeated takes the place of the pronoun, compare Deut 9:2) 1Sam 9:17 (זֶה):23 +; compare 2Sam 11:16; 2Kin 17:12; ⟦N025⟧ 21:4.

5 אֲשֶׁר sometimes in poetry = one who, a man who (men who), ὅστις, οἵτινες, Psa 24:4; 55:20; 95:4; 95:5; Job 4:19; 5:5; 9:5 (Hi) 15:17.

[… a szócikk folytatódik; a jelölt hivatkozások mind a fenti részben állnak]

=== H0977 ===
H977. bachar

בָּחַר verb choose (compare Aramaic בְּחַר, Syriac || בחן, Assyrian bêru (√ באר):3 Dl^Pr 76) —

Qal Perfect בָּחַר Deut 7:6 66t.; יִבְחַר Isa 41:24 60t.; אֶבֲחַר Job 29:25 (Baer); Imperative בְּחַר Exod 17:9 5t.; Infinitive absolute בָּחוֺר 1Sam 2:28; Isa 7:15-16, Infinitive construct suffix בָּחֳרִי Ezek 20:5; Participle בֹּחֵר 1Sam 20:30; Zech 3:2; passive בָּחוּר Exod 14:7 18t.; —

1 with בְּ

a. divine choice, of Abraham Neh 9:7; Israel Deut 7:7; Isa 44:1; Ezek 20:5; to become his people Deut 7:6; 14:2; Jeshurun Isa 44:2; the seed of the patriarchs Deut 4:37; 10:15; Levites 18:5; 21:5; 1Chr 15:2; 2Chr 29:11; Aaron Psa 105:26; Judah 1Chr 28:4 not Ephraim Psa 78:67; Levi and Judah Jer 33:24; the king Deut 17:15, especially David 1Sam 10:24; 16:8-9, 10; 2Sam 6:21; 1Kin 8:16 (= 2Chr 6:6) 1Chr 28:4-5, 29:1; 2Chr 6:5; Psa 78:70; others Num 16:5; Numbers 17:20 (P) Hag 2:23; place of sacrifice Deut 12:18; 14:25; 16:7, 15; 17:8; 2Chr 7:12; the city 1Kin 8:16, 44 (= 2Chr 6:5, 34) 1Kin 11:32; Jerusalem 2Chr 6:6; Isa 14:1; Zech 1:17; Zechariah 2:16; 3:2; Zion Psa 132:13; delusions Isa 66:4.

b. man's choice, of ways Prov 3:31; Isa 66:3; good things 7:15-16, life Deut 30:19; gods Judg 10:14; Isa 41:24; God's pleasure 56:4; 65:12; 66:4.

2 with אֲשֶׁר (alone, for ב בחר ׳אשׁר):

a. divine choice, Israel Isa 41:8 בְּחַרְתִּיךָ אֲשֶׁר יַעֲקֹב, 43:10; the people 1Kin 3:8; Psa 33:12; men Num 16:7 (P) Psa 65:5; king 2Sam 16:18; 1Kin 11:34; place of sacrifice Deut 12:14, 26; 15:20; 17:10; 31:11 (D) Josh 9:27 (P), especially שָׁם שְׁמוֺ לָשׂוּם Deut 12:5, 21; 14:24, שָׁם שְׁמוֺ לְשַׁכֵּן 12:11; 14:23; 16:2, 6, 11; Neh 1:9; the city 1Kin 8:48 (= 2Chr 6:38), שׁם שׁמי לשׂום 1Kin 11:36; 14:21 (= 2Chr 12:13); Jerusalem 1Kin 11:13; 2Kin 21:7 (= 2Chr 33:7) 2Kin 23:27; fast Isa 58:5-6, way Psa 25:12.

b. man's choice, place to dwell in Deut 23:7; gardens Isa 1:29; king 1Sam 12:13; wives Gen 6:2 (J); what to do 2Sam 15:15.

3 with accusative & לְ, choose some one or something for:

a. divine choice, of Levi 1Sam 2:28; Jacob Psa 135:4; 135:5; inheritance 47:5.

b. human choice persons Exod 17:9; Josh 24:15, 22 (E) 1Sam 8:18; 13:12; 2Sam 24:12 (= 1Chr 21:20); things Gen 13:11 (J) 1Sam 17:40; 1Kin 18:23, 25; Job 34:4.

4 with accusative and מִן, choose, select from 2Sam 10:9 (= 1Chr 19:10).

5 accusative

a. divine choice, temple 2Chr 7:16; Judah Psa 78:68; servant Isa 41:9; 49:7.

b. man's choice, persons Exodus 18:28 (E) Judg 5:8; Josh 8:3 (J) 2Sam 17:1; things Job 7:15; 9:14; 15:5; 29:25; 34:33; Psa 119:30; 119:173; Prov 1:29; Isa 40:20.

6 with מעני בחרת זה על כי עַל, for this thou hast chosen rather than affliction Job 36:21; with accusative and על pregnantly עלי תבחר אשׁר כל all that thou choosest (to lay) upon me 2Sam 19:39.

7 with לְ of accusative 1Sam 20:30 (many MSS. have בְּ; but ᵐ5 We Dr read חָבֵר companion). The participle בָּחוּר chosen, of a ruler Psa 89:20, warrior Jer 49:19 (= 50:44); as cedars Song 5:15; collective בחור רכב chosen chariots Exod 14:7 (E); בחור אישׁ chosen men, warriors Judg 20:15-16, 34; 1Sam 24:3; 2Chr 13:3, 17, for which בָּחוּר alone 1Kin 12:21 ⟦N138⟧ 2Chr 11:1; 25:5; ישׂראל בחורי 1Sam 26:2; Psa 78:31; 2Sam 10:9 בישׂראל בחורי (|| 1Chr 19:10 בישׂראל בחור, doubtless the true reading, as 2Sam 6:1).

8 test, try (Aramaic = בחן) עֹ֑נִי בְּכוּר בְּחַרְתִּיךָ Isa 48:10 I have tested thee in the furnace of affliction ᵑ7 ᵑ6 Ges Hi Ew De Che Dr, but chosen ᵑ9 Rab Calv AV. Niph`al נִבְחַר Jer 8:3; Participle נִבְחָר Prov 8:10 5t.; — to be chosen.

=== H0996 ===
H996. bayin

[בַּ֫יִן] substantive properly interval, space between ( id.) — construct בֵּין once Isa 44:4 (Baer) בֵּינוֺ בֵינֵךְ, ֵבּינְךָ, בֵּינִי, בֵּן, (בֵּינָיו Josh 3:4; 8:11 Qr); with plural suffix in plural form בֵּינֵינוּ (בֵּנֵינוּ), etc.; also בֵּינוֺת Ezek 10:2 (twice in verse); 10:6 (twice in verse); 10:7 (twice in verse) (+ 1:13 ᵐ5 Hi Ew etc. for דְּמוּת), בֵּינוֺתֵינוּ Gen 26:28; Josh 22:34; Judg 11:10, בֵּינוֺתָם Gen 42:23; 2Sam 21:7; Jer 25:16; dual בֵּנַיִם (see below); —

1 always (except dual) as

preposition in the interval of, between (so Aramaic בֵּין, & plural בֵּינֵי, , ; ), as Gen 15:17 הַגְּזָרִים בֵּין between the pieces, Exod 13:9 and elsewhere עֵינֶיךָ בֵּין between thy eyes (see עַיִן), Isa 22:11 and elsewhere between the two walls (see חוֺמָה), Job 24:11; 30:7; Job 40:30 part him between merchants; rather more Generally among Hosea 13:15; Song 2:2-3, Ezek 19:2; 31:3; Job 34:37; less exactly within ⟦N142⟧ Prov 26:13 a lion is הָֽרְחֹבוֺת בֵּין within the board places (compare 23:13 בְּתוֺךְ): once with a singular (unusual) Dan 8:16 אוּלַי בֵּין between the Ulai, i.e. between its banks. When the space separating two distinct objects is to be indicated, this is done

a. most commonly by repeating בין, as Gen 13:3 הָעָ֑י וּבֵין בֵּיתְאֵל בֵּין literally in the interval of Bethel, and in the interval of `Ai, i.e. between Bethel and `Ai, 16:14; 17:7; 31:50-51, +?

גּוֺי noun masculine^Gen 12:2 nation, people (Late Hebrew id. Gentiles, Phoenician גו community, , Sabean גו id., DHM^ZMG 1883, 348) — ׳ג 12:2 121t.; suffix 1 singular גּוֺיִ Zeph 2:9, גּוֺיֶ֑ךָ Psa 106:5, גּוֺיֵךְ Kt Ezek 36:13-14, 15 (Qr wrongly גּוֺיַיִךְ compare Co, who strike out 36:15); plural גּוֺיִם Gen 10:5 410t. + Qr 25:23; Psa 79:10 (Kt גיים) + 6 t. Ezekiel (variant reading emended Co); construct גּוֺיֵי Gen 18:18 8t., גּוֺיֵ2Chr 32:13; Ezra 6:21 (compare Baer's notes); suffix גּוֺיֵהֶם Gen 10:5, 20, 31, 32; —

1 nation, people Gen 10:5 (twice in verse); 10:20, 31, 32 (twice in verse) (all P) +; Isa 2:2, 4 (twice in verse) = Micah 4:2-3,(twice in verse); Job 12:23 (twice in verse); 34:29 ; Prov 14:34; הָאָרֶץ גּוֺיֵי כֹּל Gen 18:18; 22:18; 26:4 (all J); Deut 28:1.

a. specifically of descendants of Abraham, גָּדוֺל גּוֺי Gen 12:2; compare 18:18 (both J), גּוֺיִם 17:6, גּוֺיִם הֲמוֺן 17:4, 15 (all P); of Sarah גּוֺיִם 17:16 (P); of Ishmael גּוֺי 21:13, גָּדוֺל גָּוֺי 21:18 (both E), גָּדוֺל גּוֺי 17:20 (P); of Jacob גּוֺיִם וּקְהַל גּוֺי 35:11 (P), גָּדוֺל גּוֺי 46:3 (E); of Ephraim הַגּ ׳מְלֹא 48:19 (J); of Moses גָּדוֺל גּוֺי Exod 32:10 (J) compare Num 14:12 (J) Deut 9:14; of Jacob and Esau as two nations Gen 25:23 (J).

b. definitely of Israel Exod 19:6 (׳גּ קָדוֺשׁ) 33:13 (both J E), Deut 4:6 (הַזֶּה הַגָּדוֺל הַגּוֺי, said by heathen compare 4:7; 4:8) see also 4:34, 26:5 compare Psa 33:12; 83:5 (said by enemies) Jer 31:36; 33:24; Ezek 37:22; in narrative Josh 3:17; 4:1; 5:8 (JE), 5:6 (D), 10:13 (in poetry, no article); of Israel and Judah as two nations Ezek 35:10 (said by heathen) 37:22; of Judah Isa 26:2, 15 (twice in verse); compare 58:2; 60:22; Micah 4:7; once my people Zeph 2:9 (|| עַם); thy people Psa 106:5 (i.e. of ׳י), compare also Ezek 36:13-14, (read Kt); especially of Israel and (or) Judah as sinful, rebellious Deut 32:28; Judg 2:20; Isa 1:4; 10:6; Jer 5:9, 29; 7:28; 9:8; 12:17; Ezek 2:3 (strike out ᵐ5 Co) Hag 2:14; Mal 3:9. — Note. This definite reference to Israel and (or) Judah is comparatively rare; in Hexateuch not P (yet see Gen 17:4-5, 6, ⟦N125⟧ 16; 35:11 P); seldom in exile & post-exile prophets; not Chronicles —

c. usually of non-Hebrew peoples Exod 9:24; 34:10 (JE) Lev 25:44 (H) Num 14:15 (J) Deut 15:6 (twice in verse); 1Kin 5:11; 1Chr 14:17; 16:20; Isa 11:10, 12 + often; opposed to Israel as י ׳עם 2Sam 7:23; 1Chr 17:21 (twice in verse) etc., see עם, compare also Num 23:9; note especially הַגּוֺיִם גְּלִיל Isaiah 8:23 circle or district of the nations (see גָּלִיל); also הַגּ ׳חֲרשֶׁת Judg 4:2, 13, 16 Charosheth of the nations, & לְגִלְגָּ֖ל גּוֺיִם מֶלֶךְ Josh 12:23 king of nations (peoples, tribes) belonging to Gilgal (ᵐ5 Di to the district, i.e 'Galilee'); especially of these peoples as heathen: idolatrous Lev 8:24, 28 (P) 20:23 (H) 1Kin 14:24; 2Kin 17:8, 11, 15, 26, 29 (twice in verse); 2Chr 28:3; 32:13 +, Ezra 6:21; Ezek 5:6 +; hostile Gen 15:14 (J) Lev 26:33, 38, 45 (H) Deut 4:27; 9:4-5, 18:9; 1Chr 16:35; Jer 5:15; Ezek 4:13 +? Jeremiah Ezekiel, etc.; in simile 20:32; 25:8; sometimes || עַם Psa 33:10; 33:12; Isa 11:10; Jer 6:18, see also Isa 2:2, 4 compare with Micah 4:1, 3.

=== H1576 ===
H1576. gemul

גְּמוּל noun masculine dealing, recompence, benefit — ׳ג Psa 94:2 11t.; suffix גְּמֻלְךָ Obadiah 15, etc.; plural suffix גְּמוּלָיו 103:2; —

1 יָדַיִם גְּמוּל dealing of the hands Judg 9:16; Prov 12:14 (לְ יָשׁוּב) Isa 3:11 (לְ יֵעָשֶׂה).

2 dealing, hence (from context) equivalent of dealing, recompence: with suffix Obadiah 15 בראשׁך ישׁוב גמלך, Joel 4:4; Joel 4:7 (הֵשִׁיב), so with לְ הֵשִׁיב Psa 28:4; לְ שִׁלֵּם 137:8; ⟦N053⟧ Prov 19:17; absolutely לְ גְּמוּל (הֵשִׁיב) שִׁלֵם Isa 59:18 (twice in verse); 66:6; Jer 51:6; Lam 3:64; עַל Joel 4:4; Psa 94:2; Isa 35:4 אלהים ׳ג the recompence of God.

3 benefit: גמוליו כל תשׁכחי אַל Psa 103:2; עליו כגמול according to the benefit (done) unto him2Chr 32:25

=== H1580 ===
H1580. gamal

גָּמַל verb 1. deal fully or adequately with, deal out to.

2 wean.

3 ripen (Assyrian gamâlu, deal with, benefit, e.g. VR 2Chr 35:19; Late Hebrew גָּמַל, Aramaic גְּמַל; see Palmyrene proper name אגמלא Vog^No. 124, Nabataean proper name, feminine גמלת Vog^Nab. below No. 7; perhaps originally complete, accomplish, cognate גמר; — Arabic is collect, be beautiful, goodly) —

Qal Perfect Psa 13:6 +; Imperfect יִגְמֹל Numbers 17:23 +; Imperative גְּמֹל 119:17; Infinitive גָּמְלָהּ גָּמְלֵךְ, 1Sam 1:23; Participle גֹּמֵל Prov 11:17 +; passive גָּמוּל Isa 11:8; גָּמֻל ⟦N184⟧ Psa 131:2 (twice in verse); —

1. a. deal out to, do to, with 2 accusative רָע וְלֹא טוֺב גְּמָלַתְהוּ she doeth him good and not evil Prov 31:12; הָֽרָעָה גְּמַלְתִּיךָ וַאֲנִי הַטּוֺבָה גְּמַלְתַּנִי אַתָּה כִּי for thou hast done unto me the good but I have done unto thee the evil 1Sam 24:18; do evil (ה)רע unto Gen 50:15, 17; Prov 3:30; with לְ person Isa 3:9; do good unto, 2 accusative 63:7 (twice in verse); compare also חֶסֶד אִישׁ נַפְשׁוֺ גֹּמֵל Prov 11:17.

=== H1692 ===
H1692. dabaq

דָּבֵק דָּבַק, verb cling, cleave, keep close (Late Hebrew id., Arabic Aramaic דְּבַק דְּבֵק, , ) —

Qal Perfect דָּבַק 1Kin 11:2 2t.; וְדָבַק consecutive Gen 2:24; דָּבֵ֑ק 2Kin 3:3; 3feminine singular דָּֽבְקָה Ruth 1:14 5t.; דָּבֵ֑קָה Job 29:10; 1singular דָּבַקְתִּי Psa 119:31; 3plural דָּֽבְקוּ 2Sam 20:2; וְדָֽבְקוּ consecutive Deut 28:60; דָּבֵ֑קוּ Job 41:15; וּדְבַקְתֶּם consecutive Josh 23:12; Imperfect יִדְבַּק Deut 13:18 3t.; 3 feminine singular תִּדְבַּק 2Kin 5:27; Psa 137:6; וַתִּדְבַּק Gen 34:3 2t.; suffix תִּדְבָּקַנִי 19:19; 2masculine singular תִּדְבָּ֑ק Deut 10:20; Ezek 29:4 (strike out B Co); 2 feminine singular תִּדְבָּקִין Ruth 2:8, 21; 3plural יִדְבְּקוּ Num 36:7, 9; 2masculine plural תִּדְבָּ֑קוּ Josh 23:8; תִּדְבָּקוּ֑ן Deut 13:5; Infinitive construct וּלְדָבְקָהבּֿוֺ 11:22 2t.; — in Hexateuch only J D, except Num 36:7, 9 (P); —

1 cling, cleave to,

a. literally followed by בְּ Job 19:20 (bone to skin), so followed by לְ Psa 102:6; followed by אֶל 2Sam 23:10 (hand to sword; accidentally omitted with other words 1Chr 11:13 compare Dr 2Sam 23:11), Jer 13:11 (girdle to loins), Lam 4:4 (tongue to roof of mouth, in thirst), so followed by לְ Job 29:10; Psa 137:6 (as a judgment); so also in metaphor 44:26 בִּטְנֵנוּ לָאָרֶץ דָּֽבְקָה our belly cleaveth to the earth (|| נַפְשֵׁנוּ לֶעָפָר שָׁחָה), 119:25 נַפְשִׁי לֶעָפָר דָּֽבְקָה; fish to scales of crocodile (figurative of Pharaoh), followed by בְּ Ezek 29:4 (but compare above); absolute (reciprocal) of folds of crocodile's belly Job 41:15; further of the חֵרֶם remaining in (sticking to) the hand followed by בְּ Deut 13:18; so of spot, stain Job 31:7;

b. so also of abiding on the land of one's tribe Num 36:7, 9 (followed by בְּ).

c. = remain with, close to followed by עִם Ruth 2:8, 21 followed by בְּ 2:23.

2 cling, cleave to,

a. figurative of loyalty, affection etc., sometimes with idea of physical proximity retained, followed by בְּ Gen 2:24 (J; man to wife) compare 34:3 (J) 1Kin 11:2; Josh 23:12 (D); further Ruth 1:14 (Ruth to Naomi); 2Sam 20:2 (people to king); especially (followed by בְּ) of cleaving to ׳י Deut 11:22 (|| בְּכָלדְּֿרָכָיו הָלַךְ אָהֵב,) 30:20 (|| בְּקֹלוֺ שָׁמַע אָהֵב,), Josh 22:5 (|| מִצְוֺתָיו שָׁמַר וגו, ׳הָלַךְ אָהֵב,); compare further Deut 10:20; 13:5; Josh 23:8 (all D), 2Kin 18:6; Psa 63:9 (followed by אַחֲרֵי) & 119:31 בְֵעדְוֺתֶ֑יךָ דָּבַקְתּי; so

b. of the opposite דָּבֵ֑ק ֗֗֗ יָָֽרָבְעָם בְּחַטּאֹות 2 Kings 3:37,

c. subject disease, calamity, followed by בְּ person Deut 28:60; 2Kin 5:27; followed by אַחֲרֵי Jer 42:16; followed by accusative Gen 19:19 (J); subject sin Psa 101:3 (followed by בְּ person)

Pu`al Imperfect3masculine plural יְדֻבָּ֑קוּ passive of Hiph`il

1 followed by בְּ, of crocodile's scales, they are joined together Job 41:9 (|| יִתְלַכְּדוּ); absolute of earth-clods ⟦N087⟧ 38:38.

Hiph`il Perfect3feminine singular suffix הִדְבִּיקָ֑תְהוּ Judg 20:42; 3plural suffix הִדְבִּיקֻהוּ 2Sam 1:6; 1singular הִדְבַּקְתִּי Jer 13:11; וְהִדְבַּקְתִּ֫י Ezek 29:4; Imperfect jussive יַדְבֵּק Deut 28:21; וַיַּדְבֵּק Gen 31:23; אַדְבִּיק Ezek 3:26; וַיַּדְבִּיקוּ Judg 18:22; 20:45; וַיַּדְבְּקוּ 1Sam 14:22 2t. (compare Ges^§ 53 R 4 Kö^i. 210).

=== H1755 ===
H1755. dor

I. דֹּר דּוֺר, noun masculine^Deut 32:5 period, Generation, dwelling (compare below

verb above; also Late Hebrew דּוֺר, Aramaic (included Biblical Aramaic) דָּר, , age, Generation; Arabic gyrus, orbis, periodus; Sabean דר, one time, once, דרם דרם each once in a year DHM in MV) — absolute דּוֺר Gen 7:1 68t.; דֹּר Exod 3:15; 36t.; construct דּוֺר Deut 32:20 7t., דֹּר Exod 3:15; suffix דּוֺרִי Isa 38:12, דּוֺרוֺ 53:8; plural דּוֺרִים Psa 72:5 2t., דֹּרוֺת absolute Job 42:16; Isa 41:4; construct Judg 3:2; דּוֺרוֺת construct Isa 51:9, דֹּרֹת Gen 9:12; suffix דֹּרֹתָיו 6:9; Lev 25:30, דּוֺרוֺתֵינוּ Josh 22:27, דֹּרֹתֵינוּ 22:28, דּרֹתֵיכֶם Gen 17:12 27t., דֹּרֹתָם 17:7 10t.; —

1 period, age, Generation, mostly poetic:

a. of duration in the past, former age(s) עוֺלָמִים דּוֺרוֺת Isa 51:9 (|| ימי קדם); וָדֹר דֹּר שְׁנוֺת Deut 32:7 (song; || עולם ימות); וָדֹר בְּדֹר Psa 90:1 (compare 90:2); דּוֺרוָֿדוֺר מוֺסְדֵי Isa 58:12 (|| עולם חרבות); וָדוֺר דּוֺר שֹׁמֲמוֺת 61:4 (|| id.)

b. usually of duration to come, future age(s), דֹּר לְדֹר Exod 3:15 (E; || לעולם) compare Prov 27:24 Kt (Qr וָדוֺר לְדוֺר; || id.); דֹּר מִדֹּר Exod 17:16 (E), = לָדוֺר מִדּוֺר Isa 34:10; וָדֹר לְדֹר Psa 10:6; 33:11 (|| לעולם) 49:12; 79:13; 85:6; 102:13; 119:90; 135:13; 146:10; Joel 4:20 (all || id.); 89:2 (|| עולם) 89:5 (|| עדעֿולם) 106:31 (|| id.) Isa 34:17 (|| id.); Psa 77:9 (|| לנצח); וָדֹר עַדדֹּֿר 100:5 (|| לעולם) Isa 13:20 (|| לנצח) Jer 50:39 (|| id.); וָדֹר בְּכָלדֹּֿר Jeremiah 45:18 (|| ועד לעולם) compare Est 9:28; וָדֹר דֹר כְּמוֺ שְׁנוֺתָיו Psa 61:7 compare וָדוֺר דּוֺר שְׁנֵי Joel 2:2 (|| העולם); as modifying phrase וָדוֺר דּוֺר מְשׂוֺשׂ Isa 60:15 (|| עולם נאון); further דּוֺרִים דּוֺר Psa 72:5; דּוֺרִים לְדוֺר יְשׁוּעָתִי Isa 51:8 (|| לעולם).

c. apparently including both past and future שְׁנוֺתֶיךָ דּוֺרִים בְּדוֺר Psa 102:25; וָדֹר דּוֺר בְּכָל 145:13; וָדוֺר לְדוֺר Lam 5:19.

2 of men living at a particular time (period, age), generation, as transitory בָּא וְדוֺר הֹלֵךְ דּוֺר Eccl 1:4: specifically

a. in the present, and (or) the past Gen 7:1 (J) Exod 1:6 (P) Num 32:13 (JE) Deut 1:35; 2:14; Judg 2:10 (twice in verse); Isa 53:8 (compare Che critical note) Jer 2:31; also Psa 95:10; Job 8:8; Isa 41:4.

b. Judg 3:2 (present & future); compare plural דֹּרֹתָיו Gen 6:9 (i.e. his own Generation and those immediately contiguous, before and after).

c. especially of a future Generation Gen 15:16 (JE), with numeral, compare Deut 23:3; 23:4; 23:9; also 29:21; Psa 48:14; 71:18; 78:4; 78:6; 102:19; 109:13; Joel 1:3 compare ⟦N159⟧ Psa 22:31; Psalm 22:32 (Che^critical note); of a succession of Generations מַעֲשֶׂיךָ יְשַׂבַּח לְדוֺר דּוֺר 145:4; וָדוֺר בְּכָלדּֿוֺר Est 9:28; usually plural עוֺלָם לְדֹרֹת Gen 9:12 (P); with number דּוֺר לְאֶלֶף Psa 105:8 (|| עולם) = 1Chr 16:15 (|| לעולם); Deut 7:9; see further, of posterity, דֹּרוֺת אַרְבָּעָה Job 42:16; usually plural with suffix Gen 17:7, 9, 12; Exod 12:14, 17, 42; 16:32-33, 27:21; 29:42; 30:8, 10, 21, 31; 31:13, 16; 40:15; Lev 3:17; 6:11; 7:36; 10:9 (all P); 17:7; 21:17; 22:3; 23:14, 21, 31, 41, 43; 24:3; 25:30 (all H); Num 9:10; 10:8; 15:14-15, 21, 23, 38; 18:23; 35:29; Josh 22:27-28, (all P).

3 generation characterized by quality or condition, class of men: עִקֵּשׁ דּוֺר crooked Generation Deut 32:5 (song) compare 32:20; Psa 78:8 (twice in verse); Jer 7:29; of different classes of wicked, Prov 30:11-12, 13, 14 compare Psa 12:8; of the righteous, as a class 14:5; 24:6; 73:15 (דּוֺר בָּנֶיךָ) 112:2 (compare 22:31 ᵑ0, but < Che joins לדור to Psalm 22:32 compare above below 2c); so also אֲבוֺתָיו דּוֺר i.e. the dead 49:20 (so most; yet see below below 4).

=== H1818 ===
H1818. dam

דָּם noun masculine^Gen 9:6 blood (Late Hebrew id., Arabic , Ethiopic Assyrian damu Nor^239 Zim^BP 72, 76; Aramaic דְּמָא דַּם, , ) — absolute ׳ד 37:22 154t.; construct דַּם 9:6 62t.; suffix דָּמִי 1Sam 26:20 3t.; דָּֽמְךָ 2Sam 1:16 (Qr; Kt דמיך) + 4 t.; דָּמֵךְ Ezek 16:22 3t.; דָּמוֺ Gen 9:4 30t.; דָּמָהּ Lev 4:30 (twice in verse) + 11 t.; דִּמְכֶם Gen 9:5; דָּמָם Lev 16:27 11t.; plural דָּמִים Exod 4:25 35t.; construct דְּמֵי Gen 4:10 18t.; suffix דָּמֶיךָ 2Sam 1:16 Kt (Qr דָּֽמְךָ); דָּמַיִךְ Ezek 16:6 (3 t. in verse); 16:9; דָּמָיו Lev 20:9 3t.; דָּמֶיהָ 12:7 2t.; דְּמֵיהֶם 20:11 5t.; —

1 blood of man or animal, = the life (נֶפֶשׁ) Gen 9:4 (P) Deut 12:23; Lev 17:14 (twice in verse) (H); compare הִוא בַּדָּם הַבָּשָׂר נֶפֶשׁ 17:11 (H) & לְנַפְשֹׁתֵיכֶם דִּמְכֶם Gen 9:5 (P); see also Psa 72:14 בְּעֵינָיו דָּמָם וְיֵיקַר; hence blood of animals not to be eaten Lev 3:17; 7:26-27,(all P) 17:10 (twice in verse); 17:12 (twice in verse); 17:14; 19:26 (all H) Deut 12:16, 23; 15:23; 1Sam 14:32-33, 34; Ezek 33:25 (compare RS^K 310), but to be poured out & covered with dust Lev 17:13 (compare RS^Semitic i. 216 f.).

2 usually blood become visible,

a. as from a wound ׳ד הַמַּכָּה 1Kin 22:35 (compare דָּמִים חֲתַן Exod 4:25-26, see below h); licked by dogs 1Kin 21:19 (twice in verse); 22:38; Ezek 32:6; from the nose Prov 30:33; bloody issue of a woman Lev 15:19 compare 15:25; plural טָֽהֳרָה דְּמֵי 12:4-5, & דָּמֶיהָ מְקֹר 12:7 (all P) 20:18 (H); blood in which child welters Ezek 16:6 (twice in verse); 16:22 + 16:6 (strike out A B Co and others); of goat Gen 37:31 (JE); of slain bird Lev 14:6, 51, 52.

b. often object of שָׁפַךְ, spill, shed Gen 9:6 (P) 37:22 (E) Num 35:33 (P) Lev 17:4 (H) Deut 21:7; 1Sam 25:31; 1Kin 18:28; 2Kin 21:16; 24:4; 1Chr 22:8; Ezek 16:38; 22:4, 6, 9, 12, 27; 23:45; 33:25; 36:18 (strike out B Co) Prov 1:16 also Psa 79:3 כַּמַּיִם דָמָם שָֽׁפְכוּ; דָּם שֹׁפֵךְ a blood-shedder Ezek 18:10; דָּם שֹׁפֶכֶת עִיר 22:3 compare ⟦N036⟧ 22:4 (also 1Kin 2:31; Jer 22:3, 17; 1Chr 22:8; 28:3; Prov 6:17; Lam 4:13); less often subject of passive נִשְׁמָּךְ שָׁפוּךְ,, etc. Gen 9:6; Num 35:33 (both P) Deut 19:10; Zeph 1:17; Psa 79:10.

c. חָלָל דַּם Deut 32:42 (song) blood of slain; ׳ד חֲלָלִים 2Sam 1:22 so Num 23:24 (JE), object of שָׁתָה, of Israel under figure of lion; compare also Ezek 39:17-18, 19, & 2Sam 23:17 = 1Chr 11:19 (הָאֲנָשִׁים דַּם hyperbole for imperilled life); as food of young eagles Job 39:30.
