=== H1835 ===
H1835. Dan

דָּן proper name, masculine (judge) —

1 son of Jacob and Bilhah Gen 30:6; 35:25; Exod 1:4; Josh 19:47; Judg 18:29; 1Chr 2:2.

2 the tribe of Dan, דָן מַטֵּה Exod 31:6; 35:34; 38:23; Lev 24:11; Num 1:39; 13:12; Josh 21:5, 23 (all P); דָן בְּנֵי Gen 46:23; Num 1:38; 2:25; 7:66; 10:25; 26:42; 34:22; Josh 19:40, 47 (twice in verse); 19:48 (all P) Judg 1:34; 18:2, 16, 22, 23, 25, 26, 30; דָן מַחֲנֵה Num 2:25, 31 (P) — on Judg 13:25; 18:12 see מַחֲנֶה; דָּן מִשְׁמְּחֹת Num 26:42 (P); דָּן alone in poetry Gen 49:16-17, Deut 33:22 (twice in verse); Judg 5:17, elsewhere Num 1:12 (P) Deut 27:13; 1Chr 27:22; 2Chr 2:13; Ezek 48:1-2, 32.

3 a city on northern frontier of Israel at one of the sources of the Jordan, originally לַיִשׁ Judg 18:29 (compare 18:7); = לֶשֶׁם Josh 19:47; captured by a colony of Danites; named Dan, and made a sacred place with rites of worship that lasted until the exile (Judg 18:30); = Tell el Kâdî, Rob^BR iii. 351, 358 Bd^Pa1 265. This place is frequently referred to: Gen 14:14; Deut 34:1; 1Kin 12:29-30, 15:20; 2Kin 10:29; 2Chr 16:4; ⟦N109⟧ Jer 4:15; 8:16; Amos 8:14; read also וּבְדָן for וְכֵן 2Sam 20:18 ᵐ5 Ew We compare Dr; and see the phrase שֶׁבַע וְעַדבְּֿאֵר מִדָּן Judg 20:1; 1Sam 3:20; 2Sam 3:10; 17:11; 24:2, 15; 1Kin 5:5; given by Chronicles וְעַדדָּֿן שֶׁבַע מִבְּאֵר 1Chr 21:2; 2Chr 30:5. (Ezek 27:19 see ודן.)

Note. — יַעַן דָּ֫נָה 2Sam 24:6. As no such place is known, the text is usually regarded as corrupt, and, ᵐ5 being uncertain, is variously changed: (1) יַעַר דָּ֫נָה to Dan in the wood, Thes after ᵑ9 silvestria. (2) (וַיָּסֹבּוּ) וְעִיּוֺן דָּ֫נָה to Dan and Ijon Klo (compare 1Kin 15:20). (3) (סָֽבֲבוּ) וּמִדָּן דָּן We Dr compare ᵐ5.

=== H1870 ===
H1870. derek

דֶּ֫רֶךְ noun masculine^Deut 17:16 & (less often) feminine^Exod 18:20 way, road, distance, journey, manner — absolute ׳ד Gen 38:16 +; דָּ֑רֶךְ 38:21 +; construct דֶּרֶךְ 3:24; suffix דַּרְכִּי 24:42 4t. + 2Sam 22:33 דַּרְכִּ֯ו; דַּרְכְּךָ Hosea 10:13; 1Kin 19:15; דַּרְכֶּ֑ךָ Psa 5:9 9t.; דַּרְכֵךְ Jer 2:23 8t.; דַּרְכּוֺ Gen 6:12 +; דַּרְכָּהּ 1Sam 1:18; Job 28:23; דַּרְכֵּנוּ Judg 18:5 2t.; דַּרְכְּכֶם Gen 19:2-3t., דַּרָכָּם 1Kin 2:4 +; dual דְּרָכַיִם Prov 28:6, 18; plural דְּרָכִים Deut 28:7 6t.; construct דַּרְכֵי Prov 3:17 24t.; suffix דְּרָכַי Job 13:15 17t., דְּרָכָ֑י Psa 95:10 4t.; דְּרָכֶיךָ Deut 28:29 16t.; דְּרָכֶךָ Exod 33:13 2t.; דְּרָכַיִךְ Jer 3:18 4t.; דְּרָכָ֑יִךְ Ezek 7:3 3t.; דְּרָכָיו Deut 10:12 +; דְּרָכֶיהָ Prov 3:17 3t.; דְּרָכֵינוּ Lam 3:40; Zech 1:6; דַּרְכֵיכֶם Lev 26:22 17t.; דַּרְכֵיהֶם Jer 16:17 5t.; דַּרְכֵיהֶן Ezek 16:47; —

1 way, road, path Gen 35:3 (E) 38:16, 21 (both J) 48:7 (P), 49:17 (J; poem) Lev 26:22 (H, only here in Leviticus) Num 22:22-23, (3 t. in verse); 22:31, 34 (all J E) Deut 6:7; 22:4, 6; 23:5; 24:9; 25:17-18, Josh 3:4 (D) Ruth 1:7; 1Sam 4:13 (on text compare Dr) 15:2; 24:4; 2Sam 16:13; 1Kin 13:9-10, (twice in verse); 13:12, 17, 24 (twice in verse); 13:25-26, 28; 18:6 (twice in verse); 18:7; 20:38; 2Kin 2:23; 3:8; 6:19; 7:15, 19:28; 19:33 = Isa 37:29, 34; Hosea 6:9; 13:7; Isa 43:16, 19; 49:9, 11; 51:10; 57:14 (twice in verse); Jer 6:25; Lam 1:5; Ezek 21:24; 21:25; 42:11-12, Joel 2:7; Ezra 8:22, 31; Job 12:24; Psa 107:40; 110:7; Prov 7:8; 8:2; 26:13; Eccl 10:3; 12:5; construct way of (often = to, toward) ׳ד הַחַיִּים עֵץ Gen 3:24 (J) ׳ד שׁוּר 16:7 (J) ׳ד מְּלִשְׁתִּים אֶרֶץ Exod 13:17 (E) ׳ד הַבָּשָׁן Num 21:33 (JE); compare Deut 1:2, 19; 3:1; Josh 2:7 (JE) 12:3; 1Sam 6:9, 12; 13:17-18, (twice in verse); 17:52; 2Kin 3:20; Isa 15:5; Jer 2:18 (twice in verse); 50:5; Ezek 47:15; 48:1; ׳ד הַמִּדְבָּר (of different ways) Exod 13:18 (E) Josh 8:15 (JE) Judg 20:42, compare Deut 2:8; 2Sam 2:24; 2Kin 3:8; אֶתהַֿמִּדְבָּר ׳ד 2Sam 15:23 is corrupt; read ᵐ5 בַּטִּדְבָּר ׳ד אֲשֶׁר הַזַּיִת^L Dr compare We^ the way of the olive-tree which, etc.; ׳ד הָעֲרָבָה Deut 2:8 and (with different reference) 2Sam 4:7; 2Kin 25:4 = Jer 52:7; 39:4; ׳ד הַכִּכָּר 2Sam 18:23; ׳ד יַםסֿוּף Num 14:25; 21:4 (JE) Deut 1:40; 2:1; often of gateway, as ׳ד הַשַּׁעַר 2Sam 15:2 compare 2Kin 11:19; 25:4 = Jer 52:7; Ezek 9:2; 43:4; 44:1, 4; 46:9 (5 t. in verse); 47:2; הַשַּׁעַר אוּלָם ׳ד 44:3; 46:2, 8; ׳ד הַגָּן בֵּית 2Kin 9:27, ׳ד גַּןהַֿמֶּלֶךְ Jer 39:4; ׳ד הַיָּם Isaiah 8:23 (compare Commentaries & Schumacher^Jaulan 63 and PEF^Apr. 1889, 78); ׳ד הַסּוּסִים מְבוֺא 2Kin 11:16; followed by ָ ה locative Gen 35:19 (E), 38:14 (J) 48:7 (P; read אפרתה Ol); ׳ד נֵלֵךְ הַמֶּלֶךְ Num 20:17; 21:22 (both J E; Arabic of different highroads, compare Seetzen^i. 61, 132, ii. 336 Rob^BR iii. 141 Di; = מְסִלָּה 20:19) i.e. the highway, used by king with his army; compare || בַּדֶּרֶךְ בַּדֶּרֶךְ Deut 2:27 i.e. straight (or steadily) along the way; in particular of path, way, in which God moves דַּרְכֶּ֑ךָ בַּיָּם Psa 77:20 (|| רַבִּים בְּמַיִם וּשְׁבִילְיךָ֯); דַּרְכּוֺ וּבִשְׂעָרָה Nahum 1:3; compare ׳ד ׳י מַּנּוּ בַּמִּדְבָּר Isa 40:8 (|| לאלהינו מְסִלָּה בָּֽעֲרָבָה יַשְּׁרוּ); also Mal 3:1, compare Isa 43:16, 19 (way made by ׳י) & 51:10; path of lighting Job 28:26; 38:25; of light 38:19, 24, of camel (בִּכְרָה) Jer 2:23, of locusts Joel 2:7 (plural); see further the four wonderful things Prov 30:19 the way of an eagle in the air, the way of a serpent upon a rock, the way of a ship in the heart of the sea, and the way of a man with a maid (i.e. no trace is left in any case); compare also 30:20 מְנָאָ֑פֶת אִשָּׁה דֶּרֶךְ כֵּן; figure of death כָּלהָֿאָרֶץ בְּדֶרֶךְ הַיּוֺם הוֺלֵךְ אָנֹכִי Josh 23:14 I am going to-day in the way of all the earth (D); so also 1Kin 2:2 (compare Gen 19:31 4a).

2 journey Gen 24:21, 40, 42, 56 (all J) Num 9:10, 13 (P) Josh 9:11, 13 (both J E) 1Kin 18:27; 19:7 הַדּ מִמְּךָ ׳רַב; ד ׳לַעֲשׂוֺת Judg 17:8; שָׁ֑בַע ׳ד בְּאֵר Amos 8:14 (journey of — to — Beersheba compare Hi St; but text dubious, ᵐ5 ὁ θεός, compare Gunning; Hoffm^ZAW 1883, 123 proposes דֹּדְךָ, We בְּאֵרְךָ compare Bu^ThLz 1892, 62); חֹל דֶּרֶךְ 1Sam 21:6 a common (not sacred) journery; especially with temporary modification ׳ד יָמִים שְׁלשֶׁת Gen 30:36 (J) Exod 3:18; 5:3; 8:23; Num 10:33 (twice in verse) (all J E), 33:8 (P) compare also Gen 31:23 (E) Num 11:31 (twice in verse) (JE), 1Kin 19:4; 2Kin 3:9; go one's way לְדַרְכּוֺ הָלַךְ Gen 19:2 (J), 32:2 (E), 33:16 (J), Num 24:25; Josh 2:16 (both J E), Judg 18:26; 19:27; 1Sam 1:18; 26:25; 30:2; 1Kin 1:49; Jer 28:11, see הלך; also לְד ׳הָפַךְ 1Sam 25:12, & (figurative) לְד ׳מָּנָה Isa 53:6 (so 56:11

5 below); of זוֺנָה, figurative of Israel, לַזָּרִים אֶתדְּֿרָכַיִךְ וַתְּפַזְּרִי Jer 3:13.

3 of direction, almost or quite = toward, 1Kin 8:44, 48 2Chr 6:34, 38 pray toward the city, etc.; 2 Chron 18:43 look toward the sea; especially in Ezek. of looking, going, etc. ׳ד צָפוֺנָה Ezek 8:5 (twice in verse); compare 21:2; 40:6; construct ׳ד הַקָּדִים 40:10 (strike out ᵐ5 Hi Co) 40:22; 43:1; ׳ד הַצָּפוֺן 43:20 compare ׳ד הַדָּרוֺם 43:24; 43:24, ׳ד הַיָּם 41:12, ׳ד הֶחָצֵר 42:7 etc. (c. 28 t. Ezekiel); (see also מִדֶּרֶךְ 9:2; compare 43:2; 44:3; on ׳ד אֶחָת אַמָּה 42:4 see VB Co Da) compare כְּרָמִים דֶּרֶךְ לֹאיִֿפְנֶה Job 24:18.

4 way, manner:

a. usage ׳ד כָּלהָֿאָרֶץ Gen 19:31 (J; compare also Josh 23:14; 1Kin 2:2 above)

b. = customary experience, or condition ׳ד נָשִׁים Gen 31:35 (E).

c. ׳ד מִצְרַיִם Amos 4:10 the manner of Egypt, i.e. as was done in Egypt; Isa 10:24, 26.

d. habit of animals, ants Prov 6:6 (plural)

5 figurative of course of life, or action, undertakings, etc. אֶתדְּֿרָכֶיךָ תַצְלִיחַ לֹא Deut 28:29 compare Josh 1:8 (D) 1Sam 18:14; 2Sam 22:33 = Psa 18:33; Job 3:23; 8:19; 17:9; 22:28; 24:23; 31:4; 34:21; Psa 10:5; 35:6; 37:5; 37:7; 37:23; 91:11; 102:24; 119:37; 128:1; 139:3; 146:9; Prov 2:8; 3:6, 23; 11:5; 20:24; 29:27; 31:3; Isa 40:27; 45:13; 48:15; 55:8-9, Jer 2:36; 10:23; 12:1; 23:12; Lam 3:9, 11; Hosea 2:8; 9:8; 10:13; Amos 2:7 (׳ד עְנָוִים); thrice (Chronicles) in summary of a king's life, of Abijah וּדְבָרָיו וּדְרָכָיו2Chr 13:22; of Jotham וּדְרָכָיו וְכָלמִֿלְחֲמֹתָיו27:7; of Ahaz וְהָאַחֲרוֺנִים הָרִאשֹׁנִים וְכָלדְּֿרָכָיו28:26; ׳ד אֹרְחֹתֶיךָ Isa 3:12 compare also 30:11; and 57:18; = gain 56:11, pleasure, etc. 58:13.

6. a. of moral action and character עַלהָֿאָרֶץ אֶתדַּֿרְכּוֺ כָּלבָּֿשָׂר הִשְׁחִית Gen 6:12 (P); compare Jer 3:21; אֶתדַּֿרְכָּם יִשְׁמְרוּ 1Kin 2:4; 8:25 2Chr 6:16; compare 6:39 6:30; י לִפְנֵי דְּרָכָיו ׳הֵכִין27:6; Psa 5:9; 39:2; 49:14; 50:23; 119:5; 119:26; 119:59; 119:168; Prov 4:26; 5:8, 21; 10:9; 14:2, 8, 12; 16:2, 7, 9, 17, 25; 19:3, 16; 21:2, 29; Jer 2:33; 6:27; 7:3, 5; 16:17; 17:10; 18:11, 15; 26:13; 32:19 (twice in verse); Lam 3:40; Ezek 7:3; 7:8; 7:9; 7:27; 14:22; 14:23; 18:25; 18:29; 18:30; 24:14; 28:15; 33:17; 33:20; 36:19; Hag 1:5; 1:7; Zech 1:6; לִבְּךָ דַּרְכֵי Eccl 11:9 compare Isa 57:17 (& Jer 32:39 c. below)

b. of duty (commanded by ׳י, etc.) ׳הַדּ בָּהּ יֵלְכוּ Exod 18:20 (E) compare 32:8 (JE) Deut 9:12, 16; 13:6; 31:29; Judg 2:17; Job 31:7; Psa 25:8; 25:12; 32:8; 119:1; 143:8; Prov 21:16; 22:6; 23:19; Isa 30:21; 48:17; Jer 7:23; Mal 2:8; so too, of wisdom דַרְכֵינֹֿעַם דְּרָכֶיהָ Prov 3:17, ׳ד חָכְמָה 4:11; 8:32; compare 23:26; ׳ד בִּינָה 9:6 compare Isa 40:14.

c. specifically: in good sense ׳ד וְהַיְשָׁרָה הַטּוֺבָה 1Sam 12:23; 1Kin 8:36 2Chr 6:27; compare Prov 16:31; Jer 6:16; דְּרָכֶיךָ תֹּם Job 4:6; compare 22:8; Prov 13:6; דְּרָכָיו (i.e. of light) Job 24:13 (|| נְתִיבֹתָיו); ׳ד צַדִּיקִים Psa 1:6, ׳ד טוֺבִים Prov 2:20, יִשְׁרֵידָֿ֑רֶךְ Psa 37:14, דָ֑רֶךְ תְּמִימֵי Prov 11:20; Psa 119:1; ׳ד תָּמִים 101:2; 101:6, ׳ד אֱמוּנָה 119:30, ׳דּ שָׁלוֺם Isa 59:8; ׳ד עוֺלָּם Psa 139:24, ׳ד חַיִּים Prov 6:23, compare 12:28; Jer 21:8 (opposed to ׳ד המות see below); without modifier 1Sam 8:3, 5; Job 13:15; 23:10, אֶחָד וְד אֶחָד ׳לֵב Jer 32:39; compare also a. above; especially construct before names of good kings 1Kin 22:43; 2Kin 22:2; 2Chr 11:17; 20:32; 21:12 (twice in verse) (plural) Songs 34:2; compare הָרִאשֹׁנִים דָּוִיד דַּרְכֵי17:3 the first ways of David; also עַמִּי דַּרְכֵי Jer 12:16.

d. oftener in bad sense הַקָּשָׁה דַּרְכָּם Judg 2:19, הָרָעָה דַּרְכּוֺ 1Kin 13:33, ׳ד רַע Prov 2:12; 8:13; 28:10; compare 2Kin 17:13; 2Chr 7:14; Jer 18:11; 23:22; 25:5; 26:3; 35:15; 36:2, 7; Ezek 3:18-19, 13:22; 20:44; 33:11; Jonah 3:8, 10; Zech 1:4; ׳ד טוֺב לֹא Psa 36:5; Prov 16:29; Isa 65:2; זִמָּה דַּרְכֵךְ Ezek 16:27; compare with completed metaphor ׳ד סְלוּלָה לֹא Jer 18:15; further ׳ד שֶׁקֶר Psa 119:29, ׳ד עֹצֶב 139:24, ׳ד מִּשְׁעָם 107:17; דַּרְכֵיחֿשֶׁךְ Prov 2:13; מָוֶת דַּרְכֵי 14:12 = 16:25; המות דרך Jer 21:8, compare above c; ׳ד עָצֵל Prov 15:19 (compare 6:6); ׳ד חַטָּאִים Psa 1:1 compare 1:6; Prov 1:15; 3:31; 4:19 (׳ד רְשָׁעִים), 12:15 (׳ד אֱוִיל), 13:15 (׳ד בֹּגְדִים), also 15:9; 21:8; Isa 55:7; ׳ד עִקֵּשׁ Prov 22:5, compare דְּרָכַיִם עִקֵּשׁ ⟦N068⟧ 28:6 compare 28:18; further, without modifier, Hosea 4:9; 12:3; Prov 7:25; 14:14; Isa 53:6; 66:3; Jer 2:23 (of idolatry בַּגַּיְא דַרְכֵךְ רְאִי) 2:33; 4:11 (compare Hi Gf) 4:18; 15:7; 22:1; Ezek 16:47 (twice in verse); 16:61; 18:23; 20:43; 23:13, 31; 33:8-9, (twice in verse); 33:11; 36:17 (twice in verse); 36:31.36:32 compare also below a. above; בְּראֹשׁוֺ דַּרְכּוֺ לָתֵת 1Kin 8:32 to place his way upon his head, i.e. requite it on him = 2Chr 6:23, compare Ezek 7:4; 9:10; 11:21; 16:43; 22:31; דַּרְכָּם מְּרִי Prov 1:31; especially construct before names of bad kings ׳ד יָרָבְעָם 1Kin 15:34 (compare 15:26) 16:2, 19, 26; 22:53 compare 22:53; 22:53; also ׳ד יִשְׂרָאֵל מַלְכֵי 2Kin 8:18; 16:3; 2Chr 21:6, 13; 28:2 (plural); אַחְאָב בֵּית דַּרְכֵי 2Kin 8:27 2Chr 22:3 (of Ahaziah, grandson of Ahab); ׳ד הַזֶּה הָעָם Isa 8:11, ׳ד הַגּוֺיִם Jer 10:2, אֲבוֺתֵיכֶם ׳ד Ezek 20:30 compare 2Kin 21:21.

e. way of ׳י: (a) = his creative activity Job 26:14; applied in concrete sense to behemoth (hippopotamus) דַּרְכֵיאֵֿל רֵאשִׁית הוּא 40:19 (hyperb.); compare of wisdom דַּרְכּוֺ רֵאשִׁית Prov 8:22; (b) = his moral administration, Exod 33:13 (plural; J E); Deut 32:4; 2Sam 22:31 = Psa 18:31; Job 21:31; 36:23; דַּרְכֶּ֑ךָ בַּקֹּדֶשׁ אלהים Psa 77:14 (|| כֵּאלֹהִים גָּדוֺל אֵל מִי); Psalm 85:14; 103:7; 138:5; 145:17; Prov 10:29; Isa 55:8-9, Jer 5:4-5, Ezek 18:25 (twice in verse); 18:29 (twice in verse); 33:17, 20; Hosea 14:10. (c) = his commandments וּמִשְׁמָּ֑ט ד ׳וְשָֽׁמְרוּ ׳י צְדָקָה לַעֲשׂוֺת Gen 18:19 (J); י צִוָּה אֲשֶׁר ׳כָּלהַֿדֶּרֶךְ Deut 5:30 compare (plural) 8:6; 10:12; 11:22, 28; 19:9; 26:17; 28:9; 30:16; Josh 22:5 (D) Judg 2:22; 2Sam 22:22 = Psa 18:22, 1Kin 2:3; 3:14; 8:58; 11:33, 38; 2Kin 21:22; 2Chr 6:31; Job 21:14; 23:11; 34:27; Psa 25:4; 25:9; 27:11; 37:34; 51:15; 67:3; 81:14; 86:11; 95:10; 119:3; Isa 2:3 = Micah 4:2; Isa 42:24; 58:2; 63:17; 64:4; Zech 3:7; Mal 2:9; compare י בְּדַרְכֵי לִבּוֺ ׳וַיִּגְבַּהּ2Chr 17:6; see also ׳ד עֵדְוֺתֶיךָ Psa 119:14; ׳ד מִּקּוּדֶיךָ 119:27, ׳ד מִצְוֺתֶ֑יךָ 119:32, ׳ד חֻקֶּיךָ 119:33.

=== H1931 ===
H1931. hu

or hi הוּ Jer 29:23 Kt, see הוּא.

הוּא masculine הִיא feminine (plural masculine הֵם הֵ֫מָּה,; feminine הֵן הֵ֫נָּה, [the latter only with prefixes]; see these words),

pronoun of the 3rd person singular, he, she, used also (in both Genders) for the neuter it, Latin is, ea, id. (The א is not orthographic merely, but radical, being written on Moabite and Phoenician inscriptions, though dropped in some of the later dialects. [In Hebrew only Jer 29:23 Kt, and in the proper name אֱלִיהוּ.] Moabite (MI^6; 27) and Phoenician (often) הא; Aramaic of Zinjirli הא, once הו (DHM^Inschr. von Sendschirli 55); הִיא הוּא, ᵑ7 Syriac , ; Arabic , (for h¥°a, h£°a, W^SG 104); Ethiopic we°§t¥, ye°§t£; perhaps also Assyrian šû, šî, himself, herself suffix šu, ši; compare demonstrative šuatu, šiati (see Krae^BAS. i. 383 & references, W^SG 98, 105 Dl^§ 55b, 57). In the Pentateuch, הוא is of common Gender, the feminine form הִיא occurring only 11 times, namely Gen 14:2; 20:5; 38:25 (see Masora here), Lev 11:39; 13:10, 21; 16:31; 20:17; 21:9; Num 5:13-14,. The punctuators, however, sought to assimilate the usage of the Pentateuch to that of the rest of the OT, and accordingly wherever הוא was construed as a feminine pointed it הִוא (as a Qr perpetuum). Outside the Pentateuch the same Qr occurs 1Kin 17:15; Isa 30:33; Job 31:11a — probably for the sake of removing gramm. anomalies: five instances of the converse change, namely of היא to be read as הוּא, occur for a similar reason, 1Kin 17:15 (הוּאוָֿהִיא וַתּאֹכַל to be read as הִיאוָֿהוּא וַתּאֹכַל, on account of the feminine verb) Psa 73:16; Job 31:11b (פלילים עָוֺן והיא זמה הוא כי to be read as פלילים עון והוא זמה היא כי), Eccl 5:8; 1Chr 29:16. The origin of the peculiarity in the Pentateuch is uncertain. It can hardly be a real archaism : for the fact that arab., Aramaic, & Ethiopic have distinct forms for masculine & feminine shews that both must have formed part of the original Semitic stock, and consequently of Hebrew as well, from its earliest existence as an independent language. Nor is the peculiarity confined to the Pentateuch: in the Manuscript of the Later Prophets, of A.D. , now at St. Petersburg, published in facsimile by Strack (1876), the feminine occurs written הוא (see the passages cited in the Adnotationes Criticae, p. 026). In Phoenician both masculine and feminine are alike written הא (CIS i. 1:9 הא צדק מלך, 1:13 הא מלאכת, 3:10 הא אדם, 1:11 הא ממלכת, 1Chron 93:2; 1Chron 94:2), though naturally this would be read as hu° or hi° as occasion required. Hence, as ᵐ5 shews that in the older Hebrew MSS. the scriptio plena was not yet Generally introduced, it is probably that originally הא was written for both Genders in Hebrew likewise, and that the epicene הוא in the Pentateuch originated at a comparatively late epoch in the transmission of the text — perhaps in connection with the assumption, which is partly borne out by facts (compare De^ZKWL 1880, pp. 393-399), that in the older language feminine forms were more sparingly used than subsequently.)

In usage הוּא (feminine הִיא; plural הֵ֫נָּה ׃ הֵם, הֵ֫מָּה, see הֵ֫מָּה) is

1 an emphatic he (she, it, they), sometimes equivalent to himself (herself, itself, themselves), or (especially with the article) that (those):

a. Gen 3:15 ראשׁ ישׁופך הוא he (ᵐ5 αὐτὸς) shall bruise thee as to the head (opposed to the following אתה thou), 3:20 for she (and no one else) was the mother of all living (so often in causal sentences, where some emphasis on the subject is desirable as Judg 14:3; Psa 24:2; 25:15; 33:9; 91:3; 103:14; 148:5; Job 5:18; 11:11; 28:24; Jer 5:5; 34:7b Hosea 6:1; 11:10 : Dr^1Sam 14:18), 4:20 Adah bare Jabal אהלים ישׁב אבי היה הוא he (ἐκεῖνος) was the father of tent-dwellers, 4:21; 10:8 he began to be a mighty one in the earth, 20:5 (αὐτός), Judg 13:5; Isa 32:7; 33:22; 2Kin 14:7, 22, 25; Hosea 10:2 he — the unseen observer of their thoughts and deeds (Che), 13:15 b (he, the foe figured by the east wind). (For its use thus in circumstantial clauses see Dr^§ 157, 160, 168, 169.) And where the predicate is a substantive or participle, Gen 2:11 ֗֗֗ הַסֹּבֵב הוּא that is the one which encompasseth etc., 2:13; 2:14; 10:12 that is the great city. So in the explanatory notices, 14:3 הַמֶּלַת יָם הוּא that is the salt sea, 14:8 הוּאצֹֿעַר that is Zoar, 36:1 +?

b. pointing back to the subj. and contrasting it with something else : Gen 4:4 גסהֿוא הבל Abel, he also ... 4:26; 10:21; 20:5 וְהִיאגַֿםהִֿוא and she, herself also said, Exod 1:10 +?

c. appended alone to a verb (more rarely, but always with intentional emphasis), Exod 4:14 I know הוא ידבר דבר כי that he can speak, 4:16; 1Sam 22:18 בכהנים הוא ויפגע and he (though none else would do it) smote the priests, 23:22 for one hath told me, הוּא יַעְרִם עָרֹם He can deal subtilly, Ezek 12:12 (peculiarly), compare Dr^§ 160 n.: very rarely indeed to a noun Num 18:23 הוּא הַלֵּוִי Isa 7:14 ׳י הוא, Est 9:1 (הֵמָּה) being probably all the examples in the OT.

d. Gen 13:1 and Abram came up out of Egypt, וְאִשְׁתּוֺ הוּא himself and his wife, and all that he had, 14:15 וַעֲבָדָיו הוּא he and his servants, 19:30; so very often

e. prefixed to a noun (very rare, and mostly late), Exod 12:42b Ezek 3:8 & 33:8 רָשָׁע הוּא : to proper names Exod 6:27 ואהרן משׁה הוא, 1Chr 26:26 that Shelomoth, 27:6; 2Chr 28:22; 32:12 (different from 2Kin 18:22), 18:30; 2Ki 33:23; Ezra 7:6: compare הֵם Neh 10:38 (compare in Syriac , Nö^§ 227): compare Psa 87:5; 1Sam 20:29.

2 It resumes the subject with emphasis:

a. when the predicate is a verb (especially if it be separated from its subject by an intervening clause), Gen 15:4 but one that shall come forth out of thine own bowels, יִירָשֶׁ֑ךָ הוּא he shall be thy heir, 3:12 the woman whom thou gavest to be with me, לי נתנה הוא she gave to me, 24:7; 44:17 etc. Judg 7:4; 2Sam 14:19 (throwing stress on יוֺאָב) 1Chr 11:20; Isa 33:15-16; 34:16; 38:19; 47:10; 59:16; 63:5; Hosea 7:8; often in Proverbs, as Hosea 10:18; Hosea 10:22; Hosea 10:24; Hosea 11:28; 13:13; Hos 19:21; Hos 22:9; Hos 24:12; 1Sam 1:13 (see Dr), Psalm 68:36.

b. when the predicate is a noun, Gen 2:14 and the fourth river, פְרָת הוּא it was the Euphrates, 2:19; 9:18; 15:2; 42:6 הוּא וְיוֺסֵף הַשַּׁלִּיט and Joseph, he was the ruler etc.: in sentences of the type ׳י נַחֲלָתְךָ הוּא לָכֶם, ׳י הַנִּלְחָם הוּא ׳י, הָאֱלֹהִים הוּא, Deut 3:22; 4:35; 7:9; 10:9; Josh 13:14, 33; Isa 9:14; 33:6; Hosea 11:5 (in these cases, to avoid stiffness, it is convenient often to drop the pronoun in translating, as 'And the fourth river was the Euphrates:' the pronoun, however, though it then corresponds to the substantive verb in English, does not really express it, the copula, as the examples shew, being in fact understood. Sts. in AV the pronoun is retained for emphasis, as Deuteronomy ll. cc.) So

c. after אֲשֶׁר in an affirmative sentence, ⟦N008⟧ Gen 9:3 all creeping things הוּאחַֿי אֲשֶׁר which are living, Lev 11:39; Num 9:13; 14:8; 35:31 לָמוּת רָשָׁע הוּא אֲשֶׁר who is guilty of death, Deut 20:20; 1Sam 10:19; Hag 1:9 and elsewhere (On

2, compare Dr^§ 199, with Obs.)

[… a szócikk folytatódik; a jelölt hivatkozások mind a fenti részben állnak]

=== H1935 ===
H1935. hod

I. הוֺד noun masculine^Job 37:22 splendour, majesty, vigour (chiefly in poetry) (Late Hebrew id.) — ׳ה absolute 37:22 8t. (׳הַה 1Chr 29:11); construct Isa 30:30 3t.; suffix הוֺדִי Dan 10:8; הוֺדְךָ Num 27:20 2t.; הוֺדֶ֑ךָ Prov 5:9; Psa 145:4; הוֺדוֺ Hosea 14:7 3t.; הֹדֹה Jer 22:18; —

1 splendour, majesty, of king וַהֲדָרֶ֑ךָ הוֺדְךָ Psa 45:4; עָלָיו תְּשַׁוֶּה וְהָדָר הוֺד 21:6, subject ׳י (|| בִּישׁוּעָתֶ֑ךָ כְּבוֺדוֺ גָּדוֺל); foretold of the צֶמַח Zech 6:13 עַלכִּֿסְאוֺ וּמָשַׁל וְיָשַׁב הוֺד וְהוּאיִֿשָּׂא; compare מַלְכוּת הוֺד עָלָיו וַיִּתֵּן 1Chr 29:25 (׳י confers on Solomon); מַלְכוּת ה עָלָיו ָנתְנוּ ׳וְלֹא Dan 11:21 (of usurper); see also הֹדֹה וְהוֺי Jer 22:18 in lament for king. Hence also

2 divine splendour, majesty, light & glory which God wears as king: לָבָ֑שְׁתָּ וְהָדָר הוֺד Psa 104:1 (7 t. joined with הָדָר); compare Job 40:10 where ׳י addresses Job in irony: הוֺד נוֺרָא עַלאֱֿלוֺהַּ 37:22; ׳ה לְפָנָיו וְהָדָר Psa 96:6 = 1Chr 16:26; also וְהַה וְהַנֵּצַח וְהַתִּפְאֶרֶת ׳וְהַגְּבוּרָה הַגְּדֻלָּה 29:11; see further הוֺדוֺ שָׁמַיִם כִּסָּה Hab 3:3 (|| הָאָ֑רֶץ מָֽלְאָה וּתְהִלָּתוֺ); עַלהַֿשָּׁמַיִם הוֺדְךָ אֲשֶׁרתְּֿנָה Psa 8:2 (|| שִׁמְךָ מָהאַֿדִּיר בְּכָלהָֿאָ֑רֶץ); also וְשָׁמָ֑יִם עַלאֶֿרֶץ הוֺדוֺ 148:13 (|| לְבַדּוֺ שְׁמוֺ נִשְׁגָּב); הוֺדֶ֑ךָ כְּבוֺד הֲדַר 145:4; of Yahweh's actions מָּעֳלוֺ וְהָדָר הוֺד 111:3; of his voice י ׳זְהִשְׁמִיעַ קוֺלוֺ אֶתהֿוֺד Isa 30:30.

3. a. splendour of Israel under divine blessing הוֺדוֺ כַזַּיִת וִיהִי Hosea 14:7.

b. majesty, dignity, authority of Moses Num 27:20 (P; only here in Hexateuch) עָלָיו מֵהוֺדְךָ וְנָתַתָּ. Also,

c. manly vigour מֶּןתִּֿתֵּן הוֺדֶ֑ךָ לַאֲחֵרִים Prov 5:9, in warning against licentiousness; as displayed in outward appearance לְמַשְׁחִית עָלַי נֶהְמַּךְ וְהוֺדִי ⟦N144⟧ Dan 10:8 (|| כֹּחַ).

4 of a horse, majesty, majestic force אֵימָה נַחְרוֺ הוֺד Job 39:20 the majesty of his snorting (majestic snorting, VB) is a terror; also in simile of Yahweh's dealings with Judah, בַּמִּלְחָמָה הוֺדוֺ כְּסוּס אוֺתָם וְשָׂם Zech 10:3 and he will make them like the horse of his majesty in battle.

=== H1961 ===
H1961. hayah

הָיָה verb fall out, come to pass, become, be (SI^1; SI^6 היה, ib^3 הית; parallel form of הוה, Arabic , Aramaic הֲוָא, ; see הָוָה above) —

Qal Perfect3masculine singular הָיָה Gen 3:1 +; וְהָיָה consecutive 4:14 +; 3 feminine singular הָֽיְתָה 1:2 +; הָיָ֑תָה Isa 14:24 3t.; וְהָֽיְתָה consecutive Gen 9:13 +; והית2Kin 9:37 Kt (Qr וְהָֽיְתָה); 2 masculine singular הָיִיתָ Deut 5:15 +; 1 singular הָיִיתִיGen 31:40; 3 plural הָיוּ 6:4 +; 2 masculine plural ם הֱיִיתֶ Exod 22:20 +; וִהְיִיתֶם consecutive Gen 3:5 +; 1 plural הָיִינוּIsa 1:9 +; etc.; Imperfect3masculine singular יִהְיֶה Gen 1:29 +; jussive יְהִי1:3 +; יֶהִ֑י Ezek 16:15; וִיהִיGen 1:6 +; וַיְהִי 1:3 +; וַיֶּ֑הִי Psa 33:9; Ezek 16:19; 3feminine singular תִּהְיֶה Gen 21:30 +; 1 singular אֶהְיֶה Exod 3:12 +; וָאֶהְיֶה2Sam 7:6 9t.; וָאֱהִי Judg 18:4 12t.; 3 masculine plural יִהְיוּ Gen 6:19 +; 3 feminine plural תִּהְיֶינָהIsa 16:2 19t.; תִּהְיֶין֣Gen 41:36 11t.; וְתִהְיֶנָה Jer 18:21; 48:6; וַתִּהְיֶינָה 2Sam 20:3 6t.; וַתִּהְיֶיןָGen 26:35; 1Sam 25:43; וַתִּהְיֶנָה 1Chr 7:15; 2masculine plural תִהְיוּ Gen 34:15 +; תִהְיוּן Exod 22:20; 1plural נִהְיֶה Gen 38:23 +; וַנִּהְיֶה2Sam 11:23; Jer 44:17; וַנְּהִי Num 13:33; Isa 64:5, etc.; Imperative masculine singular הֱיֵה Exod 18:19 +; feminine singular הֲיִיGen 24:60; masculine plural הֱיוּExod 19:15; Num 16:16, וִהְיוּ 1Sam 4:9 6t.; Infinitive absolute הָיוֺ Gen 18:18 3t.; הָיֹה 1Kin 13:32; Ezek 1:3; construct הֱיוֺת Gen 2:18 +, so read probably also for הֱיֵה Ezek 21:15 compare Sm Kö^i. p. 600 f. (Co em. היהלֿה to הָהֵל shine, glitter); with prefix בִּהְיוֺת Exod 5:13 +, בִּהְיֹת 19:16 +; לִהְיוֺת Gen 10:8 +, לִהְיֹת Exod 23:1 +; suffix הֱיוֺתִי Jonah 4:2; הֱיוֺתְךָ Judg 18:19 (twice in verse); לִהְיֹתְךָ Deut 26:10, etc.; Participle feminine הוֺיָה Exod 9:3.

I. 1. a. Fall out, happen הַדָּבָר מֶההָֿיָה 1Sam 4:16 how has the matter fallen out, (gone, turned out) ? so 2Sam 1:4; לוֺ מֶההָֿיָה Exod 32:1, 23 (both J E) what has happened to him? compare 1Sam 10:12; לָנוּ הָיָה הוּא מִקְרֶה 6:9 a chance it is that has befallen us; also כֵּן וַיְהִילֿוֺ 2Kin 7:20 and so it happened to him, compare וַיְהִיכֵֿן below

b. occur, take place, come about, come to pass: — מַגֵּפָה בָּעָם הָֽיְתָה גְדוֺלָה 1Sam 4:17 a great slaughter has taken place among the people, compare 2Sam 17:9; אֶפְרַיִם בְּיַעַר הַמִּלְחָמָה וַתְּהִי 18:6 and the battle took place in the wood of Ephraim (on אפרים compare Klo Dr); Josh 22:17 (P; of plague); אָלָה נָא תְּהִי Gen 26:28 (J) let an oath take place (be taken) so Judg 21:5; compare 2Kin 17:7 (si vera lectio), Ezek 16:34; especially late, 2Chr 29:36; 32:31; Eccl 1:9-10, 3:22; 8:7; 10:14 etc.; often of fulfilment of prediction, command, expectation, etc.: — הָיָה כֵּן Gen 41:3 (E) so it came to pass, 2Sam 13:25, וַיְהִיכֵֿן Judg 6:38; 2Kin 15:12; Isa 29:5, & especially וַיְהִיכֵֿן Gen 1:7, 9, 11, 15, 24, 30 (all P) +; כִדְבָרֶ֑ךָ יְהִי 30:34 let it be as thou sayest, Zech 6:15, see also יִהְיֶה וְלֹא תָקוּם לֹא Isa 7:7 it shall not arise (be realized) & shall not come about, Isaiah 14:34; (so often בּוֺא q. v. 2c, p. 98 above)

2 especially & very often, come about, come to pass followed by substantive (subject) clause almost always + modifying (usually temporal) clause or phrase:

a. ( I) וַיְהִי and it came to pass that, most often (c. 292 t.) followed by (a) Imperfect consecutive: a. with Infinitive with כְּ temporal Gen 12:14; 19:17 (both J) + 75 t. + Est 3:4 Qr (Hexateuch chiefly J, — so always Genesis, — & J E; P only Exod 16:10, D only Deut 5:20; 31:24; Josh 5:1; 9:1; 27t. in Kings), — somewhat different is 2Kin 7:18; β. with Infinitive with בְּ Gen 4:8; 11:2 (both J) + 29 t. + Est 3:4 Kt (in Hexateuch 10 t. J E; 3 t. P, Gen 19:29; Exod 34:29; Num 17:7; not in D); γ. with בְּ followed by temporal noun (בֹּקֶר יוֺם,, etc.) Gen 21:22 (E) 26:32 (J) + 45 t. (Hes 14 t. J E; 3 t. P, Exod 6:28; 16:13; Num 7:1; not D); δ. לְ temporal 1Sam 1:20; 2Sam 13:23; 1Kin 20:26; 1Chr 20:1; ε. כְּ temporal Gen 39:11; 1Sam 25:38; ζ. מִן temporal namely: מֵאָז Gen 39:5, מִיּוֺם 1Sam 7:2; compare 30:25, מִיָּמִים Judg 11:4; 15:1, מִקֵּץ Gen 4:3; 8:6 6t., מִקְצֵה Josh 3:2; 2Kin 8:3; Ezek 3:16, especially מִמָּחֳרָת Gen 19:34; Exod 18:13 11t.; η. כַּאֲשֶׁר temporal Gen 12:11 (J) 20:13 (E) + 31 t. (Hexateuch 13 t. J E, Deut 2:16, not P), characteristic of Nehemiah's memoris, Nehemiah 3:33; Neh 4:1; 4:6; 4:9; 6:1, 16; 7:1; 13:19; θ. with כִּי temporal Gen 6:1 (J) Exod 3:21 (E) + 14 t. + Judg 16:25 Kt; ι. אַחֲרֵי temporal Gen 22:20 (J) + 13 t., אַחַר 39:7; Job 42:7, אַחֲרֵיכֵֿן Judg 16:14 6t.; κ. followed by עַד temporal 1Sam 14:19 (on which compare Dr); compare וְעַדכֹּֿה עַדכֹּֿה 1Kin 18:45; λ. with combinations; as בְּ with temporal noun + בְּ Infinitive Gen 34:25; Exod 19:16; 1Sam 25:37; בְּ Infinitive + כְּ Infinitive Josh 3:14; double preposition חֳדָשִׁים כְּמִשְּׁלשׁ Gen 38:24; מִקְצֵה + אֲשֶׁר אַחֲרֵי Josh 9:16; מִיָּמִים + id. 23:1; μ. other unusual constructions under this head are: those where temporal idea is expressed by a circumstantial clause Isa 22:7; 1Kin 13:20; 2Kin 8:21 (Dr^§ 165, & Οβς.) = 2Chr 21:9; those with indefinite כֹּל 1Sam 10:11; 2Sam 2:23; 15:2, or with participle = rel. clause 1Sam 11:1 (on all see Dr^§ 78 n. & Sm); quite unique is 1Kin 16:31, with וגו ׳לָכְתּוֺ הֲנָקֵלin place of temporal clause (b) וַיְהִי sometimes followed by simple Perfect, with a negative Gen 39:10 (with כְּ Infinitive), Exod 13:17 (with בְּ Infinitive) 2Kin 12:7; 17:25 (both with בְּ temporal); & without negative, Gen 8:13 (P) 14:1 +, usually with בְּ temporal (38 t.), Hexateuch chiefly P; rarely with other preposition & adverb as above; note especially מִדֵּי followed by Infinitive 1Sam 18:30; 2Chr 12:11; sometimes the subject of following Perfect precedes it with וְ, as Gen 22:1; 41:1; 1Sam 18:19; 30:1; 2Kin 2:9; 4:40 +; note especially 2Sam 17:27 where several subject & also several objects precede the Perfect (compare Dr); in these cases temporal modifier is occasionally a circumstantial clause 13:30; 1Kin 8:10; 2Kin 19:37 = Isa 37:38; rarely subject precedes Perfect without וְ 1Kin 11:4; 21:1, compare subject preceding Participle without וְ Neh 4:10; in 2Chr 8:1 the object (with וְ) precedes what is apparently the principal verb (c) וַיְהִי followed by וְהִנֵּה Gen 24:15 (J; טֶרֶם in temporal clause), 29:25 (E), 38:27 (J, both בְּ temporal) 38:29 (כְּ Participle), 1Sam 13:10 (כְּ Infinitive); also 2Sam 1:2; 13:36; 15:32 (circumstantial clause) 2Kin 2:11 (id.), 3:20; 13:21 (circumstantial clause). (d) rarely וַיְהִי (in this sense) followed by other constructions: יִתֵּן אָז 1Kin 9:10 (with מִקְצֵה); Imperfect frequentative 14:28 (with מִדֵּיבּֿאֹ), 2Kin 4:8 (with עָבְרוֺ מִדֵּי) Jer 36:23 (with כְּ Infinitive); Perfect consecutive frequentative 2Chr 24:11 (with בְּ temporal). (2) rarely also Perfect with וְ conjunctive וְהָיָה (compare Dr^§ 183) followed by Imperfect consecutive as subject clause: 1Sam 1:12 (c. several circumstantial clause), 10:9 (with כְּ Infinitive) 2Kin 3:15 (id.), 1Sam 17:48 (with כִּי), Jer 37:11 (with בְּ Infinitive), Amos 7:2 (with אִם temporal); also Jer 3:9 (apparently with מִן causative, but obscure); 1Sam 13:22 (with בְּ temporal) is followed by וְלֹא & Perfect as well as Imperfect consecutive

b. less often וְהָיָה Perfect consecutive and it shall come to pass, or frequentative,came to pass (repeatedly, etc.) usually (a) followed by simple Imperfect (c. 100 t.): a. with בְּ temporal Deut 21:16; 25:19 37t., (especially הַהוּא בַּיּוֺם Hosea 2:18; Hosea 2:33; Isa 7:18, 21, 23 21t.); β. with בְּ local Ezek 47:23; Zech 13:8 compare also Hosea 2:1 (אֲשֶׁר בִּמְקוֺם, but perhaps = instead of, compare VB); γ. with indefinite rel. clause, or its equivalents (כֹּל indefinite, etc.), compare Dr^§ 121, Obs. 1: Gen 4:14; Numbers 17:20; Deut 12:11; 18:19; Josh 7:14-15, Judg 7:4; 1Sam 2:36; 17:25; 2Sam 15:35; 1Kin 19:17; 20:6; Isa 4:3; 24:18; Nahum 3:7; Jer 27:8; 42:4; Ezek 47:9; ⟦N110⟧ Zech 14:17; Joel 3:5; δ. with כַּאֲשֶׁר Num 33:56; Deut 28:63; Josh 23:15; Judg 7:17; Isa 29:8; Jer 31:28; Zech 8:13; ε. less often with other modifying phrase: כְּ Infinitive Exod 33:8-9, (both frequentative), Josh 3:13; 8:8; Jer 25:12; 51:63; בְּ Infinitive Gen 9:14 (P) 2Kin 4:10; Ezek 44:17; אִם of condition Deut 20:11 (+ 20:11 Dr^§ 118 n.), also Josh 22:18, where the arrangement is peculiar, & the condition is expressed without אִם (compare Dr^§ 155); לְ temporal Deut 23:12; מִקֵּץ Isa 23:17; טֶרֶם 65:24; בְּחָדְשׁוֺ חֹדֶשׁ מִדֵּי 66:23, etc. (b) וְהָיָה followed by Perfect consecutive (c. 85 t.): α. most often + clause with כִּי temporal Gen 12:12; 46:33 (both J) Exod 1:10 (E) Deut 11:29; Isa 8:21 25t.; β. with אִם Exod 4:8-9, Num 15:24; Deut 11:13-14t.; also Gen 38:9; Num 21:9 (both frequentative); γ. with בְּ temporal Gen 30:41 (frequentative) 47:24 (but compare Ol Di) Exod 16:5 11t.; δ. with indefinite rel. clause or equivalent (Dr^§ 121 Obs. 1): Gen 24:14; Num 10:32 (Dr^§ 118 n.) 21:18; Deut 21:3; Judg 11:31; 19:30; Zech 14:16; ε. occasionally with other modifying phrase: כַּאֲשֶׁר Gen 27:40 +; כְּ Infinitive 44:31 +; בְּ Infinitive Exod 33:22 +; מִקֵּץ 2Sam 14:26; עֵקֶב causative Deut 7:12; also (c) followed by וְלֹא + simple Imperfect Exod 3:21 (with כִּי temporal). (d) וְהָיָה followed by Imperative (very rare) Deut 6:10 (with כִּי temporal), 1Sam 10:7 (with כִּי), compare 29:10, compare Perfect consecutive in command 3:9 (with אִם). (e) וְהָיָה followed by simple Perfect (also very rare) Deut 8:19 (with אִם). (f) וְהָיָה followed by clause without verb: Gen 24:43; Josh 2:19; 1Kin 18:24 (all with indefinite rel. modifier, or equivalents, see (a) γ, (b) δ, above). (g) וְהָיָה (ו conjunctive) followed by Imperfect consecutive see a. (e) above

II. Come into being, become: —

[… a szócikk folytatódik; a jelölt hivatkozások mind a fenti részben állnak]

=== H1969 ===
H1969. hin

הִין noun masculine^Lev 19:36 hin, a liquid measure (ᵐ5 εἴν (Ἴν; ᵐ5^L Ἴν); but χοῦς 19:36; Greek ἴυιον, Egyptian measure, = sextarius, accusative to Böckh^Metrol. Unters. 244 compare Brandis^Münz- Mass- & Gewichtswesen 29, 30; the Hebrew hin (6.06 litres) was, however, much larger than the Egyptian hin (0.456 litre), see Hultsch^Metrol., Tab. xxi. p. 714, & pp. 368, 450 ff.; see further Ri^HWB Smith^Dict.Bib. EMey^Geschichte. i. 228) — absolute הִין Exod 30:24 4t. Ezekiel 45:46; הַהִין 29:40 (twice in verse) + 14 t.; construct הִין Lev 19:36; —

1 a measure, used of quantity of water for drinking Ezek 4:11; usually in connection with offerings:

a. of oil used with meal-offering Exod 29:40; Num 15:4, 6, 9; 28:5 (all P), Ezek 45:24; 46:5-6, 11, 14; — usually 1/4 hin to 1/10 ephah (of meal-offering); Num 15:6 1/3; 15:9 1/2; in Ezekiel a (whole) hin, except Ezek 46:14 3-Janhin to 1/6. ephah.

b. used in mixing the anointing oil Exod 30:24.

c. of wine as drink-offering Lev 23:13 (H); elsewhere P, Exod 29:40; Num 15:5; 28:14 (all 1/4 hin) 15:7; 28:14 (both 1/3 hin), 15:10; 28:14 (both 1/2 hin; all with 1/10 to 3/10 ephah in case of meal-offering).

d. once of שֵׁכָר as drink-offering Num 28:7 (P; 1/4 hin; compare Di).

2 vessel with capacity of hin Lev 19:36 (H) הִיןצֶֿדֶק a just hin (|| צ ׳אֵיפַת ׳אַבְנֵיצֿ, צ, ׳מאֹזְנֵי). — The hin = 1/6 bath according to Joseph.^Ant. iii. 8, 3; 9, 4, = 6.06 litres (Hultsch^l.c.), see II. בַּת. On order of words in above passages, note that הִין (ה ׳רְבִיעִת etc.) is usually appositive of liquid measured, & either precedes it Exod 29:40; Num 15:4; 28:14, or, more often, follows it Exod 30:24 10t.; Num 28:7 they are in different clauses; in Exod 29:40 we find הַהִין בְּשֶׁמֶןרֶ֗֗֗בַע, compare Num 15:6, 9; ⟦N141⟧ 28:5; — 28:14 is peculiar: יָ֑יִן לַכֶּבֶשׂ הַהִין וּרְבִיעִת לָאַיִל הַהִין וּשְׁלִישִׁת לַמָּר יִהְיֶה הַהִין חֲצִי וְנִסְכֵּיהֶם (יין only at end).

=== H1980 ===
H1980. halak

הָלַךְ verb go, come, walk (MI לך ואהלך,; SI וילכו; Late Hebrew הִלֵּךְ (rare), Aramaic הַךְ הֲלַךְ, (also Biblical Aramaic), oftener הַלֵּיךְ Pa`el, , Palmyrene Ithpe`el אתילכא Vog^No. 15, 1. 4; Assyrian alâku Dl^w; also Arabic perish) —

Qal Perfect3masculine singular ׳ה Gen 26:26 +; הָלָ֑ךְ 2Sam 8:6 +; 3 plural הָֽלְכוּ Gen 14:24 +; (הֶ)הָֽלְכוּא Josh 10:24 (Ges^§ 23, 3; 138, 3 b Kö^i. 414 f.), etc.; Imperfect rarely

a.3masculine singular יִהֲלֹךְ Jer 9:3-4t. Job, Psalms; וַיַּהֲלֹךְ Job 14:20; 3feminine singular תִּהֲלַךְ Psa 73:9; וַתִּהֲלַךְ Exod 9:23 (Ew^§ 138 b Ges^§ 69, R. 8 Kö^i. 415); 1 singular אֶהֱלֹךְ 16:22; 23:8 (compare M 1:14; M 1:15); יִהֲלֹ֑כוּ Job 41:11; usually

b. (629 t.) as if from ילך (ולך; see

Hiph`il Ges^§ 69, 2 R. 8 Kö^i. 414 ff. Prät^ZAW 1882, 310 ff.; compare also יָלַד etc.) 3 masculine singular יֵלֵךְ Exod 10:24 +; יֵלֵ֑ךְ Eccl 5:15 +; וְיֵלַ֑ךְ Job 27:21; יֵלֶךְֿ Exod 34:9 +; וַיֵּלֶךְ Gen 12:4 (twice in verse) + 210 t., וַיֵּלַ֑ךְ 24:61 6t.; 3 feminine singular תֵּלֵךְ 24:39 תֵּלֵ֑ךְ Psa 97:3; וַתֵּ֫לֶךְ Gen 7:18 +; 2 masculine singular תֵּלֵךְ etc. 3:14 +; 2 feminine singular תֵּלְכִי etc. Judg 4:8 (twice in verse) +; 1singular אֵלֵךְ Exod 3:11 +; וָאֵלֵךְ Judg 19:18 +; וָאֵלַ֑ךְ Job 19:10; אֵלְכָה Gen 45:28 +, אֵילְכָה Micah 1:8; 3masculine plural יֵלְכו Exod 5:7 +; יֵלֵכ֑וּן Psa 89:31 2t.; וַיֵּלְכוּ Gen 9:23 (וילכו also SI^4) +; 3 feminine plural תֵּלַכְנָה Judg 11:40 +; 2 masculine plural תֵּלְכוּ Gen 42:38 +; תֵּלְכוּן Deut 6:14; תֵּלֵכ֑וּן Exod 3:21; Isa 52:12; 2feminine plural תֵּלַכְנָה Ruth 1:11; 1plural נֵלֵךְ Exod 8:23 +; וַנֵּ֫לֶךְ Deut 1:19, etc.; Imperative rarely

a. הֲלוֺךְ Prov 13:20 (Kt.; Qr הֹלֵךְ, compare De Now); masculine plural הִלְכוּ Jer 51:10; usually

b. (241 t.) masculine singular לֵךְ Gen 26:16 + (לך also MI^14), לֶךְֿ 27:9 +; לְכָה 19:32 +; לְךָ Num 23:13; Judg 19:13; 2Chr 25:17; לֵ֑כָה 1Sam 23:17; feminine singular לְכִי Judg 9:10 +; masculine plural לְכוּ Gen 37:20 +; feminine plural לֵ֫כְנָה Ruth 1:8, לֵ֫כְןָ 1:12, etc.; Infinitive absolute הָלוֺךְ Gen 8:3 +; הָלֹךְ 31:30 +; construct rarely a. הֲלֹךְ Exod 3:19 4t.; הֲלָךְֿ Eccl 6:9; usually b. (129 t.) לֶ֫כֶת Micah 6:8 +; לָ֑כֶת Prov 15:21 2t.; suffix לֶכְתִּי 1Kin 2:8; לֶכְתָּם 2Sam 17:21 7t. (6 Ezekiel); לֶכְתָּן Ezek 1:9, 12, 17, etc.; Participle הֹלֵךְ Gen 18:16 +; הוֺלֵךְ 15:2 +; feminine הֹלְכָה Jer 3:6; הֹלֶכֶת Gen 32:21 2t.+ הֹלָכֶתי 2Kin 4:23; plural הֹלְכִים Exod 33:15 +, etc.; construct הֹלְכֵי Judg 5:6 3t.; feminine plural הֹלְכֹת etc. Exod 2:5 3t.; — found in all documents of Hexateuch, especially J E (240 t.; D 65 t., P 30 t. + H 14 t.); — I. literal

1 of persons,

a. go, proceed, move, walk Gen 22:6, 8; Exod 17:5; Amos 3:3; Zeph 1:17 + often; of cherubim Ezek 10:16 +? 1:9, 12, 19, 20, 21, 24; opposed to sitting(ישׁב) Deut 6:7; 11:19 (both with בַּדֶּרֶךְ); || running (רוץ) Prov 4:12; Isa 40:31, compare also 2Chr 30:6; distinguished from riding, driving Gen 24:65 (J; followed by לִקְרָאתֵנוּ בַּשָּׂדֶה), Judg 5:10 (עַלהַֿדֶּרֶךְ), Isa 41:3 (בְּרַגְלָיו), compare Eccl 5:1 (followed by אֶל), 10:7 (followed by עַלהָֿאָרֶץ); on usage with preposition see below; but also || וַיִּרְכַּב 1Kin 13:13; 18:45 compare 2Kin 9:16; הַסּוּס רֹכֵב וַיֵּלֶךְ 9:18 (followed by לִקְרָאתוֺ); וַתָּבאֹ וָלֵךְוַ֗֗֗תֵּלָךְ נְהַג 4:24 drive and go forward. . . and she went, and came.

b. depart, go away: Gen 18:33 (J), and ׳י departed, 34:17 (P) then we will take our daughter and depart; compare 1Sam 14:3; 15:27; Exod 3:19 (J), 12:28 (P) 2Kin 5:25 +; go, go out (to a specific duty etc.): of Samuel 1Sam 7:16, Barak Judg 4:8 (twice in verse); go out, forth (opposed to שׁוּב) Ruth 1:21 +; = go into exile (without modifier) 1 Chronicles 5:41 compare Jer 22:10 (הֹלֵךְ of one going into exile; opposed to death); see further שָׁם הָֽלְכוּ אֲשֶׁר Ezek 37:21.

c. less often where English idiom requires or prefers come, (approach, arrive), verb in Hebrew being referred to starting point; mostly with preposition (on usage with preposition in General see below): — אֵלַי לְכָה 1Sam 17:44; so in prayer to לָ֫נוּ לִישֻׁעָ֫תָה וּלְכָה ׳י, Psa 80:3; also in Yahweh's exhortations to Israel: Isa 55:1; (לַמַּיִם), 55:3 (אֵלַי); come to, arrive at a land (ָ ה locative) Gen 29:1; come unto (אֶל) a people Ruth 2:11; but compare especially (without preposition) Imperative followed by another Imperative, etc., see below 5f, & particular cases like Num 22:6, 11, 17; 23:7 (twice in verse); Isa 55:1 (twice in verse) etc., where actual summons to approach the speaker.

d. with modifiers : (I) a. of space traversed: כָּלהַֿמִּדְבָּר אֵת Deut 1:19 (compare I. אֵת 2), כָּלהַֿבִּתְרוֺן 2Sam 2:29; especially with accusative דֶּרֶךְ way, Gen 35:3 (E) Num 20:17(JE) Deut 1:31; 2Sam 4:7; 1Kin 13:12 (twice in verse); Jer 52:7 compare הָלָ֑כְתָּ אֲשֶׁר בַּדֶּרֶךְ תָשׁוּב וְלֹא 1Kin 13:9; דֶּרֶךְ הֹלֵךְ = traveler Isa 35:8; followed by אֳרָחוֺת Judg 5:6 (song), participle construct נְתִיבוֺת הֹלְכֵי 5:6 goers of paths (wayfarers); c, accusative דֶּרֶךְ = journey Prov 7:19; β. with accusative of time Deut 2:14; 2Sam 2:32, compare Josh 5:6; 14:10; also, with combination of ideas of space & time יָמִים שְׁלשֶׁת דֶּרֶךְ Exod 3:18 (+ בַּמִּדְבָּר see below) compare 5:3; 8:23; 15:22 (all J E), Num 33:8 (P) 1Kin 19:4. (2) with preposition: a. followed by בְּ of space (territory, country, etc.) in which בַּמִּדְבָּר Judg 11:16, 18; compare הֵנָּה וְאַחַת הֵנָּה אַחַת בַּבַּיִת וַיֵּלֶךְ 2Kin 4:35 and he walked in the house once hither, once thither; especially + accusative of time or distance (compare above) Josh 5:6; 14:10 (both D) 1Kin 19:4 בָּֽעֲרָבָה 2Sam 2:29; compare בַּיַּבָּשָׁה Exod 14:29 = 15:19 (P ?); הָעֵמֶק בְּתוֺךְ Josh 8:13 (JE); followed by בְּ of way, path בְּדֶרֶךְ 1Kin 18:6 (twice in verse) ׳בַּדּ Num 21:22 (JE) Josh 24:17 (E) Deut 1:33; 2:27; Judg 18:6; 1Sam 28:22; בָּהּ הָלַכְתָּ אֲשֶׁר בַּדֶּרֶךְ לָלֶכֶת תָשׁוּב לֹא 1Kin 13:17 (הָלַךְ of both going & coming); so often figurative, compare below II. 3; walk in the streets בִּרְחֹבֹת Lam 4:18, walk by the way ׳בַּדּ (opposed to יָשַׁב sit) Deut 6:7; 11:19; β. followed by עַל, go or walk on (by) הַיְאֹר עַליַֿד Exod 2:5; עַלהַֿדֶּרֶךְ 1Sam 9:6 (i.e. set out, start, upon compare Dr); construct followed by לְ Judg 5:10. (3) go to, unto: a. followed by accusative, in General תֵּלֵךְ בְּכָלאֲֿשֶׁר Gen 28:15 (J) whithersoever thou goest Josh 1:7, 9 (D), compare 2Sam 7:9; 8:6, 14 = 1Chr 17:8; 18:6, 13; more definitely, to a land, with or without ארץ Judg 1:26; 21:21; Jer 37:12; so figurative Hosea 7:11 resort to (accusative) Asshur (|| קרא); to a city 1Sam 11:14-15, 23:5; 1Kin 2:8; 11:24; 12:1; 14:2, 4; 2Kin 9:1; 1Chr 11:4; 2Chr 8:3; Ezra 8:31; to a house בַּיִת 2Sam 13:7-8, house of ׳י Psa 122:1; a field, Gen 27:5 (J; + Infinitive of purpose); valley of salt 2Chr 25:11. β. followed by ָ ה locative, אַ֫רְצָה Gen 11:31; 12:5, אֲרָם מַּדֶּ֫נָת 28:2, 5, 7 (all P) compare 29:1 (E) Num 32:39 (JE) 2Sam 14:23; of city Gen 18:22; 28:10 (both J) Judg 9:1; 1Sam 15:34; 16:13; 19:22; 2Sam 15:9; 1Kin 3:4; 18:45; 2Chr 10:1; of river Euphrates Jer 13:4, 6, 7; of a wood 1Sam 23:16; a mountain Josh 2:16 (JE); compare also הָֽלְכוּ אָ֫נָה whither have they gone Gen 16:8; 32:18; Josh 2:5 (all J E) compare Neh 2:16; Song 6:1; Judg 19:17, וָאָ֫נָה אָ֫נֶה 1Kin 2:42 (but also ה ׳אָן 1Sam 10:14); שָׁ֫מָּה Jer 40:4 but also שָׁם 1Sam 9:6; Jer 45:5; הֵ֫נָּה 2Kin 4:35. γ. with preposition: (a) most often followed by אֶלֿ unto,, e.g., unto the place, הַמָּקוֺם, Gen 22:3 (E) 30:25 (J), etc.; of land 22:2 (E) 24:4, 10 (J) 36:6 (P) Josh 22:9 (D), etc.; of city Gen 22:19 (E) 1Sam 9:10; Jonah 3:3, etc.; of house Gen 24:38 (J) Eccl 7:2 (twice in verse); house of God Ecclesiastes 4:17; of people Exod 19:10 (E) etc.; in other designations of place, Josh 8:9 (JE), Ezra 10:6; Ruth 2:9,.etc., also followed by אֶל = against, of military expedition: Judg 1:10 against Canaanites; a city 9:1; 1Kin 22:15 2Chr 18:5, 14; also followed by אֶל of individual persons Gen 26:1, 26 (both J), ⟦N065⟧ 41:55 (E) 2Kin 6:22-23, 22:14 2Chr 34:22; Jer 41:14 +; once with implicit coire cum femina Amos.2:7 (compare בּוֺא 1e, p. 98); see especially בַּמִּשְׁמָּט אֶלאֵֿל לַהֲלֹךְ Job 34:23 of judicial inquiry by God; come unto the speaker Num 22:16, 37 ( both J E), unto the one addressed Isa 60:14. (b) rarely followed by עַלֿ unto (Jeremiah Kings Ezekiel) with בַּיִת 1Kin 20:43, שָׂדֶה 2:26 (+ accusative of location), compare אֲשֶׂר עַלכָּֿל Jer 1:7, שָׁם יִהְיֶה עַלאֲֿשֶׁר Ezek 1:12 (of the רוּחַ; compare שָׁ֫מָּה יִהְיֶה אֶלאֲֿשֶׁר 1:20); also הוֺלֵךְ אֲשֶׁראֲֿנִי עַלֹ הוֺלֵךְ וַאֲנִיִ 2Sam 15:20 = I am going I know not where compare Lag^Psalt. Hieron. 1874, 156 f. Dr^Sm; see further Jer 3:6 (followed by both עַל & אֶל; on confusion of אֶל and לְַ see these words); followed by עַל against city 1Kin 22:6, king 2Chr 22:5. (c) followed by עַד as far as, unto, place עַדכֹּֿה Gen 22:5 (E), compare 1Kin 12:30 (דָּ֑ן עַד הָאֶחָד לִפְנֵי הָעָם וַיֵּלְכוּ), 2Kin 6:2; 10:25; 7:15 compare 2Chr 26:8; persons 1Sam 9:9. (d) followed by לְ, to a land 1Kin 10:13 etc.; a city 2Chr 8:17; a mountain 1Chr 4:42; a bamah 2Chr 1:3; compare גְדֹר לִמְבוֺא 1Chr 4:39 & see below; also before proper name of person Gen 24:56 (J); further לַמִּלְחָמָה Ezek 7:14; לְסִבְלֹתיכֶם Exod 5:4 (J) go to your burdens compare Isa 60:3, לְמוֺאל הַהוֺלֶכֶת הַשֵׁנִית וְהַתּוֺדָה Neh 12:38 the second thanksgiving choir, that went the opposite way; frequently לְדַרְכּוֺ etc., go (to, on) one's way Gen 19:2 (J) 32:2 (E) Num 24:25; Josh 2:16 (both J E) Judg 18:26; 1Sam 1:18; 26:25 +; לְמַסָּעָיו Gen 13:3 (J); compare לְמֶרְחֲבֵיאֶֿדֶץ הַהוֺלֵךְ Hab 1:6 that marcheth through the breath of the land (of invader); note especially in sense of depart to, go away to, almost = return to; לְאֹהָלָיךָ וְהָלַכְתָּ Deut 16:7 and thou shalt go (back) to thy tents, so Judg 19:6; 20:8; 1Kin 8:66; 12:16 2Chr 10:16; לְבֵיתוֺ 1Sam 10:26; compare 14:8; 23:18; 1Kin 14:12; 1Chr 16:43; לִמְקוֺמוֺ 1Sam 2:20 (compare VB Dr) 14:46; see also go away or off to 1Chr 4:42; 2Chr 8:17. (e) followed by בְּ into בַּשָּׂדֶה Num 22:23 (of Balsam's ass); especially in ׳ה בּשְּׁבִי go into captivity Amos 9:4; Nahum 3:10; Deut 28:41; Jer 20:6; 22:22; 30:16; Lam 1:18 (= ׳ה שְׁבִי 1:5) compare Ezek 30:17-18, Isa 46:2; 48:11 בַּגּוֺלָה׳ה Jer 48:11; Ezek 25:3; see also ׳ה בַּכְּלִמָּה Isa 45:16; ׳ה בַּמִּלְתָמָה 1Sam 17:13; ׳ה בַּקְּרָ֑ב 2Sam 17:11 (but see Dr). (4) go from, depart from, followed by מִן, e.g. 1Kin 2:41, etc., מִשָּׁם Gen 42:26 (E) 1Kin 19:19; compare מֵרוּחֶ֑ךָ אֵלֵךְ אָנָה Psa 139:7; note especially י ׳וּמַלְאַךְ מֵעֵינָיו הָלַךְ Judg 6:21 compare 1Sam 14:17 (מֵעִמָנוּ), Hosea 9:6 (מִשֹּׁד, from destruction; also followed by מֵאֵת of divorced woman Jer 3:1 (מֵאִתּוֺ), compare also Gen 26:31 (J), Josh 22:9 (?P) מֵאֵצֶל 1Kin 20:36. (5) with preposition denoting other relations: — a. אֵת of accompaniment, ׳ה אִתִּי (on warlike expedition) Gen 14:24 compare 2Sam 15:11; Josh 10:24; Judg 1:3 (followed by בְּ into), 7:4 (twice in verse), see also 1Sam 23:23; 2Sam 16:17 +. β. עִם Judg 4:8 (twice in verse); 4:9 (twice in verse); 7:4; 11:8; 1Sam 30:22 (twice in verse); 2Sam 19:26; Num 22:13-14, (JE) +. γ. בְּ with (compare בְּ III. 1, p. 89), especially Exod 10:9, compare Hosea 5:6 אֶתיֿ לְבַקֵּשׁ יֵלְכוּ וּבִבְּקָרָם ׳בְּצאֹנָם, of vain sacrifices; 2Chr 30:6 the runners went with (בְּ) letters. δ בְּקֶרֶב in the midst of, Exod 34:9 (JE). ε. לִפְנֵי in front of, before, before Cyrus Isa 45:2, (compare of fire & pestilence, below

3); of ׳י going before Israel Exod 13:21; Num 14:14, compare also Exod 23:23; 32:34 & (in meaning) 33:14 (יֵלֵ֑כוּ מָּנַי), 33:15, Isa 52:12, with which camp. ׳וה צִדְקֶ֑ךָ לְפָנֶיךָ 58:8; of other gods Exod 32:1, 23 (all J E); לְנֶגְדֶּ֑ךָ Gen 33:12 (J). **׳ה לִפְנֵי also (less often) = go (and present oneself) before a deity or shrine 1Kin 12:30; 1Chr 21:30 (compare לפני בוא Exod 28:30, מְּנֵי קִדֵּם Psa 95:2). ζ. with אַחַר go after, follow, persons Gen 37:17 (J) 2Kin 7:15; אַחֲרֵי Gen 24:5, 8, 39, 61 (all J), Josh 3:3 (D), Num 16:25 (JE) +; compare also II. 3 d (going after false gods, etc.); מֵאַחֲרֵיהֶם Exod 14:19 (J). η. לְ with sense of dativus ethicus [dative of interest]: אֶלאַֿרְצוֺ לוֺ וַיֵּלֶךְ 18:27 (E) and he gat him (betook himself) to his country; אֶל לִי אֵ֫לֶךְ Song 4:6 I will get me unto; וְנֵלְכָהלָֿנוּ 1Sam 26:11 and let us be gone, compare 26:12, Song 2:11 (of rain compare infr, 3); Imperative in same construction: לֶךְלְֿךָאֶל Gen 22:2 (E) get thee unto, לְאָהֳלֵיכֶם לָכֶם לְכוּ Josh 22:4 (D) also מִן לֶךְלְֿךָ Gen 12:1 (J), & לְכִילָֿךְ Song 2:10, 13 get thee away, come away. θ adverbial phrases of manner with preposition: (a) ׳ה בְּשָׁלוֺם בְּ, go in peace Gen 26:31(J) 1Sam 29:7; 2Sam 3:21-22, 23; 15:9; ׳ה הַהִיא הָאֲכִילָה בְּכֹהַ 1Kin 19:8, ׳ה בְּחֵמָה 2Kin 5:12 compare also 1Sam 17:39; Isa 52:12; ׳וְה תֵּימָן בְּסַעֲרוֺת Zech 9:14 of ׳י, and he shall go in (or with) storms of the south; בְּ pretii, בְּנַפְשׁוֺתָם הַהֹלְכִים 2Sam 23:17 who went at risk of their life (otherwise in || 1Chr 11:19); compare (b) ׳ה אֶלנַֿפְשׁוֺ אֶל, 1Kin 19:3 he went for his life. (c) ׳ה לְרַגְלָהּ לְ, 1Sam 25:42 go according to her foot, i.e. follow, attend her; לְשָׁלוֺם לֵךְ go to, for (in) peace Exod 4:18 (E) Judg 18:6; 1Sam 1:17; 20:13, 42; 2Kin 5:19; לְתֻמָּם וְהֹלְכִים 2Sam 15:11. κ. with adjective or noun, agreeing with subject of verb, in accusative (Ges^§ 118.5), sometimes where Latin & English use an attributive or appositive: walk barefoot 15:30; walk naked and barefoot Isa 20:3 (with accusative of duration); רגו גָּרוֺן נְטֻו֯וֺת ׳וַתֵּלַכְנָה 3:16; compare 1Kin 8:66; also בְּעַמֶּ֑ךָ רָכִיל תֵלֵךְ לֹא Lev 19:16 (H) thou shalt not walk as a slanderer among thy people, Jer 9:3, רָכִיל הוֺלֵךְ Prov 11:13; 20:19; and even רָכִיל הֹלְכֵי Jer 6:28 (yet רכיל possibly abstract, ׳ה ׳ר walk in slander); with adverb accusative, construed more Generally (Ges^§ 118, 5.c., Ew^§ 279 c); ye shall not walk loftily (רוֺמָה) Micah 2:3; they shall come bending (שְׁחוֺחַ) Isa 60:14; and Agag came delicately (מַעֲדַנֹּת) 1Sam 15:32; compare also Lev 26:23 (II. 3b) Exod 3:21; 2Sam 17:18.

=== H1992 ===
H1992. hem

הֵ֫מָּה and הֵם (without appreciable distinction in usage, except probably in so far as the longer or shorter from was better adapted to the rhythm of particular sentences: on the whole הֵמָּה (alone) is somewhat more frequently than הָהֵם הֵם, on the contrary is said regularly, הָהֵמָּה occurring only 12 t., namely Num 9:7 (P), Jer 14:15; Zech 14:15, and in the phrase הָהֵמָּה בַּ(הַ)מִָים 2Kin 18:4; Jer 3:16, 18; 5:18; 50:4; Joel 3:2; Joel 4:1; Zech 8:23; Neh 13:15, though הָהֵם בַּיָּמִים is much more common)

pronoun 3 plural masculine (הֵמָּה used incorrectly for the feminine Zech 5:10; Ruth 1:22; Song 6:8), feminine הֵ֫נָּה [Late Hebrew also הֵן, which occurs however in Biblical Hebrew only after a prefix: see below 8],

pronoun 3 plural they (Biblical Aramaic הִמּוֺ, q. v.; , ; Ethiopic (one form) 'em¥n-t¥, em¹n-t¥; compare also for the dissyllabic form, the suffix -œm¥ [feminine +?]; perhaps also Assyrian suffix šunu, šina [ni], compare references below הוּא). In usage not different in Genl. from הִיא הוּא, (which see, under the corresponding headings, for fuller explanations), thus: —

1. a. Gen 6:4; Exod 5:7 יֵָֽלְכוּ הֵם let them (emphatic) go and gather straw, ⟦N049⟧ Num 31:16; Isa 24:14; Hosea 4:14; 7:3 etc.; after כִּי Jer 5:5; 34:7. (For the use in circumstantial clauses see Dr^§ 160, 169.)

b. Judg 1:22 הֵם גַם ֗֗֗ ויעלו, Deut 2:11 (הֵם אַף), 3:20; Josh 9:4; Ezek 30:17.

[… a szócikk folytatódik; a jelölt hivatkozások mind a fenti részben állnak]

=== H2022 ===
H2022. har

[הוֺר], assumed by Thes and others as ancient form for הַר mountain, to explain הוֺרַי Gen 49:26, read הוֺרֵיעָֿד; but better הַרֲרֵיעַֿד everlasting mountains, see הַר below הרר.

הַר noun masculine^Gen 7:19 mountain, hill, hill-country (Late Hebrew id., plural הֲרָרִים הָרִים,; Phoenician הר CIS^i. 3, 17; Sabean suffix הרתהמו DHM^ZMG 1876, 673); — ׳ה absolute Josh 17:18 +; with article הָהָר Exod 3:12 +; with ָ ה locative הֶ֫רָה Gen 14:10, הָהָ֫רָה 12:8 12t.; construct הַר 10:30 +; suffix הַרֲרִי Psa 30:8, הֲרָרִי Jer 17:3; הַרְכֶם Psa 11:1; הַרֲרָם Gen 14:6; plural הָרִים Deut 11:11 +; with article הֶהָרִים Gen 7:19 +; construct הָרֵי 8:4 27t.; הַרֲרֵי Deut 33:15 8t. (all poetry); suffix הָרַי Isa 14:25; 49:11; Zech 14:5 + Ezek 38:21 (B Co חרדה), הָרָ֑י Isa 65:9; הָרָיו Ezek 35:8; Mal 1:3; הֲרָרֶיהָ Deut 8:9; —

1 mountain, hill (these often not sharply distinguished, but):

a. specifically mountain, high elevation, often in all the literature; — e. g. סִינַי הַר Mount Sinai (properly, the Mount of Sinai; so always) Exod 19:11, 18, 20, 23; 24:16; 31:18; 34:2, 4, 29, 32; Lev 7:38; 25:1; 26:46; 27:34; Num 3:1; 28:6; Neh 9:13 (see סִינַי); also הָהָר = id., Exod 19:2 10t. Exodus 19 (19:18 read הָעָם, so Manuscripts ᵐ5 Di) 20:18; 24:4 6t. Exodus 24; 25:40; 26:30; 27:8; 32:1, 15, 19; 34:2-3,(twice in verse); 34:29; = חוֺרֵב הֵר 33:6 (see חֹרֵב), also חֹרֵ֑בָה הָאֱלֹהִים אֶלהַֿר 3:1, חֹרֵב הָאֱלֹהִים הַר עַד 1Kin 19:8; further הָהָר = id., Deut 4:11 (twice in verse); 5:4-5, 19, 20; 9:9-10t. Deuteronomy 9.. 10, 1Kin 19:11; = הָאֱלֹהִים הַר Exod 4:27; 18:5; 24:13 (all J E) = יחוה הַר Num 10:33 (compare Di); see further הָהָר הֹר Hor the mount (so always) 20:22 10t. Numbers; Deut 32:50; = חָחָר Num 20:28 (twice in verse); נְבוֺ הַר Deut 32:49; 34:1 = הָהָר 32:50; = הַזֶּה הָעֲבָרִים הַר Num 27:12; Deut 32:49, compare (נְבוֺ לִפְנֵי) הָעֲבָרִים הָרֵי Num 33:47-48, גְּרִזִּים הַר Mt. Tabor, Judg 4:6, 12, 14, compare Jer 46:18 בֶּהָרִים תָּבוֺר (opposed to בַּיָּם כַּרְמֶל); הַגִּלְבֹּעַ הַר 1Sam 31:1, 8; 2Sam 1:6, also (poetry) בַּגּ ׳הָרֵי 1:21 (compare Dr); הַרחֶֿרֶס Judg 1:35 (Stu חֶרֶס עָר) compare חֶרֶס; הַזֵּיתִים הַר Zech 14:4 (twice in verse) Mount of Olives, (opposed to גֵּיא) compare לָעִיר מִקֶּדֶם אֲשֶׁר הָהָר Ezek 11:23; also יְרוּשָׁלַםִ עַלמְּֿנֵי אֲשֶׁר בָּהָר 1Kin 11:7 (place of Solomon's idolatry) = הַרהַֿמַּשְׁחִית 2Kin 23:13 mountain of the destroyer (same combination in another, figurative sense Jer 51:25), others derive ׳הַמ here from משׁח anoint, and render as = Mt. of Olives (cited as המשׁחה הר Talmud, Shabb. 56 b), compare Hoffm^ZAW 1882, 175; הֶהָרִים אַחַד in land of Moriah Gen 22:2; mountains about Jerusalem in General, לָהּ סָבִיב הָרִים יְרוּשָׁלַםִ Psa 125:2; compare also 121:1 (|| יהוה); הָהָר of citadel of Jerusalem Isa 22:5; of temple hill הַבַּיִת הַר Micah 3:12 = Jer 26:18; בֵּיתיֿ ׳הַר Isa 2:2 = Micah 4:1; 2Chr 33:15; also בַּשָּׂדֶה הֲרָרִי Jer 17:3; further צִיּוֺן הַר Mt. Note particular הַרמֿוֺעֵד Isa 14:13 mountain of meeting or of assembly, i.e. the dwelling-place of the gods, according to Babylonian conception, here represented as in the far north, see especially Che De Di, Dl^Pa 117 ff. COT^on the passage Jr^57ff. Jen^Kosmologie 23. Upon the mountain is עָלהָֿהָר compare עַלהֶֿהָרִים עַלבְּֿתוּלָיהָ וַתֵּבְךְּ Judg 11:38, & the peculiar phrase הֶהָרִים עַלֿ וְיָרַדְתִּי וְאֵלְכָה 11:37; go up upon אָלֿ עָלָה Exod 19:23; 24:13; 34:4 (all J E); compare accusative in הָהָר הֹר אֹתָם הַעַל Num 20:25; speak with one upon is בהר דבר Exod 31:18; 34:32; ⟦N136⟧ Num 3:1 (all P), Lev 25:1 (H), compare command, give commands, law, etc., בהר 7:38; 26:46; 27:34 (all P or H); other phrases with בְּ upon (literally in, i.e. in the midst of a group of mountains) Exod 34:3 בְּכָל (JE) 25:40; 26:30; 27:8; Num 28:6 (all P), Isa 13:4; but בְּחֹרֵב Psa 106:19 = at Horeb, הָהָר בְּהֹר Num 33:37 at Mt. Hor; go down from the mountain is מִןהָֿהָר יָרַד Exod 32:1, 15 (both J E) 34:29 (twice in verse) (both P), compare Judg 9:36 etc.

b. mountain-range הַרבָּֿשָׁן Psa 68:16 (twice in verse) = הַראֱֿלֹהִים 68:16; = הַרגַּֿבְנֻנִּים 68:16, גַּבְנֻנִּים הָרִים 68:17 (of the Jebel Hauran: see בשׁן).

c. mountain, indefinite, Job 14:18 (|| צוּר); usually plural mountains, in General, or the mountains, especially in poetry & the higher style; often figurative; הֶהָרִים הָרִים,, covered by flood Gen 7:20 compare 7:19; 8:5; covered by waters & freed therefrom by word of God (at creation) Psa 104:6; a chief work of God (in creation) Amos 4:13; Psa 65:7; 90:2; Prov 8:25 (|| גְּבָעוֺת); weighed by God Isa 40:12; removed and overturned in anger of God Job 9:5 compare Psa 46:3; 46:4; devastated by God Isa 42:15 (|| גְּבָעוֺת) compare Jer 9:9; smoking at God's touch Psa 104:32; 144:5; melting at presence of ׳י Judg 5:5; Micah 1:4; Psa 97:5; Isa 63:19; 64:2; trembling 5:25 compare Jer 4:24 (|| גְּבָעוֺת), Nahum 1:5 (|| id.), Hab 3:10; called to witness Yahweh's dealings with his people Micah 6:2 (|| אָ֑רֶץ מֹסְדֵי הָאֵתָנִים) compare 6:1 (|| הַגְּבָעוֺת), entreated to cover the guilty Hosea 10:8 (|| גְּבָעוֺת); addressed by ׳י also Ezek 6:3 (|| גְּבָעוֺת, opposed to גֵּאָוֺת אֲפִיקִים,); specifically, mountains of Israel summoned to hear ׳י and addressed by him 36:1 (twice in verse); 36:4, 8; 37:22; 38:8; 39:2, 4; summoned to praise ׳י Psa 148:9 (|| גְּבָעוֺת) compare Isa 44:23; 49:13; 55:12 (|| גְּבָעוֺת); leaping in praise of ׳י Psa 114:4; 114:6 (both || גְּבָעוֺת); הַרֲרֵיקֶֿדֶם Deut 33:15 (|| עוֺלָם גִּבְעוֺת); הַרֲרֵיעַֿד Hab 3:6 (|| id.); הַרֲרֵיעַֿד also Psa 76:5 (according to ᵐ5 Bi Che; ᵑ0 has טֶרֶף for עַד), and probably Gen 49:26 for עַד הוֺרַי ᵑ0 (|| עוֺלָם גִּבְעֹת) compare Di.

d. high mountain גָּבֹהַּ הַר Isa 30:25 (|| נִשָּׂאָה גִּבְעָה) compare 40:9; 52:7; מְאֹד גָּכֹהַּ הַר Ezek 40:2; וְתָלוּל גָּבֹהַּ הַר 17:22; plural הַגְּבֹהִים הֶהָרִים Gen 7:19; Psa 104:18 (|| סְלָעִים), ׳הֶה הָרָמִים Isa 2:14 as symbol of strength and pride (|| הַנִּשָּׂאוֺת הַגְּבָעוֺת), compare הֶהָרִים Ezek 38:20 (|| חוֺמָה מַדְרֵגוֺת,).

e. opposite valley or plain Josh 12:8 (D; opposed to שְׁפֵלָה עֲרָבָה, אֲשֵׁדוֺת, מִדְּבָּר,), compare ⟦N189⟧ Deut 1:7, also Jer 17:26 (opposed to שְׁפֵלָה); Gen 19:17 (J; opposed to כִּכָּר); often opposed to גַּיְא 2Kin 2:16; Ezek 31:12 (figurative) 32:5 (id.) 35:8 (|| גִּבְעָה), 36:4 (|| id.), Isa 40:4 (|| id.), opposed to בִּקְעָה Deut 8:7; 11:11; Psa 104:8; opposed to מִדְבָּר Lam 4:19 (compare Josh 12:8 above); הָרִים בֵּין Psa 104:10 is || בַּנְּחָלִים; see also י הָרִים ׳אֱלֹהֵי עֲמָקִים וְלֹאאֱֿלֹהֵי 1Kin 20:28, compare 20:23 (opposed to מִישׁוֺר); note also הָעֵמֶק הַר Josh 13:19 (P), & גֵּיהָֿרִים גֵּיאהָֿרַי, Zech 14:5 (twice in verse); further הָרִים מִדְבַּר Psa 75:7 mountainous desert Vrss. & most modern (Baer מִדְבָּר but compare De); mountain-ward is הֶ֫רָה Gen 14:10.

f. mountains as hiding-places: — בֶּהָרִים אֲוֶר הַמִּנְהָרוֺת Judg 6:2 the burrows (Stanley, VB) which are in the mountains; compare Psa 11:1; Jer 16:16 (|| גִּבְעָה); הָרִים מִשֹּׁרֶשׁ הָפַךְ Job 28:9 (of mining), see also Deut 8:9.

g. mountains as running-place of gazelles 1Chr 12:8; of leopards נְמֵרִים הַרֲרֵי Song 4:8; hunting-ground for partridges 1Sam 26:20; הָרִים עוֺף Psa 50:11; compare 11:1 (figurative); wandering-place of lost sheep (figurative) Nahum 3:18; 1Kin 22:17; Jer 50:6 compare 50:6 (|| גִּבְעָה), Ezek 34:6 (|| רָמָה כָּלגִּֿבְעָה) 2Chr 18:16.

h. grazing-places for cattle בְּהַרֲרֵיאָֿ֑לֶף בְּהֵמוֺתPsa 50:10 (read אֵל for אלף Ol Bi Che), compare הָרִים יְתוּר Job 39:8 of pasture of wild ass; also (si vera lectio) הָרִים בּוּל ⟦N034⟧ 40:20, i.e. mountains as furnishing food for hippopotamus; see further Song 4:6; 8:14; Prov 27:25.

i. as place of field and vineyards2Chr 26:10 (opposed to מִישׁוֺר שְׁפֵלָה,) Isa 7:25; see also חָצִיר הָרִים הַמַּצְמִיחַ Psa 147:8 compare Hag 1:11; figurative Psa 72:3 (|| גְּבָעוֺת); in promise חָלָב תֵּלַכְנָה וְהַגְּבָעוֺת עָסִיס הֶהָרִים יִטְּפוּ Joel 4:18 compare Amos 9:13;

=== H2091 ===
H2091. zahab

זָהָב noun masculine^Gen 2:12 gold (Late Hebrew id.; Arabic Sabean דֿהב e.g. Os^29 DHM^ZMG 1883, 338; Aramaic דְּהַב, , Palmyrene דהבא Vog^No. 23) — ׳ז absolute 2:11 364t.; construct 2:12 6t.; suffix זְהָבִי 1Kin 20:7 2t.; זְהָֽבְךָ 20:3, 5; זְהָבֶ֑ךָ Isa 30:22; זְהָבוֺ 2:20; 31:7; זְהָבָם Hosea 8:4 4t.; — gold,

1 = gold-ore, gold in a raw state, Gen 2:11-12, Jer 10:9; Prov 17:3; 27:21; Job 28:1 +; perhaps also 1Kin 9:28; 10:11; 22:49 etc.; compare ז ׳עַפְרֹת Job 28:6 gold-dust; see also Zech 13:9 (simile of tried people ׳י) and compare Mal 3:3; likewise Job 23:10 (simile of Job's purity, as shewn by divine tests); זָהָב מִצָּפוֺן 37:22 is dubious **read perhaps זֹהַר Du and Dr; Ew De and others out of the North comes gold; others, however, golden light, brightness, of sun after clouds dispersed by north wind, ᵐ5 Rabb Da and others; or golden splendour of God coming from north Bö^Ä 76 Hi and others; Siegf conjecture יהוה for זהב

2 gold as wealth Gen 13:2 (J; + כֶּסֶף מִקְנֶה,) 24:34 (J; + חֲמֹרִים גְּמַלִּים, שְׁפָחֹת, עֲבָדִם, כֶּסֶף, בָּקָר, צאֹן,) compare Hosea 2:8; Isa 2:7; Deut 8:13; 17:17; Job 3:15; 31:24; Zech 14:14; Ezra 1:4, 6 +; in General, as precious: טוֺב חֵן וּמִזָּהָב מִכֶּסֶף רָ֑ב מֵעשֶׁר שֵׁם נִבְחָר Prov 22:1 to be chosen is a name more than great riches, and good favour more than silver and than gold; Job 28:17 (of wisdom) gold and glass do not equal it; Psa 19:10 (ordinances of ׳י) which are more to be desired than gold; compare 119:72; 119:127; also metaphor of princes and chief men of Zion Lam 4:1 (compare 4:2).

3 gold as spoil of war Josh 6:19 (+ בַּרְזֶל כְּלֵינְֿחשֶׁת, כֶּסֶף,) so 6:24, compare 7:21, 24; 22:8; 2Kin 7:8 +.

4 gold as merchandise Ezek 27:22 (+ כָּלבּֿשֶֹׁם ראֹשׁ יְקָרָה, אֶבֶן), compare also gold from Ophir 1Kin 9:28 etc. (see below 7 below)

5 gold as costly gift: to a prophet וְז כֶּסֶף בֵּיתוֺ ׳מְלֹא (hypothet.), Num 22:18; 24:13 (both E); brought by queen of Sheba to Solomon 1Kin 10:2, 10 (both + יְקָרָה אֶבֶן and בְּשָׂמִים) = 2Chr 9:1, 9; as tribute 2Kin 23:35 (twice in verse) (compare 23:33; all + כֶּסֶף); as satisfaction for murder 2Sam 21:4 (+ כֶּסֶף).

6 gold as material: of (nose- or ear-) ring, נֶזֶם Gen 24:22; Exod 32:2-3, Judg 8:24-25, Prov 11:22; 25:12; Job 42:11; of chain Gen 41:42, shields 2Sam 8:7, images of emerods 1Sam 6:4, 17 and mice 6:4; 6:11; 6:18; ז ׳תַּמּוּחֵי כֶּסֶף בְּמַשְׂכִּיּוֺת Prov 25:11; ז ׳גֻּלַּת Exod 12:6; ׳כְּלֵיזֿ 3:22; 11:2; 12:35; 1Sam 6:8; Psa 105:37 etc.; זָהָב וּכְלֵי כְּלֵיכֶֿסֶף Gen 24:53 as presents to a bride (+ בְּגָדִים); so of presents to secure king's favour 2Sam 8:10 (+ נְחשֶׁת כְּלֵי), and in list of yearly gifts or tribute to Solomon 1Kin 10:25 2Chr 9:24; as material of idols ז וֵאלֹהֵי ׳כֶסֶף אֱלֹהֵי Exod 20:23; compare 32:31; Hosea 8:4; Isa 2:20; 30:22; 31:7; Deut 29:16; Psa 115:4; 135:15; as plating of idols Deut 7:25; especially as material of the furnishings of Solomon's temple, or (oftener) the platings of temple, or parts thereof, or of its furnishings 1Kin 6:20-21, (3 t. in verse); 6:22 (twice in verse); 6:28; 7:48 10t. 6:7; also 2Chr 3:5 (twice in verse); 3:6 14t. 3:4; cf 1Chr 28:14 (twice in verse) + 14 t. 1 Chronicles 28:29 (David's preparation for temple); see also 2Kin 24:13; compare 14:14 2Chr 25:24; 2Kin 25:15 = Jer 52:19 (in accounts of plundering temple etc.); but more often still of furnishing and utensils of tabernacle, insignia of high-priest etc., Exod 25:3, 11, 24 97t. Exodus 25-40, Lev 8:9; Num 4:11; 8:4; (in case of large objects frequently of plating or sheathing, compare 12c below) Note the expressions ז ׳תְּנוּפַת ליהוה Exod 35:22 a wave-offering of gold, הַתְּנוּפָה זְהַב 38:24; also הַתְּרוּמָה זְהַב Num 31:52 (all P) compare Ezra 8:25, and (of offering to heathen god) בְּזָהָב יְכַבֵּד Dan 11:38.

7 gold described by its source, especially gold from Ophir (compare Glass: Skizze ii. 357 ff. 368) 1Kin 9:28; 10:11; 22:49; 1Chr 29:4; 2Chr 8:18; 9:10; so perh Jer 10:9 (read אוֺפִיר for אוּפַז Ew Klo and others); שְׁבָא זְהַב Psa 72:15; on ׳ז מַּרְוָ֑יִם2Chr 3:6 see פרוים; compare further Gen 2:11-12,.

8 gold defined by var. adjective and pts.: ׳ז שָׁחוּט beaten gold (see שָׁחַט) 1Kin 10:16-17, 2Chr 9:15 (twice in verse); 9:16; ׳ז סָגוּר 1Kin 6:20-21, also 7:49-50, 10:21 2Chr 4:20, 22; 9:21 (on meaning see סָגַר); especially (but only P & Chronicles) ׳ז טָהוֺר pure gold Exod 25:11, 17, 24 21t. Exodus 25.. 28. 30. 37. 39 (of equipments of tabernacle and high priest's insignia), 1Chr 28:17; 2Chr 3:4; 9:17; compare ׳ז טוֺב3:5, 8; on ׳ז מוּפָז 1Kin 10:18, AV best gold, compare מָּזַז אוּפָז,.

9 ׳ז as measure of weight and value:

a. = gold-shekel, ז ׳עֲשָׂרָה מִשְׁקָלָם Gen 24:22; ז עֲשָׂרָה אַחַת ׳כַּף Num 7:14 11t. Numbers 7; ז ׳וּשְׁבַעמֵֿאוֺת אֶלֶף Judg 8:26, compare 1Kin 10:16; ⟦N111⟧ 2Kin 5:5; 2Chr 9:15-16, ז ׳אַלְפֵי וָכָ֑סֶף Psa 119:72; compare further Josh 7:21; but also (only Chronicles) שֶׁקֶל expressed: מֵאוֺת שֵׁשׁ ז ׳שִׁקְלֵי מִשְׁקַל 1Chr 21:25 (50 shekels of silver in || 2Sam 24:24); ז חֲמִשִּׁים לִשְׁקָלִים לְמִסְמְרוֺת ׳וּמִשְׁקָל2Chr 3:9.

b. ז מָנִים ׳שְׁלשֶׁת 1Kin 10:17 three minas of gold (see מָנֶה).

=== H2142 ===
H2142. zakar

זָכַר verb remember (Assyrian zikâru, name, mention, compare noun zikru, Hpt in KAT^2Glossary i Lyon^Sargontexte; Zinjirli זכר, Arabic , Aramaic דְּכַר, , Palmyrene דרכנא monument, Vog^No. 36 b, דכיר memorial, Vog^No. 62, Sabean in proper name ידֿכראל DHM^Epigr. Denkm. 43, but Ethiopic ) —

Qal Perfect3masculine singular ׳ז Gen 40:23 +; 3 feminine singular זָָֽכְרָה Lam 1:7, 9; 2masculine singular וְזָכַרְתָּ֫ Deut 5:15 +; suffix זְכַרְתַּנִי Gen 40:14 compare 1Sam 1:11, זְכַרְתָּם Psa 88:6; 2feminine singular זָכַרְתְּ Isa 47:7; 57:11; compare 17:10 + Ezek 16:22, 43 Qr (both Kt זכרתי), וְזָכַרְתְּ consecutive 16:61; 3plural זָָֽכְרוּ Judg 8:34 +, etc.; Imperfect3masculine singular יִזְכֹּר Hosea 8:13 +; יִזְכָּרֿ 2Sam 14:11; Prov 31:7; וַיִּזְכֹּר Gen 8:1 +; suffix וַיִּוְכְּרֶהָ 1Sam 1:19; 2feminine singular תִּזְכְּרִי Isa 54:4 2t.; 1 singular אֶזְכֹּר Lev 26:42 (twice in verse); Isa 43:25 (Psa 77:12 Qr see infra), אֶזְכָּרֿ Jer 31:34, וָאֶזְכֹּר Exod 6:5; suffix 2masculine singular אֶזְכָּרְךָ Psa 42:7, suffix 2feminine singular אֶזְכְּרֵכִי 137:6 (see Ges^§ 58, 3, R, 1 Sta^§§ 356, a, 2, 634 a, 2), etc.; Imperative masculine singular זְכֹר Exod 32:13 +; זְכָרֿ Psa 25:7 +; זָכְרָה Neh 6:14 +; suffix זָכְרֵנִי Judg 16:28 2t.; masculine plural זִכְרוּ Isa 46:9 +, זְכֹ֑רוּ Neh 4:8; Infinitive absolute זָכוֺר Exod 13:3 +; זָכֹר Jer 31:30 +; construct לִזְכֹּר Gen 9:16; Ezek 23:19, etc.; suffix בְּזָכְרֵנִי Psa 137:1; Participle active plural construct וּלְֹזכְרֵי 103:18; זָכוּר 103:14 see below —

I. human subj.

1 remember, recall, call to mind, usually as affecting present feeling, thought, or action:

a. remember past experiences (accusative) Gen 42:9 (E), 2Kin 9:25 (accusative of person agent); followed by clause with מִי Job 4:7; things formerly known (accusative) Jer 17:2; followed by clause with כִּי Judg 9:2; with implied regret, longing Num 11:5 (JE), Psa 42:5; 137:1; Lam 1:7 compare Psa 77:7, negative ⟦N072⟧ 137:6 (|| שׁכח 137:5); so (followed by בְּ) Jer 3:16 (|| לב על עלה and פקד).

b. recall past distress, etc.; object not expressed, Lam 3:20 (זכר זכור), Job 11:16 (שׁכח in || clause); negative, followed by accusative Isa 54:4 (|| שׁכח), Prov 31:7 (|| id.).

=== H2263 ===
H2263. chabaq

[חָבַק] verb clasp, embrace (Late Hebrew id.; חַבֵּק ᵑ7; Mandean , NSyriac ; Arabic II. collect one's possessions, etc., Frey) —

Qal Infinitive לַחֲבוֺק Eccl 3:5; Participle חֹבֵּק 4:5; חֹבֶקֶת 2Kin 4:16; — embrace, with accusative בֵּן 4:16; יָדָיו Eccl 4:5 (said of הַכְּסִיל = fold his arms, i.e. be idle; compare חִבֻּק); no object expressed 3:5 (|| Pi`el).

Pi`el Perfect חִבְּקוּ Job 24:8; ⟦N048⟧ Lam 4:5; Imperfect וַיְחַבֵּק Gen 48:10; וַיְחַבֶּקֿ 29:13; וַיְחַבְּקֵהוּ 33:4; 3feminine singular suffix תְּחַבְּקֵ֫נִי Song 2:6; 8:3; 2masculine singular וּתְחַבֵּק Prov 5:20, suffix תְּחַבְּקֶ֫נָּה 4:8; Infinitive חַבֵּק Eccl 3:5; — embrace of person, no object מֵחַבֵּק לִרְחֹק עת 3:5 (|| Qal); followed by לְ Gen 29:13 (J), 48:10 (E); followed by suffix 33:4 (J), Prov 4:8; Song 2:6; 8:3; with accusative Prov 5:20; embrace צוּר for want of shelter Job 24:8 (of poor); embrace refuse-heaps אשׁפתות Lam 4:5, i.e. are outcasts.

=== H2416 ===
H2416. chay

I. חַי adjective alive, living; — ׳ח Gen 25:6 +; חָ֑י 3:20 +, חֵי Amos 8:14 15t. (Ew^§ 329 explains as construct, but Ges^§ 93, R 7 n and others as contracted absolute); feminine חַיָּה Gen 1:20 +; plural חַיִּים Psa 116:9 +; —

1. a. of God, as the living one, the fountain of life חַי אֵל Josh 3:10 (J), Hosea 2:1; Psa 42:3; 84:3; חי אלהים 2Kin 19:4, 16 = Isa 37:4, 17; חיים אלהים Deut 5:23; 1Sam 17:26, 36; Jer 10:10; 23:36; יהוה חי Yahweh is living Psa 18:47 = 2Sam 22:47; חי גאלי my avenger is living Job 19:25; the formula of the oath is י ׳חַי Judg 8:19; Ruth 3:13; 1Sam 14:39, 45; 19:6; 20:21; 25:34; 26:10, 16; 28:10; 29:6; 2Sam 4:9; 12:5; 14:11; 15:21; 1Kin 1:29; 2:24; 17:1, 12; 18:10; 22:14 2Chr 18:13; 2Kin 5:16, 20; Hosea 4:15; Jer 4:2; 5:2; 12:16; 16:14-15, 23:7-8, 38:16; אל חי Job 27:2; ׳ח האלהים 2Sam 2:27; י אדני ׳חי Jer 44:26; י ׳תי צבאות 1Kin 18:15; 2Kin 3:14; as used by God Himself it is אנכי חי Deut 32:40, elsewhere אני חי Num 14:21, 28 (P) Isa 49:18; Jer 22:24; 46:18; Ezek 5:11; 14:16, 18, 20; 16:48; 17:16, 19; 18:3; 20:3, 31, 33; 33:11, 27; 34:8; 35:6, 11; Zeph 2:9, compare also דן אלהיך חֵי שׁבע, באר דרך חֵי Amos 8:14; with the exception of הָעֹולָם בְּחֵי by him who liveth for ever Dan 12:7, חֵי is always (as an artificial distinction of scribes) used of non-sacred oaths, see

b. of man: חַי אָדָם a living man Lam 3:39, in antith ׃מֵת חי חי Isa 38:19; הַחַי collective Eccl 7:2; Absalom 2Sam 19:7; Naboth 1Kin 21:15; a son or lad 2Sam 12:21; 1Kin 3:22 (twice in verse); 3:23 (twice in verse); 3:25-26, (twice in verse); 3:27; 17:23; usually plural חיים alive, living Num 16:30, 33 (J), 17:13 (P), Deut 4:4; 5:3; Isa 8:19; Psa 55:16; 124:3; Prov 1:12; Eccl 4:2 (twice in verse); 4:15; 6:8; 9:4-5, Ruth 2:20; taking prisoners alive Josh 8:23 (J) 1Sam 15:8; 1Kin 20:18 (twice in verse); 2Kin 7:12; 10:14 (twice in verse); 2Chr 25:12; living (prosperously) 1Sam 25:6 (We reads לָחִי = לְאָחִי (see Klo Dr) to my brother, after ᵑ9 fratribus meis); elsewhere in phrase (פני) על חיים (אַתֶּם) הֵם אשׁר כלהֿימים האדמה all the days that they (ye) live upon (the face of) of the land Deut 4:10; 12:1; 31:13; 1Kin 8:40 2Chr 6:31. Note phrases: (יםׅ חַי עוֺד yet alive Gen 25:6; 43:7, 27, 28; 45:28; 46:30 (J), 45:3, 26; Exod 4:18 (E), Deut 31:27; 1Sam 20:14; 2Sam 12:22; 18:14; 1Kin 20:32; חי בהיות 2Sam 12:18; 1Kin 12:6; 2Chr 10:6; (ה)חַיִים אֶרֶץ land of the living Isa 38:11; 53:8; Jer 11:19; Ezek 26:20; 32:23-24, 25, 26, 27, 32; Psa 27:13; 52:7; 142:6; Job 28:13; הח ׳ארצות Psa 116:9; ח ׳ספר 69:29; הח ׳צרור bundle of the living 1Sam 25:29; הח ׳אור light of the living Job 33:30; Psalm 56:14. In the oath by life of men חי is pointed always המלך ׃חֵי אדני חֵי as my lord the king liveth 2Sam 15:21; פרעה חֵי Gen 42:15-16, (E); נַפְשְׁךָ חֵי 1Sam 1:26; 17:55; 2Sam 14:19; ׳י נַפְשְׁךָ וְחֵי ׳חַ as Yahweh liveth and as thy soul (or thyself) liveth 1Sam 20:3; 25:26; 2Kin 2:2, 4, 6; 4:30 נ ׳וְחֵי חַיֶּךָ 2Sam 11:11 (but on text see We Dr).

c. of animals, alive, living: ox Exod 21:35; 22:3 (E); goat Lev 16:10, 20, 21 (P); bird 14:4, 6 (twice in verse); 14:7, 51, 53; dog, הַמֵּת מִןהָֿאַרְיֵה טוֺב הוּא חַי לְכֶלֶב Eccl 9:4 (compare Arabic proverb Kelb µei wa-lâ meijit, a living dog and no dead lion Wetzst^Verhand. Berl. Anthrop. Ges. 1878, 388); reptiles Gen 1:28 (P); animals in General 9:3 (P); חי בשׂר living, raw flesh Lev 13:10, 14, 15 (twice in verse); 13:16 (P) 1Sam 2:15.

d. animals and man, phrases for either or both: ח ׳כל Gen 3:20; 8:21 (J) Job 12:10; 28:21; 30:23; Psa 143:2; 145:16; החי כל Gen 6:19 (P); חַיָּה נֶפֶשׁ 1:20, 24, 30 (P) 2:7, 19 (J); חַיָּה נֶפֶשׁ כל 9:12, 15, 16 (P) Ezek 47:9; החיה נפשׁ כל Gen 1:21; 9:10; Lev 11:10, 46 (P).

e. (dubious) of vegetation, as thorns, green, Psa 58:10 (Ges Ew Ol Pe, but De Ri Che Bae raw flesh, see

c. above near the end) feminine of water, flowing, fresh חיים מים Gen 26:19 (J), Lev 14:5-6, 50, 51, 52; 15:13; Num 19:17 (P), Jer 2:13; 17:13; Zech 14:8; Song 4:15.

2 (dubious) lively, active: חַי אישׁ an active man 2Sam 23:20 (but Qr חַיִל אישׁ is to be preferred); חיים איבי mine enemies are lively Psa 38:20 (RV, but Houb Ol Ew Hu Che and others read חִנָּם, || שׁקר).

3 reviving: חַיָּה כָּעֵת at the time (when it is) reviving, the spring Gen 18:10, 14 (J), 2Kin 4:16-17,.

II. [חַי] noun [masculine] kinsfolk (Arabic a group of families united by vital ties RS^K 36-40 Dr^ Sm 119), plural suffix חַיַּי 1Sam 18:18, incorrectly pointed with the interpretation my life RV, but read חַיִּי my kins-flok, We Ki Dr RS SS RVm VB. It is explained by the gloss אבי משׁפחת We (Klo reads וּ אַחַי).

I. חַיָּה noun feminine living thing, animal (Zinjirli wild beast DHM^Sendsch. 34) — ׳ח Gen 8:17 +; construct חַיַּת Isa 57:10 +; old case ending (poetic) חַיְתוֺ Gen 1:24; Psa 50:10; 79:2; 104:11; 104:20; Isa 56:9 (twice in verse); Zeph 2:14; suffix חַיָּתִי Psa 143:3, etc.; plural חַיּוֺת Lev 14:4 12t. —

1 animal, as living, active being:

a. in General, Gen 8:17; Lev 11:2, 27, 47 (twice in verse); Num 35:3 (P) Psa 104:25; Isa 46:1; Dan 8:4.

b. wild animals, on account of their vital energy and activity Gen 7:14, 21; 8:1, 19; 9:5 (P) Lev 17:13; 25:7 (H) Job 37:8; Psa 148:10; Isa 40:16; Ezek 14:15; 33:27; Zeph 2:14-15, קנה ׳ח Psa 68:31 wild animal of the reeds; ׳ח רעה evil beast Gen 37:20, 33 (JE) Lev 26:6 (H) Ezek 5:17; 14:15, 21; 34:25; ׳ח טמאה Lev 5:2 (P) unclean beast; ׳ח הארץ Gen 1:25, 30; 9:2, 10 (twice in verse) (P) 1Sam 17:46; Ezek 29:5; 32:4; 34:28; Job 5:22; ארץ חיתו Gen 1:24 (P) Psa 79:2; ׳ח השׂדה Exod 23:11, 29 (covt. code.) Lev 26:22 (H) Deut 7:22; 2Sam 21:10; 2Kin 14:9 2Chr 25:18; Hosea 2:14; 2:20; 4:3; 13:8; Isa 43:20; Jer 27:6; 28:14; Ezek 38:20; 39:4; Job 5:23; 39:15; ח ׳כל השׂדה Gen 2:19-20, 3:1, 14 (J) Job 40:20; Jer 12:9; Ezek 31:6, 13; 34:5, 8; 39:17; שׂדי חיתו Isa 56:9; Psa 104:11; (ב)יער חיתו Isa 56:9; Psa 50:10; 104:20; חיות מְּרִיץ Isa 35:9 destroyer among beasts.

c. living beings, of the cherubic chariot Ezek 1:5, 13 (twice in verse); 1:14-15, (twice in verse); 1:19 (twice in verse); 1:20-21, 22; 3:13; 10:15, 17, 20.

2 life, only in late poetry, Psa 143:3; Job 33:18, 20, 22, 28; 36:14; Ezek 7:13 (twice in verse), and (dubious) Psa 74:19; 78:50.

3 appetite, activity of hunger: כפרים חית appetite of young lions Job 38:39.

4 revival, renewal: מָצָאתְ יָדֵךְ חַיַּת thou didst find renewal of thy strength (re-invigoration) Isa 57:10; see חָיָה 2.

II. חַיָּה noun feminine (si vera lectio) community ( = feminine of II. חַי according to Nö^ ZMG, 1886, 176) — חֹנָה מְּלִשְׁתִּים וְחַיַּת and a community of Philistines (i.e. a group of allied families, making a raid together) was encamping 2Sam 23:13 ( = מַחֲנֵה 1Chr 11:15); perhaps also Psa 68:11 (compare below)

Note. — Several other passages are dubious: לַחַיָּה פְלִשְׁתִּים וַיֵּאָסְפוּ 2Sam 23:11 assembled into a troop, or by bands (RV, but neither rendering justified: Bö Ew We Klo Dr rightly read לֶ֫חְיָה to Leµi, compare Judg 15:9 word omitted by accident in || 1Chr 11:13 Dr); יָָֽשְׁבוּבָֿהּ חַיָּתְךָ Psa 68:11 thy troop dwelt in it (Thes SS RVm Hi Ri Che, but ᵑ6 ᵐ5 Jerome Pe thy (living) creatures, of the people; Hup id., reference to quails; improbable); לָנֶ֫צַח אַלתִּֿשְׁכַּח עֲנִיֶּיךָ חַיַּת תּוֺרֶךָ נֶפֶשׁ לְחַיַּת אַלתִּֿתֵּן 74:19 give not the soul of thy turtle-dove to the wild beasts, the life of thine afflicted do not forget for ever: RV Hi Pe Bae Sch render thus, giving חית different meanings in the two clauses, the former being archaic feminine absolute Ges^§ 80, 2. R. 2; text doubtless corrupt, read possibly למות Schr Ri, for לחית, or לשׁחת Kroch; Gr either of these; Che either, or better לחרב.

חַיִּים noun masculine^Deut 28:66

plural abstract emphatic life, ׳ח Gen 2:7 +; חַיִּין Job 24:22 (Aramaism Ges^§ 87 (1) a); חַיֵּי Gen 23:1 +; suffix חַיַּי 47:9 +; חַיָּ֑יְכִי Psa 103:4; (Ges^§ 91 (2) R. 2), etc.; —

1 life: physical Gen 27:46; Exod 1:14 (P), Deut 28:66 (twice in verse); 2Sam 11:11; 15:21; Isa 38:12; Jeremiah 8:23; Lam 3:53, 58; Ezek 7:13; Job 3:20; 7:7; 9:21; 10:12; 24:22; Psa 7:6; 17:14; 21:5; 26:9; 31:11; 34:13; 63:4; 64:2; 66:9; 88:4; 103:4; Prov 18:21; Eccl 2:17; 6:12; 7:13; 9:9 (twice in verse); 10:19; Jonah 2:7; 4:3, 8; שׂרה חַיֵּי life of Sarah Gen 23:1(P) ח ׳שְׁנֵי years of the life of 2Sam 19:35; Gen 23:1; 25:7, 17; 47:8-9, (twice in verse); 47:28; Exod 6:16, 18, 20 (P); לח ׳שָׁנָה Gen 7:11 (P); חַיִּים שְׁנוֺת Prov 3:2; 4:10; 9:11; ח ׳יְמֵי days of the life of Eccl 2:3; 5:17; 5:19; 6:12; 8:15; 9:9; ח יְמֵי ׳כֹּל Gen 3:14, 17 (J) Deut 4:9; 6:2; 16:3; 17:19; Josh 1:5; 4:14 (D) 1Sam 1:11; 7:15; 1Kin 5:1; 11:34; 15:5-6, 2Kin 25:29-30, = Jer 52:33-34, Isa 38:20 (poem of Hez.) Psa 23:6; 27:4; 128:5; Prov 31:12; ׳בּח in or during one's life Gen 27:46 (P) Lev 18:18 (H) Judg 16:30; 2Sam 1:23; 18:18; Psa 49:19; 63:5; 104:33; 146:2; Job 10:1; Eccl 3:12; 9:3; ח ׳רוּחַ Gen 6:17; 7:15 (P); ׳ח רוּחִי Isa 38:16 (poem of Hez.); ׳ח בשׂרים Prov 14:30; ח (רוּחַ) ׳נִשְׁמַת Gen 2:7; 7:22 (J).

2 life: as welfare and happiness in king's presence Prov 16:15; as consisting of earthly felicity combined (often) with spiritual blessedness Deut 30:6, 15, 19 (twice in verse); 30:20; 32:47; Psa 30:6; 133:3; Prov 3:22; 4:13, 22; 8:35; 10:16; 11:19; 12:28; 19:23; 21:21; 22:4; Mal 2:5; used only once distinctly of eternal life (late) ׳ח עולם Dan 12:2; חַיָּ֑י אֵל Psa 42:9 God of my life; ח ׳מעוז 27:1; ח ׳מקור 36:10; Prov 10:11; 13:14; 14:27; 16:22; (ה)חַיִּים עֵץ Gen 2:9; 3:22, 24 (J) Prov 3:18; 11:30; 13:12; 15:4; ח ׳ארח Psa 16:11; ⟦N080⟧ Prov 5:6; 15:24; לח ׳ארח 10:17; ח ׳ארחות 2:19; (ה)חַיִּים דֶּרֶךְ 6:23; Jer 21:8; ח ׳חקּות Ezek 33:15; ח ׳תּוצאות Prov 4:23 sources (origin and direction) of life; ח ׳תוכחת 15:31; לַחַיִּים כָּתוּב Isa 4:3 written unto life.

3 sustenance, maintenance: לְנַעֲרוֺתֶיךָ חַיִּים Prov 27:27 maintenance for thy maidens, see מִחְיַה below

=== H2493 ===
H2493. chelem

=== H2654 ===
H2654. chaphets

חָפֵץ verb delight in (compare Arabic be mindful of, attentive to, keep, protect, Aramaic whence eager, zealous, Arabic anger (excitement), enrage (Aramaic and Arabic of excited attention, Hebrew of delighted attention), Dl^Pr 168 Nö^ZMG 1886, 742; Late Hebrew חֵפֶץ weakened to thing, see De^Ec. Glossary, Phoenician in proper name חפצבעל); —

Qal Perfect ׳ח Gen 34:19 28t.; feminine הָפֵ֑צָה Isa 66:3?; 2masculine חָפַצְתָּ Deut 21:14 4t.; חָפָ֫צְנוּ Job 21:14, etc., + 14 t. Perfect; Imperfect יַחְמֹּץ Deut 25:7 7t.; יֶחְמָּ֑ץ Psa 37:23; 147:10; plural יַחְמְּצוּ Isa 13:17; Jer 6:10; יֶחְמָּ֑צוּ Psa 68:31; יֶחְמָּצוּ֑ן Isa 58:2 (twice in verse) etc. + 9 t. 1 of men:

=== H2671 ===
H2671. chets

חֵץ noun masculine^1Sam 20:20 arrow, mostly in poetry and prophets (compare חֵצִי above below חצה) — absolute ׳ח Psa 91:5 9t. + 1Sam 17:7 Kt (Qr עֵץ q. v.); construct id. 2 Kings 1; 2Kin 3:17; 13:17; suffix חִצִּי Job 34:6, חִצּוֺ Psa 58:8; Zech 9:14, חִצָּם Psa 11:2; 64:4; Plural חִצִּים Gen 49:23 17t. + 1Sam 20:38 Qr (Kt חֵ֫צִי q. v.) + 2Sam 22:15 (< חִצָּיו || Psa 18:15); construct חִצֵּת Job 6:4; Psa 120:4 + Ezek 5:16 (Co חִצַּי compare ᵑ6 ᵐ5 Hi); חִצַּי Deut 32:23, 42 (Ezek 5:16 see above), חִצֶּיךָ Psa 38:3-4t.; חִצָּיו Isa 5:28 5t.; — arrow:

1 literally, shot from bow by hand 1Sam 20:20-21, (twice in verse); 20:22 (ᵐ5 We singular, i.e. הַחֵצִי, in 20:21; 20:22, see also Dr) 20:36; ⟦N174⟧ 20:38 Qr 2Kin 19:32 = Isa 37:33; 5:28; 7:24; Jer 50:9, 14; 51:11; Ezek 39:3, 9; 1Chr 12:2; shot from engine of war 2Chr 26:15; used in divination Ezek 21:26 compare 2Kin 13:15 (twice in verse); 13:17 (twice in verse); 13:18; חִצִּים בַּעֲלֵי = archers Gen 49:23.

2 figurative of Israel's weapons Num 24:8, of the (Messianic) king Psa 45:6, of Yahweh's judgments Deut 32:23, 42; Job 6:4 (compare חִצִּי = my wound 34:6), Psa 7:14; 38:3; 58:8 (but on text compare Che^critical note.) 64:8; 120:4 (? compare Che); further Prov 7:23; Lam 3:12; Ezek 5:16; in theoph. Psa 18:15 = 2Sam 22:15, compare Hab 3:11; Zech 9:14; Psa 144:6; of wicked men's violence 11:2; compare 91:5, under figure of lion's teeth 57:5; their words 64:4 compare Prov 25:18; Jer 9:8; simile, of strength and efficiency derived from one's children, Psa 127:4; of a deceiver's words Prov 26:18; metaphor of servant of ׳ח י ׳בָּרוּר Isa 49:2. — In 1Sam 17:7 read עֵץ (for חֵץ) as Qr and || 2Sam 21:19 = the shaft; see עֵץ; so Klo Dr.
