=== H3027 ===
H3027. yad

יָד noun feminine^Gen 25:26 + often (masculine Exod 17:12 see Di; on יָדַיִם יִרְמּוּ 2Sam 4:1; Zeph 3:16; 2Chr 15:7; Neh 6:9, see Ges^§ 145, 7 a, R. 1) hand (Late Hebrew id.; Aramaic יְדָא, ; Zinjirliיד DHM^Sendsch.Gloss; Arabic ; Sabean יד DHM^ZMG 1883, 343 Mordt^ib. 1879, 492; Ethiopic : (see Lag^BN 22); Samaritan ; Assyrian îdu, strength COT^Gloss; — der by most from √ לי֞, on ground of plural and suffix forms in cognate languages, see especially Philippi^ZMG 1878, 74 Ba^ZMG 1887, 637 but no trace of final י or ו in Hebrew, and meaning of such √ לי֞ not clear; Thes and others from ידה extend, throw, but this in Arabic Ethiopic פו֞, not פי֞, compare Hebrew Hiph`il; Philippi compare Arabic IV. strengthen; — Sta^§ 182, 183 regards יד as biliteral) — absolute יָד Gen 38:28 +; construct יַד 41:35 +; suffix יָדִי 14:22 +; יָדְךָ 22:12 + יָדְכָה Exod 13:16; יֶדְכֶם Gen 9:2 +; יֶדְכֶן Ezek 13:21 (twice in verse); 13:23, etc.; dual יָדַיִם Gen 34:21 +; construct יְדֵי 24:30 + וִידֵי Exod 15:17 +, etc.; feminine plural (in figurative senses) absoluteיָדוֺת Gen 43:24 8t.; יָדֹת 47:24 2t.; construct וִידוֺת 1Kin 7:32; suffix יְדֹתָיו Exod 26:19 (twice in verse) + 2t.; cstr יְדֹתֶיהָ 1Kin 7:35-36,.; יְדוֺתָם 7:33; —

1 hand of man Gen 3:22; 4:11; 8:9 + often; יָד תַּבְנִית Ezek 8:3 the shape of a hand (of God in Ezek,'s vision); of cherubim ׳ת אָדָם יַד 10:8, אָדָם יְדֵי דְּמוּת V:21:

a. right hand (וֺׅ יַדיְֿמִינ Gen 48:17 (JE), Judg 7:20; 2Sam 20:9; Jer 22:24; Ezek 39:3; Psa 73:23; 121:5; הַיְמָנִית[ם]יד Exod 29:20; Lev 8:23-24, 14:14, 17, 25, 28 (all P); a left-handed man is יַדיְֿמִינוֺ אִטֵּר Judg 3:15; 20:16 (V.אטר); left hand שְׂמאֹל(וֺׅ יַד 3:21; 7:20; Ezek 39:8; both hands of one person are denoted by dual, יָדַיִם Gen 27:22; 2Kin 3:11 + often; occasionally + numeral, יָדָ֑י שְׁתֵּי Deut 9:15, 17, so Lev 16:21 (P); dual also of hands of several persons Gen 5:29; Exod 29:10, 15, 19; Deut 31:29; 2Kin 11:16 2Chr 23:15; Ezek 21:12 +; yet sometimes singular of hand of several persons Gen 19:16; Exod 29:9; Lev 8:24; Deut 1:25; 17:7 (twice in verse); Judg 7:20 (twice in verse) +

b. ידיו כַּמּוֺת שְׁתֵּי 1Sam 5:4 the two palms of his hands; הידים כַּמּוֺת 2Kin 9:35; Dan 10:10; יָדֶיךָ אַצִּילוֺת armpits Jer 38:12; Ezek 13:18; יָדָיו בֹּהֶן Exod 29:20 (P) = his thumbs (opposed to רַגְלָם בֹּהֶן); ורגל(יוׅ יד(יו) בְּהֹנוֺת Judg 1:6-7, bracelets were worn on hands, i.e. wrists Gen 24:22, 30, 47 (J), compare thread bound on hand of Zerach 38:28 (twice in verse); 38:29-30, (J), and cords on hands of Samson Judg 15:14; the ring was worn on hand, i.e. finger Gen 41:42 (E), Est 3:10.

c.as to hands in use, note ידיו זְרֹעֵי Gen 49:24 arms of his hands, i.e. arms which make his hands serviceable; בַּקֶּשֶׁת יָדוֺ מִלֵּא 2Kin 9:24 he filled his hand with the bow, i.e. caused his hand to grasp it, seized it; עַלהַֿקֶּשֶׁת יָדְךָ הַרְכֵּב 13:16 (twice in verse); especially figurative of consecrating or installing (as priest), יַד מִלֵּא he filled the hand of any one (perhaps originally gave the selected portions of animal-sacrifices to, see Lev 8:25 ff, so Di), installed as priest Judg 17:5, 12. 1Kin 13:33 (followed by וְ subordinate); elsewhere only P and late: Exod 28:41 (|| קִדַּשׁ), 29:9 also 29:29 (|| משׁח), 29:33 קדּשׁ, 29:35; Lev 8:33; 2Chr 13:9; 16:33, followed by infinitive Lev 16:33 (|| משׁח), 21:10 (|| שֶׁמֶן יוּצַק), Num 3:3(|| משׁח); ליהוה יֶרְכֶם מִלֵּאתֶם2Chr 29:31 +?׳י, is addressed apparently to the whole congregation (otherwise Be Öttli, and Di Exod 32:29); so the same expression 32:29; so the same expression 32:29 (possibly) and 1Chr 29:5 (certainly) of offering gifts to ׳י; יָד֯ו מִלְאוּ Ezek 43:26 of consecrating the altar (|| טִהַר כִּמֶּר,); לְ יַד שָׁלַח Judg 5:26 stretch out hand to, so אֶלֿ ישׁלח 2Sam 6:6 (insert ידו Vrss Dr); hence יד(כ)ם מִשְׁלַח that to which one puts the hand, figurative, = undertaking, Deut 12:7, 18; 15:10; 23:21; 28:8, 20 ; יד שׁלח also in hostile sense, with בְּ, Exod 22:7; 1Sam 24:7; 24:11; 26:11; Psa 55:21 +; compare יָדָם מִשְׁלוֺחַ מוֺאָב Isa 11:14; אֶבֶןיָֿד Num 35:17 (P) a stone (thrown from) the hand; עֵץיָֿד כְּלִי 35:18 a weapon in the hand; יָד מַקֵּל Ezek 39:9 a staff in the hand; idols are וגו יְדֵי ׳מַעֲשֵׂה Isa 2:8 +?מַעֲשֶׂה; man is work of God's hand Job 14:15.

d. special phrases: kissing with the hand לְפִי יָדִי וַתִּשַּׁק Job 31:27; (in silence) פִי לְמוֺ שַׂמְתִּי יָדִי 40:4; Micah 7:16 (עַלמֶּֿה); the creditor is יָדוֺ מַשֵּׁה בַּעַל Deut 15:2; the debt כָליָֿד מַשָּׁא Neh 10:32; יֶדְכֶם תְּרוּמַת Deut 12:6, 11, 17 heave-offering of your hand; the hand is placed יָרֵךְ תחת in taking an oath Gen 24:2 (J); lifted (הרים) to ׳י 14:22; so perhaps יָהּ עַלכֵּֿס יָד Exod 17:16 (E) hand on the throne of Yah ! (but difficult, see Di VB); often with ׃נשׂא the hand is lifted (נשׂא) to heaven Deut 32:40 (of ׳י's oath, poem); elsewhere chiefly Ezekiel and P: simply lift (נשׂא) the hand (= נשׁבע), sq, infinitive Exod 6:8; Num 14:30 (both P), Ezek 20:28, 42; 47:14; followed by לְ person ⟦N126⟧ 20:5 (twice in verse) (strike out Co as gloss); followed by לְ person + infinitive 20:6, 15, 23; absolute 36:7; hence (citations) Neh 9:15; Psa 106:26; compare Neh 8:6 the people answered יְדֵיהֶם בְּמֹעַל אָמֵן אָמֵן; יד(יםׅ נשׂא elsewhere (of men) in prayer Psa 28:2; compare 68:32, and נִגְּרָה לַיְלָה יָדִי 77:3. Also of God, to give a signal Isaiah 49:29; to rescue 10:12. יָדוֺ יְנֹפֵף Isa 10:32 he brandisheth his hand (Assyrian, in defiance); יָדוֺ הֵנִיף 11:15 (of ׳י in judgment), compareIsaiah 19:16; Zech 2:13 (followed byעל); יָדוֺ הֵנִיעַ); Zeph 2:15 (in derision); ידלְ הוֺשִׁיעַ one's hand bringing deliverance to, gaining success, by force, for 1Sam 25:26 +? 25:31 (ᵐ5 We Dr); the hand is weary יָגְָֽעָה 2Sam 23:10, it cleaves (דבק) to the sword (אֶלהַֿחֶרֶב) 23:10 (both omitted by accident in || 1Chr 11:13, see VB); יָד שֶׁכֶר Lev 21:9 (H) fracture of hand = arm (|| רגל שֶׁבֶר); after נָתַן: give a pledge נְשֵׁיהֶם לְהוֺצִיא יָדָם וִיִּתְּנוּ Ezra 10:19; submit שְׁלֹמֹה תַּחַת יַד נָתַן 1Chr 29:24, i.e. they acknowledged him as their lord; ליהוה תְּנויָֿד; 2Chr 30:8; other phrase with preposition see below

e. of hand as strong, helpful, etc.: — (I) of man : יָדִי עֹצֶם Deut 8:17 (|| כֹּחִי); of fighting power of Edom חֲזָקָה בְּיָד Num 20:20 (J E; || כָּבֵד בְּעַם; compare below of God); קִצְרֵייָֿד small in power 2Kin 19:26 = Isa 37:27 (compare below of God); Israel went out of Egypt רָמָה בְּיָד according to P, Exod 14:8; Num 33:3 i.e. boldly, defiantly; same phrase of presumptuousness (against ׳י) 15:30 (P, compare רָ֫מָה יָדֵנוּDeut 32:27). Phrases of strengthening are: יָדָתו חִזַּק Judg 9:24; Neh 6:9; בְּיָדַיִם חִזֵּק Ezra 1:6; בְּיַדֿ החזיק Job 8:20 (see חזק); אִתּוֺ יָדָיו לִהְיוֺת 2Kin 15:19 that his hands might be with him, to confirm the kingdom in his hand; note also יִתָּקֵעַ לְיָדִי מִיהֿוּא Job 17:3 who is he (that) will strike his hand into mine, i.e. give me a pledge (see below תקע); strength fails when hands drop: יָדָיו וַיִּרְמּוּ 2Sam 4:1 then his hands dropped down, he grew feeble, spiritless (see רפה); יָדַיִם רְפֵי 17:2 weak-handed, weak (|| יָגֵעַ). (2) of (mighty) hand of God, pointing to earlier anthropomorphism; as strengthening Joseph Gen 49:24 (poem in J); as stretched out (שׁלח) to smite Exod 9:15 (J); so of י ׳מַלְאַךְ 2Sam 24:16, followed by accusative + לְ infinitive, opposed to יָדֶ֑ךָ הֶ֫רֶף 24:16; as smiting (בְּ נָָֽגְעָה) 1Sam 6:9; Job 19:21; as against (בְ among, in) cattle, etc., 19:3 (J); city 1Sam 5:9; the murrain 5:3 (J); יַדיֿהוה as heavy against (אֶל כבדה) 5:6; כָּֽבְדָה שָׁם 5:11; עַל קָֽשְׁתה 5:7 of withdrawal of ׳יs chastising hand מִכֶּם יָדוֺ תָּסוּר 6:3; and (׳י subject) מֵעֲלֵיכֶם אֶתיָֿדוֺ יָקֵל 6:5; of ׳יs power to deliver his people: — יָד חֹ֫זֶק Exod 13:3, 14, 16 (all J E) ; חֲזָקָה יָד 6:1 (twice in verse) (JE), 13:9 (JE), Deut 6:21; 7:8; Josh 4:24 (D); esp: in phrase נְטוּיָה וּ(בִ)זְרוֺעַ חֲזָקָה (בְּ)יָד Deut 4:34 + (see זְרוֺעַ p. 284); חזקה יד + גָּדוֺל כֹּחַ Exod 32:11 (JE), Neh 1:10; ׳י הֲיַד תִּקְצָ֑ר Num 11:23 (JE) is the hand of ׳י shortened ? Isa 50:2; 59:1 (both followed by מִן); נְטוּיָה יָד in judgment 9:11; 9:16; 9:20; 10:4; יַדיֿ ׳הָֽיְתָה עלי(וׅ of grasp of ׳יs hand in prophetic inspiration Ezek 1:3; 3:22; 37:1; 40:1; 2Kin 3:15; ׳יַדיֿ חזקה עָלַי Ezek 3:14; so הַיָּד֑ בְּחֶזְקַת Isa 8:11; of God's (׳י's) good hand = favour, (late) יַד על(יו) טוֺבָה אלה(יו) Ezra 7:9; 8:18; Neh 2:8, 18; without טוֺבָה Ezra 7:6, 28; 8:22, 31; followed by inf, לָהֶם לָתֵת האלהים יַד2Chr 30:12; לְגַדֵּל בְּיָָֽדְךָ 1Chr 29:12; in General בְּיָָֽדְךָ וּגְבוּרָה כֹּחַ 29:12; of protection יָדוֺ בְּצֵל Isa 49:2 in the shadow of his (׳י's) hand.

=== H3045 ===
H3045. yada

יָדַע verb know (Late Hebrew id., Aramaic יְדַע ; Phoenician ידע; Ethiopic II. I. indicate, announce, narrate; Assyrian idû, know, COT^Gloss; Sabean ידע, especially in compound proper name DHM^ZMG 1875, 612) —

Qal Perfect יָדַע Gen 4:1 +; יָדָ֑ע Lev 5:1 +; suffix יְדָעוֺ Deut 34:10; Job 28:7; יְדָעָהּ Gen 24:16; 1Kin 1:4; יְדָעָ֫נוּ Isa 63:16; 3feminine singular יָָֽדְעָה Judg 11:39 4t.; 2 masculine singular יָדַעְתָּ Gen 30:26 +; יָדַ֫עְתָּה 2Sam 2:26; יָדַ֫עַתְּ Ruth 2:11; 1Kin 2:15; יָדָ֑עַתְּ Jer 50:24; וְיָדַ֫עַתְּ consecutive Ruth 3:4 6t. (including Ezek 22:16, Co וִידַעְתֶּם, so ᵑ7 ᵐ5) + 28:22 Co (for וְיָדְעוּ), so ᵐ5; יָדַ֫עְתִּי Gen 4:9 +; suffix יְדַעְתִּ֫יךָ Exod 33:12 2t.; יְדַעְתִּיו Gen 18:19; יְדַעְתִּים Prov 30:18; יְדַעְתִּין Isa 48:7; יָָֽדְעוּ Gen 19:8 +; יָדָ֑עוּ 2Kin 4:39 +; יָָֽדְעוּן Deut 8:3, 16 (Dr^§ 6 n.); 2 masculine plural יְדַעְתֶּם Gen 44:15 +; 2 feminine plural יְדַעְתֶּן 31:6; וִידַעְתֶּן consecutive Ezek 13:21, 23, etc.; Imperfect יֵדַע Isa 7:16 +; יֵדָ֑ע Josh 22:22 5t.; וְיֵדָ֑ע Psa 138:6 (read probably יֵידָ֑ע see Ges^§ 69, 2 R, 3); וַיֵּ֫דַע Gen 4:17 +; suffix יֵדָעֶנּוּ Jer 17:9; וְיֵדָעֵם Hosea 14:10 Imperative דַּע Gen 20:7 10t.; דְּעֶה Prov 24:14; דָּ֑ע Ecclesiastes 11:19; suffix דָּעֵ֫הוּ 3:6; דְּעִי Jer 2:19 4t.; דְּעוּ Num 32:23 15t.; Infinitive construct דֵּעָה Exod 2:4; דַּ֫עַת Gen 3:22 +; דַּעְתִּי Deut 9:24, etc.; Infinitive absolute יָדוֺעַ Gen 43:7; Josh 23:13; יַדֹעַ Gen 15:3 11t.; Participle active יֹדֵעַ 3:5 +; feminine יֹדַ֫עַת Num 31:17 2t.; יֹדְעִים 2Kin 17:26 +; passive וִידוּעַ construct Isa 53:3; plural וִידֻעִים Deut 1:13, 15; —

1. a. know, learn to know, good and evil Gen 3:22 (J), followed by אֶתאֲֿשֶׁר 1Sam 28:9; הָעָם מִסְמַּר 2Sam 24:2; anything מְאוּמָה 1Sam 20:39; מְאוּמָה אִתּוֺ יָדַע לֹא Gen 36:6 (see II. אֵךְ

1. b), so Gen 36:8; כָּלדָּֿבָר 2Sam 15:11, subject God, knowing fowls Psa 50:11; way to wisdom Job 28:23; etc.; of bird of prey עָ֑יִט יְדָעוֺ לֹא נָתִיב 28:7; the price of wisdom 28:13; something future מוֺתִי יום Gen 27:2 (J); especially followed by clause 12:11 (J) I Know that thou art a woman of fair appearance, 22:12 (E), Eccl 1:17; ידעתי לֹא Gen 28:16; answering question Where ? without other object, 4:9 (both J); followed by accusative + בְּ, in, in the matter of (see בְּ 12b) גדוֺל אוֺ קָטֹן דָּבָר זֹאת בְּכָלֿ עַבְדְּךָ יָדַע לֹא 1Sam 22:15 thy servant knoweth not of all this anything, little or much; followed by בְּ (rare), gain knowledge of, learn of or about, with infinitive עֲלֵיהֶם בְּשׂוּםאֱֿלוֺהַּ הֲתֵדַע Job 37:15 dost thou know about God's enjoining upon them? also with substantive הָאֵלֶּה בַּדְּבָרִים אַליֵֿדַע אִישׁ Jer 38:24 let not a man know of these words; similarly followed by עַל, only עַלמִֿפְלְשֵׂיעָֿ֑ב הֲתֵדַע Job 37:16 dost thou know concerning the balancings of clouds? (in 12:9 בְּ is instrumental, as Gen 15:8; Psa 41:12); followed by מִכָּלֿ וּדְעוּ רְאוּ הַמַּחֲבֹאִים מִן, 1Sam 23:23 observe and get knowledge of all the hiding-places; but followed by object clause + מִן of source, כִּי מֵעֵדֹתֶ֑יךָ יָדַעְתִּי קֶדֶם Psa 119:152 long ago I gained knowledge out of thy testimonies that etc.; followed by לְ, have knowledge of, לְאִוַּלְתִּי יָדַעְתָּ 69:6 thou knowest my foolishness; especially אני כי וידע יהוה and he shall (thou shalt, etc.) know that I am Yahweh Exod 10:2 (R), 6:7; 14:4; 16:12; 29:46 (all P), Ezek 6:7, 10, 13 +? b. perceive Gen 19:33, 35 (J; object clause infinitive with בְּ); with added idea of observing, taking note of, a place, accusative Ruth 3:4; with נַפְשִׁי בְּצָרוֺת יָדַעְתָּ בְּ, Psa 31:8 (|| אֶתֿ רָאָה).

e. know by experience, כִּי וּבְכָלנַֿפְשְׁכֶם לְבַבְכֶם בְּכָלֿ וִידַעְתֶּם Josh 23:14 (D) and know in all your hearts, and all your souls, that, etc.; learn to know ׳י Hosea 13:14; learn (a bitter lesson) 9:7; Isa 9:8; Psa 14:4; experience ׳יs vengeance Ezek 25:14; Psa 14:4 (absolute); quietness Job 20:20.

2 know a person, be acquainted with Gen 29:5 (J), Exod 1:8 (E), Job 42:11, know ׳י 24:1; c לֹא, object especially other gods, strange land, etc. = have no knowledge of, or acquaintance with, have not heard of, especially Deuteronomy, Jeremiah: — Deut 11:28; 13:3; 13:7; 13:14; 28:64; 29:25; 32:17 (poem), Jer 7:9; 9:15; 14:18; 16:13; 17:4; 19:4; 22:28; 44:3; participle active acquaintance Job 19:13; passive participle acquainted with חֹ֑לִי וִידוּעַ מַכְאֹבוֺת אִישׁ Isa 53:3 (literally known of sickness, see Thes Ew Che Di Du); well-known (with implied trustworthiness) Deut 1:13, ⟦N093⟧ 15 ; of beasts knowing owner and master's crib Isa 1:3 (compare Jer 8:7); especially of knowing God ׳אֶתיֿ (י יָדַעְתִּי ׅ׳לֹא Exod 5:2 (J); involving intelligent worship, obedience, etc. Judg 2:10; 1Sam 2:12 (read יֵדַע for יָדַע ᵑ0 Dr), 3:7; Hosea 2:22; 8:2; 5:4; Job 18:21; Psa 79:6 (see also דַּעַת

2b); of God's Knowing persons etc., throughly Hosea 5:3; Job 11:11; knowing the heart 1Kin 8:39; 2Chr 6:30; Psa 139:4; knowing his true servants, recognising and acknowledgingthem 2Sam 7:20; Nahum 1:7; 1Chr 17:18 +; בְּשֵׁם יְדַעְתִּיךָ Exod 33:12 (JE); = take notice of, regardPsa 1:6; 31:7; 37:18; so of Israel as chosen people Amos 3:2; Hosea 13:5 (Assyrian idûšu, God regardeth him, Hpt^BAS i, 15) compare Gen 18:19 (J).

3 know a personcarnally, of sexual intercourse, followed by accusative: man subject Gen 4:1, 17, 25; 24:16; 38:26 (all J), 1Sam 1:19; Judg 19:25; 1Kin 1:4; woman subject Gen 19:8 (J), Num 31:17-18, 35 (all P), Judg 11:39; זָכָר מִשְׁכַּב יֹדַעַת 21:11; זָכָר לְמִשְׁכַּב אִישׁ יָדְעָה לֹא 21:12; man subject and object (of sodomy) Gen 19:5 (J), Judg 19:22.

4. a. know how to do a thing, be able to do it, often with לֹא and followed by infinitive נְכֹחָה עֲשׂוֺתֿ יָדְעוּ לֹא Amos 3:10 they know not how to do right; דַבֵּר יָדַעְתִּי לֹא Jer 1:6; 6:15; 1Kin 3:7; Isa 56:11 (twice in verse), with לְ 50:4; Eccl 10:15; לְהִזָּהֵר יָדַע לֹא 4:13 know not how to be admonished (Ecclesiastes 4:17 know that); infinitive absolute בַּטּוֺב וּבָחוֺר בָּרָע מָאוֺס ידע Isa 7:15-16,.

5 absolute have knowledge, be wise: לַֹיּדְעִים Eccl 9:11 (|| נְבֹנִים חֲכָמִים,); especially have knowledge of God and duty, or in practical affairs Isa 1:3 (|| בין), 56:10; often with accusative of congnate meaning with verb: דַּעַת Prov 17:27; 30:3; בִּינָה Isa 29:24; Prov 4:1; 2Chr 2:12, compare לָעִתִּים בִינָה יוֺדְעֵי 1Chr 12:33 (Baer), Est 1:13; also וָדִין דָּת יֹדְעֵי 1:13; מַדָּע וּמְבִינֵי דַעַת יֹדְעֵי Dan 1:4; object וּבִינָה שֶׂכֶל2Chr 2:11 (|| חָכָם בֵּן); וּמוּסָר חָכְמָה Prov 1:2 (|| בִּינָה אִמְרֵי הבין); חָכְמָה 24:14; Eccl 8:16.

=== H3068 ===
H3068. Yhvh

יהוה i.e. יַהְוֶה

proper name, of deity Yahweh, the proper name of the God of Israel — (

1 יְהוָֺה ᵑ0^C518 (Qr אֲדֹנָי), or יֱהוִֺה (Qr אֱלֹהִים), in the combinations יהוה אדני & אדני יהוה (see אֲדֹנֶי), and with preposition מֵיהוָֺה לַיהוָֺה, בַּיהוָֺה, (Qr מֵאדֹנָי לַאדֹנָי, בַּאדֹנָי,), do not give the original form. ᵐ5 and other Vrss follow the Qr. On the basis of Exod 20:7; Lev 24:11 יהוה was regarded as a nomen ineffabile (see Philo^de Vita Mosis iii. 519, 529), called by the Jews הַשֵּׁם and by the Samaritans שׁימא. The pronunciation Jehovah was unknown until 1520, when it was introduced by Galatinus; but it was contested by Le Mercier, J. Drusius, and L. Capellus, as against grammatical and historical propriety (compare Bö^§ 88). The traditional Ἰαβέ of Theodoret and Epiphanius, the יְהוֺֿ יָֿהוּ, of compound proper name and the contracted form יָהּ all favour יַהְוֶךְ (compare יַהֲלֹמ֑וּן Psa 74:6; תַּהֲרוּ Isa 33:11), see Lag^Sym i.14 Baud^Studien i.179 ff.; Dr^Stud.Bib.i.1 ff. For Jeve see Sta^ZAW 1881, 346 De^ib.1882, 173 f. & Gn. Excurs. ii.

2 on literature of interpretations see Nes^Eg.67 Dr^l.c. — Many recent scholars explain יַהְוֶה as Hiph`il of היה (=היה) the one bringing into being, life-giver (compare חַוָּה Gen 3:20) Schr HSch; giver of existence, creator, Kue Tiele; he who brings to pass (so already Le Clerc),performer of his promises, Lag, Nes^Eg.88 (but Nes^Eg.91 inclines to Qal as RS^Brit. & For. Ev. Rev see below); or from היה he who causes to fall, rain or lightning RS^OTJC.ed.1, 423; om.ed.2, 245, compare We^Skizzen iii.175; 'Fäller,' destroying foes, Sta^G.i.429 (dubiously). But most take it as Qal of הוה (= היה); the one who is: i.e. the absolute and unchangeable one, Ri; the existing, ever living, as self-consistent and unchangeable, Di; or the one ever coming into manifestation as the God of redemption, De Oehl; compare also RS^Brit. & For. Ev. Rev. 1876, he will be it, i.e all that his servants look for (compare Ew^infr), he will approve himself (give evidence of being, assert his being Dr^l.c.17)). **Theories of non-Hebrew or non-Semitic origin, opposed (in their older forms) by Bau^Rel. i. 181 ff. (see especially 230); Dl^Pa 162 ff. claimed Babylonian origin for יהו, against this Kue^national Religions, etc., Note iv (Eng. Trans. 329 ff.) Jastr^JBL xiil {1894}, 103 f. compare Hpt^BAS i. 170 N; Dl^Babel u. Bibel, 46 f., 73 f. makes same claim for יהוה, against this see especially Hirsch^ZAW xxiil {1903}, 355 ff. Zim^KATS. 465 ff.; Spiegelb^ZMG:liii {1899}, 633 ff. proposes (improbable) Egyptian etymology for יהוה; further discussions see in Kö^EB NAMES, § 112 and n.3. 'Jehovah' found in Jacob (? Johannes) Wessel († 1480), according to Schw^ThLZ, 1905, col. 612.

I. יהוה is not used by E in Genesis, but is given Exod 3:12-15 as the name of the God who revealed Himself to Moses at Horeb, and is explained thus : עִמָּ֑ךְ אֶהְיֶה I shall be with thee (3:12), which is then implied in אֶהְיֶה אֲשֶׁר אֶהְיֶה I shall be the one who will be it 3:14a (i.e: with thee 3:12) and then compressed into אֶהְיֶה 3:14b (i.e. with thee 3:12), which then is given in the nominal form יהוה He who will be it 3:15 (i.e. with thee 3:12). compare Ew^BTh ii. 337, 338 RS^l.c., Proph. 385 ff. Other interpretations are: I am he who I am, i.e. it is no concern of yours (Le Clerc Lag^Psalt.Hieron.156); I am (this is my name), inasmuch as I am (אֲשֶׁר = כִּי; AE JDMich We^JD Th xxi, 540 = compare Hexateuch 72); Di and others I am who I am, he who is essentially unnameable, inexplicable, — E uses יהוה sparingly by the side of אלהים and האלהים in his subsequent narrative. The Ephraimitic writers in Judges Samuel Kings use it in similar proportions. P abstains from the use of יהוה until he gives an account of its revelation to Moses 6:3; but subsequently uses it freely. He gives no explanation of its meaning. He represents that שַׁדַּי אֵל was the God of the patriarchs. J uses יהוה from the beginning of his narrative, possibly explaining it, Genesis 21:83 by עולם אל, the evergreen tamarisk being a symbol of the ever-living God; compare De Gen 21:33. Elsewhere יהוה is the common divine name in pre-exilic writers, but in post-exilic writers gradually falls into disuse, and is supplanted by אלהים and אדני. In Job it is used 31 t. in prose parts, and Job 12:9 (a proverb); not elsewhere in the poem. Chronicles apart from his sources prefers אלהים and האלהים. Daniel uses יהוה only in chap. 9 (7 t.); Ecclesiastes not at all. In the Elohistic group of Psalm 42-83 it is used 39 t. (see אלהים) . It occurs as the name of Israel's God MI^18. It is doubtful whether it was used by other branches of the Shemitic family, compare COT Gen 2:4b Dl^Pa 158 ff. Dr^Stud. Bib. i. 7 ff.

II. 1. יהוה is used with אלהים and suffixes, especially in D;

a. with אֱלֹהֶיךָ in the Ten Words Exod 20:2-12 (5 t.) = Deut 5:6-16; in the law of worship of J E, Exod 23:19; 34:24, 26; in D 234 t.; Josh 1:9, 17; 9:9, 24 (D^2); elsewhere Gen 27:20; Exod 15:26 (JE), Judg 6:26; Samuel & Kings 20 t.; 1Chr 11:2; 22:11-12, 2Chr 9:8 (twice in verse); 16:7; Isa 7:11; 37:4 (twice in verse); 41:13; 43:3; 51:15; 55:5; Jer 40:2 + (3t.); Hosea 12:10; 13:4; 14:2; Amos 9:15; Psa 81:11.

b. with אֱלֹהֵיכֶם in D 46 t.; D^228t.; H 15 t.; P 15 t.; elsewhere Exod 23:25 (E); 8:24; 10:8, 16, 17 (JE); Judg 6:10; 1Sam 12:12, 14; 2Kin 17:39; 23:21; 1Chr 22:18 + (10 t. Chronicles) Psa 76:12; Jer 13:16 + (5 t.) Ezek 20:5, 7, 19, 20; Joel 2:13 + (6 t.) Zech 6:15.

c. with אֱלֹהֵינוּ in D 23 t.; in D^25t.; Exod 8:6 (JE) 3:18; 5:3; 8:22; 8:23; 10:25-26, (E) Judg 11:24; 1Sam 7:8; 1Kin 8:57, 59, 61, 65; 2Kin 18:22; 19:10 = Isa 36:7; 37:20; 1Chr 13:2 + (15 t. Chronicles) Micah 4:5; 7:17; Isa 26:13; Jer 3:22 + (17 t.) Psa 20:8; 90:17 (?;Baer אֲדנָֹי); 94:23; 99:5; 99:8; 99:9 (twice in verse); 105:7; 106:47; 113:5; 122:9; 123:2; Dan 9:10, 13, 14.

d. with אֱלֹהֵיהֶם Exod 10:7 (J) 29:46 (twice in verse); Lev 26:44 (P) Judg 3:7; 8:34; 1Sam 12:9; 1Kin 9:9; 2Kin 17:7, 9, 14, 16, 19; 18:12; 2Chr 31:6; 33:17; 34:33; Neh 9:3 (twice in verse); 9:4; Jer 3:21; 22:9; 30:9; 43:1 (twice in verse); 50:4; Ezek 28:26; 34:30; 39:22, 28; Hosea 1:7; 3:5; 7:10; Zeph 2:7; Hag 1:12 (twice in verse); Zech 9:16; 10:6.

e. with אֱלֹהָיו Num 23:21 (E) Exod 32:11 (J) Lev 4:22 (P) Deut 17:19; 18:7; 1Sam 30:6; 1Kin 5:17; 11:4; 15:3-4, 2Kin 5:11; 16:2; 2Chr 1:1 13t. Chronicles; Micah 5:3; Jer 7:28; Psa 33:12; 144:15; 146:5; Jonah 2:2.

f. with אֱלֹהַי Num 22:18 (JE) Deut 4:5; 18:16; 26:14; Josh 14:8-9, 2Sam 24:24; 1Kin 3:7; 5:18; 1 Kings 5:19; 8:28; 17:20-21, 1Chr 21:17; 22:7; 2Chr 2:3; 6:19; Ezra 7:28; 9:5; Psa 7:2; 7:4; 13:4; 18:29; 30:3; Psalm 30:13; 35:24; 40:6; 104:1; 109:26; Isa 25:1; Jer 31:18; Dan 9:4, 20; Jonah 2:7; Hab 1:12; Zech 11:4; 13:9; 14:5.

g. with אֱלֹהַיִךְ Isa 60:9; Jer 2:17, 19; 3:13; Micah 7:10; Zeph 3:17.

h. with אלהים, probably always due to later editors, or to a Qr which has crept into the text Gen 2:4b — 3:23 (J, 20 t. either אלהים inserted by R^P as Di De; or יהוה inserted by J in an older source); Exod 9:30 (J, but not in ⅏ ᵐ5; Samaritan יהוה אדני; possibly ᵑ0 from earlier Qr, & ⅏ from later Qr); 2Sam 7:22, 25 (יהוה אדני ᵐ5 and 1Chr 17:20-23 only יהוה); 17:16-17, (but 2Sam 7:18-19, יהוה אדני) 1Chr 28:20; 29:1; 2Chr 1:9; 6:41 (twice in verse); 6:42; 26:18 (but in the original Psa 132:8 stood יהוה (so ℌ), or else no divine name); 72:18 (the late doxology) 84:12 (but it makes the line too long); Jonah 4:6. For the combinations with other divine names see those names.

2 the phrase יהוה אֲנִי is noteworthy: —

a. after אמר either alone Exod 6:2, 29 (P) or before relative and other clauses: Gen 28:13 (J) 15:7 (R) Exod 6:6 (P) with אלהיכם Judg 6:10; Ezek 20:5.

b. after כי ידע (α) Exod 7:17; 8:18; 10:2 (J); 7:5; 14:4, 18 (P); 1Kin 20:13, 28; Jer 24:7; Ezek 6:7 48t. Ezekiel; (β) with אלהיכם Exod 6:7; 16:12; Deut 29:5 (P) Exod 20:20; Joel 4:17; (γ) with אלהיהם 29:46 (P) Ezek 28:26; 34:30; 39:22, 28; (δ) before relative and other clauses Isa 45:3; 49:23, 26; 60:16; Ezek 7:9; 17:24; 21:10; 22:22; 35:12; 36:36; (ε) with various forms of קדשׁ Exod 31:13 (P) Ezek 20:12; 37:28; 39:7; (ζ) with דברתי 5:13; 17:21, compare י אני אשׁר ׳יֵדְעוּ 20:26.

c. after כִּי in various combinations Lev 11:44-45, Num 35:34 (P), Lev 20:7, 26; 21:8, 15, 23; 22:16; 24:22; ⟦N054⟧ 25:17; 26:1, 44 (all H); Exod 15:26 (R) Isa 41:13; 43:3; 61:8; Jer 9:23; Ezek 12:25; 21:4; Zech 10:6; Mal 3:6.

d. emphatic Exod 6:8; 12:12; Lev 26:2, 45; Num 3:13, 41, 45 (all P); Lev 18:5-6, 21; 19:12, 14, 16, 18, 28, 30, 32, 37; 21:12; 22:2-3, 8, 30, 31, 33 (all H) Isa 43:15; with אלהיהם Exod 29:46; with אלהיךָ Isa 48:17; with אלהיכם Lev 23:43; 25:38, 55; Num 10:10; 15:41 (twice in verse) (P) Lev 18:2, 4, 30; 19:2-3, 4, 10, 25, 31, 34, 36; 20:24; 23:22; 26:13 (all H) Ezek 20:7, 19; Joel 2:27; with מְקַדֵּשׁ Lev 20:8; 22:9, 32 (H), with דברתי Num 14:35 (P) Ezek 5:15 + (11 t. Ezekiel); with clauses Isa 27:3; 41:4, 17; 42:6, 8; 45:5-6, 7, 8, 18, 19, 21; 60:22; Jer 17:10; 32:27; Ezek 14:4, 7, 9; 34:24; יהוה אָנֹכִי is used in the Ten Words Exod 20:2, 5 = Deut 5:6, 9 cited Psa 81:11; Hosea 12:10; 13:4; elsewhere only Exod 4:11 (J) Isa 43:11; 44:24; 51:15.

=== H3117 ===
H3117. yom

יוֺם noun masculine^Gen 1:5 day (Late Hebrew id.; Aramaic יומָא, ; Phoenician ים; MI^5 ימן, suffix ימי 1:6; 1:9; Genesis 1:33, plural construct ימי 1:8, suffix 3masculine singular ימה; SI^3 ים; Zinjirli יום DHM^Sendsch Gloss; Plam. יום Vog^No. 123a, Oxon. iii.; Arabic ; Ethiopic Sabean ים יום, Mordt^ZMG 1876, 29, יומה DHM^ZMG 1883, 328 SabDenkm^61; Assyrian ummu, ûmu COT^Gloss; derivation unknown; on √ and relation of יום to Phoenician ימם, Aramaic ימם, , see Nö^ZMG 1886, 721 Ba^ZMG 1887, 632 f.) — יוֺם absolute 1:5 +; construct 2:4 +; suffix יוֺמְךָ Jer 50:31; יוֺמוֺ Exod 5:13 22t.; יוֺמָם Jer 50:27; Ezekiel 21:34; dual יוֺמַ֫יִם Exod 21:21; יוֺמָ֑יִם 16:29; Num 11:19; יֹמַ֫יִם 9:22; יֹמָ֑יִם Hosea 6:2 ; plural יָמִים Gen 4:3 +; יָמִם; Num 6:5; יָמִין (Aramaic form; Ges^§ 87, 1 a) Dan 12:13; יָמִ֫ימָה מִיָּמִים Exod 13:10 4t.; construct יְמֵי Gen 3:14 +; יְמוֺת Deut 32:7; Psa 90:15 ; suffix יָמַי Job 7:6 9t.; יָמָ֑י Gen 29:21+ 8 t.; יָמֵינוּ Jer 35:8 7t., etc.; —

1 day, opposed to night, Gen 7:14, 12; 8:22 (all J), 31:39-40, (E), Exod 24:18; 34:28; Num 11:32 (twice in verse); Josh 10:13 (all J E), Deut 9:9, 11, 18, 25; 10:10 (D), Gen 1:5, 14, 16, 18 (P), 1Sam 30:12; Judg 19:8-9, (twice in verse); 19:11; Amos 5:8; 1Kin 8:29; 19:8; Neh 4:16; Eccl 8:16; || בוֺאהַֿשֶּׁמֶשׁ לִפְנֵי 2Sam 3:35; הַיּ ׳חֹם Gen 18:1(J) the heat of the day 1Sam 11:11; 2Sam 4:5; ׳הַיּ גָּדוֺל עוֺד Gen 29:7 (J) the day is still high, not near its end; הַיּ ׳רוּחַ 3:8 coll of the day; הַיּ ׳מַחֲצִית עַדֿ מִןהָֿאוֺר Neh 8:3 from dawn until mid-day; so also probably of mid-day הַיּוֺם עַדנְֿכוֺן ואור הוֺלך Prov 4:18 growing lighter and lighter until the full day; note phrase in Judges 19, הַיּ ׳עַדנְֿטוֺת Judg 19:8 until the declining of the day; הַיּ ׳רָפָה לַעֲרוֺב 19:9 the day hath sunk down to become evening; הַיּ ׳חֲנוֺת 19:9 the declining of the day; ׳הַיּ מְאֹד רַד 19:11 the day has gone down exceedingly (is far spent).

2 Day as division of time:

a. working-day Exod 20:9-10, (E) = Deut 5:18; Exod 16:26, 30 (twice in verse) (J), ⟦N001⟧ 23:12 (JE), 31:15 (P), Lev 23:3 (H); הַמַּעֲשֶׂה יְמֵי Ezek 46:1.

b. יוֺם דֶּרֶךְ a day's journey Num 11:31 (twice in verse)( J E), 1Kin 19:4; אֶחָד יוֺם מַהֲלַךְ Jonah 3:4; יָמִים שְׁלשֶׁת דֶּרֶךְ a three days' journey Gen 30:36; Exod 3:18; 5:3; 8:23 (all J), Num 10:33 (twice in verse) (JE), 33:8 (P); יָמִים שְׁלשֶׁת מַהֲלַךְ Jonah 3:3; so seven days' journey Gen 31:23 (E), 2Kin 3:9; without דֶּרֶךְ etc., יָמִים שְׁלשֶׁת Exod 15:22 (E) they went three days, etc.

c. to denote duration of various other acts or states: seven days Gen 7:4, 10; 8:10, 12; forty days 7:17; 8:6 (all J); 150 days 7:24; 8:3 (both P), 1Sam 25:38; 1Kin 8:65 (twice in verse); Jer 42:7; Ezek 4:5-6, 1Chr 9:25; Ezra 6:22; Est 1:4 etc. In 4:16

1 &

2 are combined: וָיוֺם לַיְלָה יָמִים שְׁלשֶׁת וְאַלתִּֿשְׁתּוּ do not eat and do not drink for thee days, night or day (see Zech 14:7 below

3 below)

d. day as defined by evening and morning Gen 1:5, 8, 13, 19, 23, 31 (all P; compare further עֶרֶב בֹּקֶר,); see also 2:2 (twice in verse); 2:3 (P), Exod 20:11 (twice in verse)(E), 32:17 (twice in verse) (P).

e. day of month (with ordinal number), chiefly P and late: Gen 7:11; 8:4, 14; Exod 12:6, 18 (twice in verse) +, 1Kin 12:32; 12:33; Ezek 45:21; 45:25; Zech 1:7; Hag 1:1; 1:15; Haggai 1:18; 2Chr 29:17 (twice in verse); Ezra 3:6; Neh 8:2; 9:1; Dan 10:4; Est 3:12; 9:1 +; (יוֺם often omitted e.g. Gen 8:5, 13; Exod 12:10; 2Kin 25:1, 3; Ezek 1:1-2, Hag 2:1, 20; Zech 7:1; 2Chr 3:2; Ezra 6:19; Est 3:13, etc.)

f. יוֺם defined by substantive, infinitive, or other clause: (1) construct הַשֶּׁלֶג יוֺם = the snowy day 2Sam 23:20 = 1Chr 11:22; סַגְרִיר יוֺם Prov 27:15 = rainy d.; קָרָה יוֺם 25:20 = cold d.; (so, = time צָרָתִי יוֺם Gen 35:3 (E) d. of my distrees; Jer 18:17; Lam 1:7; Prov 24:10; 25:19; 27:10; מוֺתוֺ יוֺם etc. Judg 13:7; 2Sam 6:23; 2Kin 15:5 + often; compare אֶתמַּֿרְעֹה הֻלֶּדֶת יוֺם Gen 40:20 (E) = Pharaoh's birthday; הִוָּלְדָהּ כְּיוֺם Hosea 2:5 compare Eccl 7:1 (see also 7d below; compare יוֺמוֺ Job 3:1), חֲתֻנָּה יום Song 3:11; of day emphatically characterized by prophets and others וּמְבוּכָה וּמְבוּסָה מְהוּמָה יוֺם Isa 22:5 (see מְהוּמָה below הום); וּנְאָצָה וְתוֺכֵחָה צָרָה יוֺם 37:3 = 2Kin 19:3; on the other hand לי רָצוֺן ׳יוֺם Isa 58:5 a day of acceptableness to ׳י; plural followed by substantive שָׂכִיר יְמֵי Lev 25:50 (H) the days of an hireling; (מִלֻּאֵיכֶםׅ יְמֵי 8:33; Num 6:13; followed by rel. clause וגו ׳אִמִּי אֲשֶׁריְֿלָדַתְנִי יוֺם בּ֑וֺ יֻלַּדְתִּי אֲשֶׁר הַיּוֺם אָרוּר Jer 20:14 compare Job 3:3; also Psa 118:24 (see i).

g. particular days defined by proper name, of a location: יִזְרְעֶאל יוֺם Hosea 2:2 i.e. of judgement, with implied restoration; see also Isa 9:3; Ezek 30:9; הַגִּבְעָה יְמֵי Hosea 9:9; 10:9, i.e. of the outrage at Gibeah (Judg 19:22ff.); ירושׁלם יוֺם Psa 137:7 i.e. of Jerusalem's calamity, compare Jer 50:27, 31.

h. with suffix, thy, his, or their day, in sense of (1) day of disaster or death: Psalms יוֺמְךָ בָּא Jer 50:31 thy day has come; וָמֵת יָבאֹ יומו אוֺ 1Sam 26:10; יומו in this sense also Ezek 21:30; Psa 37:13; Job 18:20; יוֺמָם בָּא Jer 50:27; Ezekiel 21:34.

i. specifically a holy day: הַשַּׁבָּת יוֺם the sabbath day (see also שַׁבָּת), Exod 20:8, 11(E) = Deut 5:12, 15; Exod 31:15; 35:3 +; = קָדְשִׁי יוֺם Isa 58:13; repetition הַשַּׁבָּת בְּיוֺם הַשַּׁבָּת בְּיוֺם Lev 24:8 every Sabbath day; also הַכִּמֻּרים יוֺם 23:27; 25:9; כִּמֻּרִים יוֺם 23:28; הַבִּכּוּרִים יוֺם Num 28:26; חַגֵּנוּ יוֺם Psa 81:4; מַלְכֵּנוּ יוֺם Hosea 7:5; also of false gods, הַבְּעָלִים יִמֵי 2:11.

3 י ׳יוֺם day of Yahweh, chiefly as time of his coming in judgment, involving often blessedness for righteous (see RS^Proph. 396 f. Dr^Isaiah 28); Amos 5:18 (twice in verse); 5:20; Isa 2:12; 13:6, 9; Zeph 1:7 (compare 1:8), 1:14; 1:14 (compare 1:15; 1:16), Jer 46:10; Ezek 13:5; 30:3 (compare 36:33; 39:8, 11, 13); Obad 15; Zech 14:1; Malachi 3:23 (compare 14:7; Mal 3:2, 17; Malachi 3:19 (twice in verse); Malachi 3:21), Joel 1:15; 2:1, 11 (compare 2:2; 2:2), 3:4; Joel 4:14; י עֶבְרַת ׳יוֺם Zeph 1:18; עֶבְרָה יוֺם Prov 11:4; אַמּוֺ חֲרוֺן יוֺם Isa 13:13; Lam 1:12; נָקָם יוֺם Isa 34:8; 61:2; 63:4; נְקָמָה יוֺם Jer 46:10; י אַף ׳יוֺם Zeph 2:2-3, Lam 2:22; אַמּוֺ יוֺם 2:1; compare also Zech 14:7 (where senses of 1 and

2 are combined, compare Est 4:16 2c above); לי יִּוָּדַע הוּא ׳אֶחָד לָ֑יְלָה וְלֹא יוֺם לֹא יוֺםֿ וְהָיָה but there shall be one day, known shall it be of ׳י not day and not night; compare also ההוא ביום 7g below

4 Plural days of any one:

a. = his life, his age Gen 6:3 (J), Deut 22:19, 29; 23:7; Josh 24:31 (twice in verse) (D), Judg 2:7 (twice in verse); 2:18; 1Sam 25:28, etc.; Gen 5:4, 8, 11; 9:29; 11:32 (all P), Job 7:1 (twice in verse); 7:6, 16; 8:9, Psa 39:5 (twice in verse); 90:9; 90:10; 90:12; 90:14; 103:15; חַיֶּיךָ כָּליְֿמֵי Gen 3:14, 17 (J), Psa 23:6, so Prov 31:12; חַיִּים שְׁנֵי יְמֵי 2Sam 19:35; Gen 25:7; 47:8-9, (all P); חַיָּיו יְמֵי Eccl 5:17; 5:19; שָׁנָיו יְמֵי 6:3; שְׁנוֺתֵינוּ יְמֵי Psa 90:10; חַיֵּיהֶם יְמֵי מִסְמַּר Eccl 2:3; הֶבְלוֺ חַיֵּי יְמֵי מִסְמַּר 6:12 the number of the days of the years of his vanity, i.e. his empty, fleeting years; הָאֲדָמָה עַלמְּֿנֵי חַיִּים אֲשֶׁרהֵֿם כָּלהַּֿיָּמִים 1Kin 8:40; רַבִּים יָמִים long life 3:11= 2Chr 1:11; בַּיָּמִים בָּא advanced in days = of advanced age Gen 18:11; 24:1 (both J), Josh 13:1 (twice in verse) (JE), 23:1-2,(D), 1Kin 1:1; מִיָּמָיו 1:6 from his (earliest) days, so מִיָּמֶיךָ 1Sam 25:28 (see Dr), Job 38:12; יָמִים שְׂבַע 42:17; 1Kin 8:40; יָמֶיךָ יַאֲרִכוּן Exod 20:12 (JE) that thy days may be long = Deut 5:16, see further ארך; תּוֺסִיף עַליְֿמֵימֶֿלֶךְ יָמִים Psa 61:7 i.e. prolong the king's life; outlive is אַחֲרֵי יָמִים הֶאֱרִיךְ Josh 24:31(D), Judg 2:7; חַיִּים וּשְׁנוֺת יָמִים אֹרֶךְ Prov 3:2 length of days and years of life; יָמִים אֹרֶךְ 3:16; דבאך כְּיָמֶיךָ Deut 33:25 according to (the length of) thy life shall be [ thy strength ? read רבאך ?] see Di VB; rarely singular e.g. יוֺם קְשֵׁהֿ Job 30:25 one hurd of day, i.e. whose day (= life) was hard; of life as approaching its end, לָמוּת ישׂראל יְמֵי וַיִּקְרְבוּ Gen 47:29 (J) and the days of Israel drew near for dying, so Deut 31:14; 1Kin 2:1 (compare Job 7:6, 16; 8:9; Eccl 2:3 above)

b. (in) the days of (i.e. life-time, reign, or activity of) Gen 10:25 (J), 14:1; Judg 5:6 (twice in verse); 8:28; 2Sam 21:1; 1Kin 16:34; 21:29 (twice in verse); 22:47; 2Kin 20:19; 2Chr 13:20; 35:18 + often (so M 1:6. 8.8.9.33).

c. hence in phrase לְ הַיָּמִים דִּבְרֵי סֵפֶר 1Kin 14:19, 29 31t. Kings, Neh 12:23; Est 2:23; 10:2; הַיָּמִים דִּבְרֵי מִסְמַּר 1Chr 27:24; הַיָּמִים דִּבְרֵי הַזִּכְרֹנוֺת סֵפֶר Est 6:1.

5 Days:

a. indefinite: אֲחָדִים יָמִים some days, a few days Gen 27:44 (J), 29:10 (E), Dan 11:20; יָמִים alone Dan 40:4 (E) they were days (a certain time) in the prison 1Kin 17:15; Neh 1:4; יָמִים עוּל Isa 65:20 a suckling of (a few) days; מִיָּמִים Judg 11:4 after a time, 15:1; so יָמִים מִקֵּץ Gen 4:3 (J) after the end of days, 1Kin 17:7; יוֺמַיִם אוֺ יוֺם Exod 21:21(JE) a day or two. עָשׂוֺר אוֺ יָמִים Gen 24:55 (J) some days, or ten (days or a dekad; on question of text see Di).

b. of long time, שָׁנִים זֶה אוֺ יָמִים זֶה 1Sam 29:3 these days or these years; יָמִים אוֺ חֹדֶשׁ אוֺ יֹמַיִם אוֺ Num 9:22 (P) whether two days or a month or days (an indefinitely long period); רַבִּים יָמִים many days Gen 21:34 (JE) 37:34 (J) +; וגו הַיָּמִים ׳וַיִּרְבּוּ 38:12 (J) and the days were multiplied (= time passed) and Judah's wife died, 1Sam 7:2; הַיָּמִים שָׁם לוֺ אָֽרְכוּ Gen 26:8 (J) the days were long to him there = he had been there a long time; רַבִּים הֶעָנָןיָ֗֗֗מִים בְּהַאֲרִיךְ Num 9:19 (P) when the cloud prolonged many days (remained a long time) upon the tabern — See also 6 below

c. days of old, former or ancient times (especially of early period of Israel hist): עוֺלָם יְמוֺת Deut 32:7 (poem); עוֺלם יְמֵי Micah 5:1; 7:14; Isa 63:9, 11; קֶדֶם יְמֵי Micah 7:20; מִקֶּדֶם יָמִים Psa 77:6; 143:5; compare הָרִאשֹׁנִים הַיָּמִים Eccl 7:10; coming days הַבָּאִים הַיָּמִים 2:16; coming time אַהֲרוֺן יוֺם Prov 31:25; especially הַיָּמִים (ב)אַחֲרִית see above; הַיָּמִין לְקֵץ Dan 12:13.

6 יוֺם = time;

a. vividly in General sense (see also

5 above): time of harvest Prov 25:13; usually יְמֵי Gen 30:14 (J), Josh 3:15; Num 13:20 (twice in verse) (all J E), Judg 15:1; 2Sam 21:9; proper time for paying wages Deut 24:15 compare Job 14:6; time of parturition לָלֶדֶת יָמֶיהָ וַיִּמְלְאוּ Gen 25:24.

b. apposition to other expressions of time (Dr^§ 192 (1) Da^Synt. § 29. d): יָמִים חֹדֶשׁ a month of time Gen 29:14 (J; literally a month, time), Num 11:20-21, (JE); = יָמִים יֶרַח Deut 21:13; 2Kin 15:13; חדשׁים ארבעה יָמִים Judg 19:2 time, four months (si vera lectio, see 1Sam 27:7); יָמִים שְׁנָתַיִם (מִקֵּץ) Gen 41:1 (E) two years (of) time, 2Sam 13:23; 14:28; Jer 28:3, 11; יָמִים שָׁבֻעִים שְׁלשָׁה Dan 10:2-3,three weeks (of) time.

c. plural in specific sense, apparently = year, literal יָמִים 1Sam 27:7; Lev 25:29 (H); לַיָּמִים Judg 17:10; הַיָּמִים זֶבַךְ 1Sam 1:21; 2:19; 20:6; חדשׁים וארבעה יָמִים 27:7 = a year and four months (compare Judg 19:2 above

b.); יָמִ֫ימָה מִיָּמִים = from year to year, yearly Exod 13:10 (JE), Judg 11:40 (compare v^b), 21:19; 1Sam 1:3; 2:19; לַיָּמִים יָמִים מִקֵּץ 2Sam 14:26; אוֺיָֿמִים חֹדֶשׁ אוֺ יֹמַיִם Num 9:22 (P); distributive 14:34 (twice in verse) (P), Ezek 4:6 (twice in verse); שְׁנַיִם לְיָמִים הַקֵּץ צֵּאת וּכְעֵת מִיָּמִים לְיָמִים וַיְהִי2Chr 21:19 and it came to pass at days from days (=after some days) even about the time of the outgoing of the end of two (series of) days (i.e. probably years, see Be).

7 Phrases, without preposition and with, are:

a.-1הַיּוֺם = to-day Gen 4:14; 31:43, 48; <a href="http://bible

b. וַֹֿ הַיּוֺם וַיְהִי and the day came, that (or when) 1Sam 1:4 (see Dr), 2Kin 4:8, 11, 18; Job 1:6, 13; 2:1.

c. בָּאִים יָמִים הִנֵּה lo! days are coming, when, etc.; especially in Amos, Jeremiah: 1Sam 2:31; Amos 4:2; 8:11; 9:13; Isa 39:6 = 2Kin 20:17; Jer 7:32 13t. Jeremiah

d. יוֺם in construct def. verbs, both literally, the day of, and (often) in General sense = the time of (forcible and pregnantly, representing the act vividly as that of a single day): (1) before infinitive, (α) singular without preposition צֵאתְךָ אֶתיֿוֺם Deut 16:3 and thou shalt remember the day of thy going out from the land Egypt, Isa 58:5; Ezek 39:13; Mal 3:2 compare Eccl 7:1 (מִן comparative); (β) עַדיֿוֺם Josh 6:10; Judg 18:30 5t.; (γ) בְּיוֺם Gen 2:4, 17; 3:5 53t.; (δ) כְּיוֺם Hosea 2:5; 2:17; Zeph 3:8; Zech 14:3; (ε) מִיּוֺם ⟦N150⟧ 1Sam 7:2; 8:8; 29:6; 2Sam 13:32; 2Kin 8:6; Deut 9:24; Lev 23:15; Ezek 28:15; (ζ) לְמִיּוֺם Judg 19:30; 2Sam 7:6; Isa 7:17; (2) plural construct before infinitive: (α) כָּליְֿמֵי Judg 18:31; 1Sam 22:4; 25:7, 16; Lev 26:34-35, Num 6:6; 2Chr 36:21; (β) בִּימֵי Ruth 1:1; 2Chr 26:5; (γ) כִּימֵי Micah 7:15; (3) singular construct with preposition before finite verb in perfect: (α) compare י דִּבֶּר ׳בְּיוֺם בְּ, Exod 6:28 in the day (when) ׳י spoke, so Num 3:1; Deut 4:15; also Lev 7:35; 2Sam 22:1 = Psa 18:1; 138:3; Zech 8:9; (β) מִיּוֺם Jer 36:2; (4) singular construct before imperfect: אֶקְרָא בְּיוֺם Psa 56:10 in the day (when) I cry, 103:3; Lam 5:7; also without אִירָא יוֺם בְּ, Psa 56:4 (at the) time (when) I am afraid; (5) plural construct before perfect: אִתָּם הִתְהַלַּכְנוּ כָּליְֿמֵי 1Sam 25:15, so Lev 14:46 (probably, see Di); כִּימוֺת Psa 90:15; (6) plural construct before imperfect: יִשְׁמְרֵנִי אֱלוֺתַּ כִּימֵי Job 29:2; -7מִיּוֺם construct before rel. clause: לְפָנֶיךָ הָיִיתִי אֲשֶׁר מִיּוֺם 1Sam 29:8 since the day when (= as long as) I have been before thee, Neh 5:14; (8) plural construct before rel. clause יִשְׁכֹּן אֲשֶׁר כָּליְֿמֵי Num 9:18 as long as the cloud remained, etc.

=== H3162 ===
H3162. yachad

יַ֫חַד noun [masculine] unitedness. 1 as substantive only 1Chr 12:17 (peculiar) לֳי֑חַד לֵבָב עֲלֵיכֶֹם יִהְיֶהלִּֿי I will have a heart toward you for unitedness, i.e. my heart shall be ready to become one with yours (compare v.12:38 אחד לב).

2 elsewhere always in accusative as

adverb in union, together (compare Arabic in accusative with suffix in his solitariness = alone) —

a. together, of community in action, place, or time (often combined, but one usually more prominent than the others); (1) in action, Job 38:7 אֵל כּוֺכְבֵי בְּרָןיַֿחַד when the stars of God shouted together; Ezra 4:3 we together will build (opposed to you), with נִלְחַם 1Sam 17:10, נִשְׁמַּט Isa 43:26; (2) in place 1Sam 11:11 יָ֑חַד שׁנים בם נשׁארו ולא; 2Sam 10:15 יָחַ֑ד וַיֵּאָֽסְפוּ Psa 2:2; 31:14; 88:18Psalm 1יַחַד שֶׁבֶת Isa 50:8, as predicate Micah 2:12 אֲשִׂימֶנּוּ יַחַד; (3) in place and time at once. 2Sam 21:9 יָ֑חַד וַיִּמְּלוּ and they perished together, 14:16 to destroy יחד בני ואת אתי; (4) of time alone (poetry) Isa 42:14 יחד וְאֶשְׁאַף אֶשֹּׁם I will gasp and pant together, 45:8; Job 6:2; 17:16 **read נֵחָ֑ת for יַחַ֑ת; יַחַד here will then belong to (1).; Psa 141:10 אעבור עד אנכי יחד while I at the same time pass on.

b. all together, altogether (poetic synonym of כֻּלָּם but more forcible, suggesting often, especially with כל, all at once, as well as altogether), Isa 44:11 יחד יבשׁו יפחדו (compare Psa 40:15), 62:10 יַחַד מֵהֶבֶל הֵמָּה they are all together (made) of vanity, Isa 22:3 יחד נדדו קציניך כל (with כל also Psa 41:8; Job 34:15): often in poetry beginning a clause with emphasis, Deut 33:5; Job 3:18 שׁאננו אסירים יחד 16:10; 19:12 גדודיו יבאו יחד, 24:4; 31:38; Psa 41:8; 98:8; Hosea 11:7 (with a negative), 11:8; in connection with a suffix, or object of a verb, Isa 27:4 יָ֑חַד אציתנה I will burn it altogether, Psa 33:15 לִבָּם יַחַד הַיֹּצֵר who formeth the hearts of them all together, 74:6; 74:8; Job 10:8 סָבִיב יַחַד altogether round about (but read perhaps with ᵑ6 ᵐ5 Del Di תִסּוֺב אַחַר), 40:13.

c. together, in the sense of alike, the one as well as the other, Psa 49:3; 49:11 יאבדו ובער כסיל יחד Job 21:26; 34:29. More frequent is

יַחְדָּו (Jer 46:12, 21; ⟦N188⟧ 49:3 יַחְדָּיו),

adverb together (properly (in) his or its unitednesses (compare Arabic alone; and for the form אַשְׁרָיו), but, the origin of the term. being forgotten, applied Generally as an adverb, without regard to number, Gender, or person: so Ges Ol^§ 135c Sta^§ 370a) — together:

verb be able, have power prevail, endure (Late Hebrew id.; Aramaic יְכִיל; Assyrian akâlu, Hpt in KAT^2Gloss i,) —

=== H3282 ===
H3282. yaan

I. יַ֫עַן preposition and conjunction see below I. ענה.

יַעַן properly substantive, purpose, intention, but always used as

preposition or

conjunction, on account of, because (for יַעֲנֶה, of the form יִצְהָר etc., Sta^§ 259 Kö^ii. 403; compare mean, intend): — 1 as

preposition, rarely with a substantive, Ezek 5:9 תּוֺעֲבוֺתֵיכֶם יַעַן, Hag 1:9 מָה יַעַן, 1:9 בֵּיתִי יַעַן; with a participle Ezek 36:13 אמרים יען because of men saying to you (but read probablyאָמְרָם, Co Toy Berthol); frequently with infinitive with, 1Kin 21:20 הִתְמַכֶּרְךָ יַעַן on account of thy having sold thyself, Amos 5:11; Isa 30:12; 37:29 (= 2Kin 19:28) אֵלַי הִתְרַגֶּזְךָ יַעֵן, Jer 5:14; 7:13; 23:38; 48:7, and often. (c. 18 t.) in Ezek., as 5:7 (read הַמְרֹתְכֶם יַעַן), 13:8, 22; 25:3, 6, 8, 12.

2 As

conjunction:

a. אֲשֶׁר יַעַן (32 t.), usually with perfect, as Gen 22:16 זאת עשׂית אשׁר יען because that thou hast done this, Deut 1:36 (Josh 14:14), Judg 2:20; 1Sam 30:22; 1Kin 3:11; 8:18; Jer 19:4; 25:8; 29:23 (not Isaiah) +; with imperfect (in frequent. sense) Ezek 44:12.

b. כִּי יַען, ⟦N177⟧ Num 11:20 אֶתיֿ מְאַסְתֶּם כִּי ׳יַעַן, 1Kin 13:21; 21:29; Isa 3:16; 7:5; 8:6; 29:13.

c. יַעַן alone (23 t.), with perfect Num 20:12 בִּי הֶאֱמַנְתֶּם לֹא יַעַן, 1Sam 15:23; 1Kin 14:13; 20:42; 2Kin 22:19; Hosea 8:1; Isa 61:1; 65:12; 66:4; with imperfect (frequently) Ezek 34:21; of the future 12:12 because he shall not see, etc., (but ᵐ5 Co Kau Berthol לָעַיִן יֵרָאֶה לא אשׁר לְמַעַן): usually the verb follows immediately, but Ezek. sometimes puts the object first for emphasis, 5:11 טִמֵּאתָ אֶתמִֿקְדָּשִׁי יַעַן, 20:16, 24; 36:6, compare 34:21.

=== H3318 ===
H3318. yatsa

יָצָא verb go or come out (Late Hebrew id.; Aramaic יְעָא; Ethiopic , Assyrian âƒû Dl^HWB 237, all = go out, forth; Syriac go forth (in sense of germinate), grow; Phoenician יצא march out; compare SI^5 המוצא the source (of water); Sabean וצַא go out, DHM in MV; Arabic is be or become fair, beautiful, neat, clean, compare Nö^ZMG 1886, 725) — Perfect יָצָא Gen 10:11 92t.; 3 feminine singular יָָֽצְאָה Num 16:35 13t.; יָצָ֑אָה Isa 28:29; 2masculine singular יָצָאתָ Gen 24:5 7t.; 2 feminine singular וְיָצָאת Jer 31:4; 2masculine plural יְצָאתֶם Exod 13:3; Deut 11:10 וִיצָאתֶם Judg 21:21; Malachi 3:20; 3plural suffix יְצָאֻנִי Jer 10:20, etc.; Imperfect יֵצֵא Gen 15:4 64t.; וַיֵּצֵא 4:16 132t.; 3 feminine singular תֵּצֵא Exod 21:7 22t.; וַתֵּצֵא Gen 30:16 14t.; 2 masculine singular תֵּצֵא Deut 20:1 16t.; 3 plural יֵצְאוּ Gen 15:14 +; יֵצֵ֑אוּ 17:6 +; 3 feminine plural וַתֵּצֶ֫אנָה 1Sam 18:6; 2Kin 2:24; וַתֵּצֶאןָ Exod 15:20; 2feminine plural תֵּצֶ֫אנָה Amos 4:3, etc.; Imperative צֵא Gen 8:16 15t.; וָצֵ֑אָה Judg 9:29; feminine singular צְאִי Song 1:8; masculine plural Gen 19:14 11t.; צֵ֑אוּ Isa 49:9; Ezek 9:7 + Jer 58:8 Qr (Kt יֵצֵ֑אוּ) צְאֶינָה Song 3:11; Infinitive absolute יָצוֺא Gen 8:7 2t.; יָצאֹ 27:30 3t.; Infinitive construct צֵאת 24:11 31t.; suffix צֵאתִי Exod 9:29 3t.; צֵאתֵנוּ Josh 9:12; צֵאתְךָ Deut 16:3 10t., etc.; Participle יֹצֵא (יוֺצֵא) Gen 2:10 62t.; feminine singular שֶׁיֹּצָא Eccl 10:5; יֹצֵאת׳(יו ) Gen 24:15 11t.; יֹצֵת Deut 28:37, etc.; —

1 go or come out or forth:

a. from (מִן) a place, e.g. a tent Gen 31:33 (E; opposed to בְ בּוֺא), מוֺעֵד אֹהֶל מִמֶּתַח Lev 8:33; 10:7 (P), compare מִןהַֿמִּקְדָּשׁ 21:22 (H); from a house 2Sam 11:8, the doors (דלתי) of a house Josh 2:19 (JE), Judg 11:31; ביתו מִפֶתח Exod 12:22 (JE), from a camp 1Sam 13:17; 2Kin 7:12, a city Gen 19:14 (J), 12:4 (P), 1Kin 11:29; 20:17; Micah 4:10, a cave 1Sam 24:9, the ark Gen 9:18 (J), 8:16, 19 (both P); out of vineyards Judg 21:21; followed by accusative אֶתהָֿעִיר Gen 44:4 (E), Exod 9:29 (J); with בְּ also, of gate Jer 17:19; Neh 2:13; followed by accusative local. פָ֑תַח אֵצֵא לֹא Job 31:34; participle construct הֵַתּבָה יֹצְאֵי Gen 9:10 (P), עִירוֺ שַׁעַר יֹצְאֵי 34:24 (P); absolute Josh 2:5 (JE), Judg 3:24; Exod 34:34 (P), Num 33:3 (P; Israel going out [from Egypt]), 2 h 26:20 +; specify., of going out (מִן) from a land (of emigration) Gen 10:11, 14; 24:5 (all J); patric. of coming forth from (the land of) Egypt (the Exodus) Exod 12:41 (J), 13:3, 8; 23:15; 34:18; Num 11:20; 22:5 (all J E), and especially D, Deut 4:45-46, 9:7; 11:10; 16:3 (twice in verse); 16:6; 23:5; 24:9; 25:17; Josh 2:10; 5:4-5, 6 (all D), Num 33:1 (P), 1Kin 6:1; 8:9 2Chr 5:10; 2Kin 21:15; Jer 7:25; Hag 2:5; Psa 114:1.

b go forth from (the presence of) a person: Exod 8:26; 9:33; 10:6 (all followed by מֵעִם), Gen 44:28; Exod 5:20; Jer 2:37 (מֵאֵת), Judg 3:19 (מֵעָלָיו); followed by מִלִּפְנֵי Gen 4:16 (J), 41:46; 47:10; Est 8:15; Eccl 10:5; followed by מְּנֵי מֵעִם Job 1:12, מְּנֵי מֵאֵת 2:7; followed by suffix יְצָאֻנִי Jer 10:20 (see De Eccl 7:18).

c. in technical senses: absolute go forth, of emancipation Exod 21:2-3,(twice in verse); 21:4-5, 7 (3 t. in verse); 21:11 (all J E), compare יַדאֲֿרָם מִתַּחַת יצא 2Kin 13:5; of release in the year of jubilee, land, etc. Lev 25:28, 30, 31, 33 (all H), 27:21 (P); also of a debtor-slave 25:54 (H); suffix divorce מִבֵּיתוֺ וְיָָֽצְאָה Deut 24:2; of condemnation רָשָׁע יֵצֵא בְּהִשָּֽׁפְטוֺ ⟦N182⟧ Psa 109:7 when he is judged let him go out as a criminal, i.e. be condemned: of bowels (מֵעִים) falling out by reason of disease 2Chr 21:15, 19.

d. of flight, involving escape: followed by הַחוּ֫צָה, || נוּס, flee Gen 39:12, 15 (J), תֵצֵא נָצאֹ כִּי Jer 48:9; opposed to taken (by lot) 1Sam 14:41; מִמֶּ֑נָּה לָצֵאת לֹאיֿוּכְלוּ אֲשֶׁר רָעָה Jer 11:11 calamity from which they shall not be able to escape; perhaps also אֶתכֻּֿלָּם יֵצֵא אֱלֹהִים יְרֵא Eccl 7:18 he that feareth God shall escape (or be freed from, see De Hi-Now Mishna) all of them (see VB), — on accusative with יצא see De.

=== H3320 ===
H3320. yatsab

[יָצַב] verb only

Hithpa`el set or station oneself, take one's stand (יצב ᵑ7 Pa`el & derivatives; Arabic be constant, firm) —

Hithpa`el Perfect3plural הִתְיַצְּבוּ2Chr 11:13; ׳וְהִת consecutive Num 11:16; Imperfect יִתְיַצֵּב Deut 7:24 6t.; יִתְיַצָּ֑ב Job 41:2; יִתְיַצַּ֑ב Prov 22:29; וַיִּתְיַצֵּב 1Sam 3:10 5t.; 3 feminine singular וַתֵּתַצֵּב Exod 2:4 (but read וַתִּתְיַצֵּב compare Sam Ges^§ 71 Kö^i, 430); 2 masculine singular תִּתְיַצֵּב 2Sam 18:13; וְאֶתְיַצְּבָה Hab 2:1; יִתְיַצְּבוּ Psa 2:2 2t.; וַיִּתְיַצְּבוּ Exod 19:17 4t.; Imperative הִתְיַצֵּב 8:16 5t.: הִתְיַצְּבוּ 1Sam 10:19 6t.; infinitive construct (מֵ)הִתְיַצֵּב 2Sam 21:5 4t.; — station oneself, take one's stand, stand:

b. absolute 1Sam 3:10 (׳י subject); especially of standing quiet and passive, to see the mighty deliverance of ׳י Exod 14:13 (J), 1Sam 12:7, ⟦N130⟧ 16; 2Chr 20:17 (|| עמד); of taking a stand to fight 1Sam 17:16; Psa 2:2; in military array Jer 46:4 (with ב accompare), 46:14 (|| לָךְ הָכֵן); to answer a charge Job 33:5; לְבוּשׁ כְמוֺ וְיִתְיַצְּבוּ 38:14 and they (terrestrial things) stand forth (in the light) like a garment.

c. with לִפְנֵי person = present oneself before Exod 8:16; 9:13 (both J), Prov 22:29 (twice in verse); י ׳לִפְנֵי Josh 24:1 (E), 1Sam 10:19 (see also

=== H3374 ===
H3374. yirah

יִרְאָה noun feminine fear; — ׳יר Ezek 30:13 6t.; construct יִרְאַת Gen 20:11 27t.; suffix יִרְאָתִי Jer 32:40; יִרְאָתוֺ Exod 20:20; יִרְאָֽתְךָ Job 4:6 3t.; יִרְאָתֶ֑ךָ Psa 5:8 2t.; —

1 fear, terror Isa 7:25; Ezek 30:13; ׳י גְדוֺלָה great fear Jonah 1:10, 16; || מַּחַד Deut 2:25; || רעד(הׅ Psa 2:11; 55:6.

2 a terror = object of terror, || גֹּבַהּ Ezek 1:18 (strike out Co).

3 fear of God, reverence, piety אֱלֹהִים יִרְאַת Gen 20:11 (E), 2Sam 23:3; Neh 5:15; ׳יר שַׁדַּי Job 6:14; ׳יר אֱלֹהֵינוּ Neh 5:9; ׳יר יהוה2Chr 19:9; Isa 11:3; 33:6; Psa 34:12; Prov 10:27; 14:26-27, 15:16; 19:23; 22:4; 23:17; || the knowledge (of God) 1:29; 2:5; Isa 11:2; is the beginning of wisdom Psa 111:10; Prov 9:10, and knowledge 1:7; the instruction of wisdom 15:33, is to hate evil 8:13, and it involves departing from evil 16:6; ׳י אֲדֹנָי is wisdom Job 28:28; יִרְאָה is defined by context 15:4; with suffixes these are usually object, fear of him Exod 20:20 (E), of thee Psa 5:8; 90:11; 119:38; Isa 63:17, of me Jer 32:40, but subject, thy fear (i.e. thy religion) Job 4:6; ⟦N175⟧ 22:4.

4 יהוֺה יִרְאַת Psa 19:10 the fear of ׳י = the law as revered.

=== H3405 ===
H3405. Yericho

or Yerecho or Yerichoh יְרֵחוֺ יְרִיחֹה, יְרִיחוֺ,

proper name, of a location Jericho (on form see Baer on Josh 2:1; Jer 39:5 Kö^Einl. 49; √ and meaning dubious; according to Thes and others from רָוַח, = regio fragrans, the district abounding in palms, rose-gardens, balsam, etc.); — יְרִיחוֺ Josh 2:1 27t. Joshua. (19 t. J E, 3 t. D, 6 t. P), + 5 t. 2 Kings 2; יְרִחוֺ 18:21; 2Sam 10:5; Jer 39:5; 52:8; יְרִחֹה ⟦N170⟧ 1Kin 16:34; יְרֵחוֺ Deut 34:1 (P), 34:3 (D), Num 22:1 (P) + 9 t. P, 2Kin 25:5; Ezra 2:34; Neh 3:2; 7:36 3t. Chronicles; — Canaanite city taken by Joshua Josh 6:1 (JE) +; having a king 2:2-3, 8:2; 10:1 (all J E), 10:28, 30; 12:9 (all D); near Jordan, whence (יְרִיחוֺ) יְרֵחוֺ יַרְדֵּן, see יַרְדֵּן above; Mt. Nebo is described as יְרֵחוֺ עַלמְּֿנֵי Deut 32:49; 34:1 (P); (called הַתְּמָרִים עִיר Judg 1:16; 3:13; so in apposition Deut 34:3; 2Chr 28:15); the adjacent plain is called יְרֵחוֺ בִּקְעַת Deut 34:3 (JE); יְרֵחוֺ עַרְבוֺת 2Kin 25:5 = Jer 39:5 = 52:8; ׳ע יְרִיחוֺ Josh 4:13; 5:10 (both P); among returning exiles appear יְרֵחוֺ בְּנֵי Ezra 2:34 = Neh 7:36, and among builders of wall יְרֵחוֺ אַנְשֵׁי 3:2 (Be Ry Ryle on the passage and Ezra 2:34); — modern (E)rî µâ; see also GASm^Geogr. 266 ff.

ירך (√ of following; meaning dubious).

=== H3426 ===
H3426. yesh

יֵשׁ substantive being, substance, existence (on etymology see 2) — יֵשׁ Gen 18:24 etc.; יֶשֿׁ 31:29 + often; הֲיֵשׁ 24:23 18t. (so אִשׁ 2Sam 14:19 , הַאִשׁ Micah 6:10 ), with suffix יֶשְׁךָ Gen 24:42 2t.; יֶשְׁכֶם 24:49 ; הֲיִשְׁכֶם Deut 13:4 ; יֶשְׁנוֺ (Ol^§ 97 b Sta^§ 370 b) 29:14; 1Sam 14:39 (+ 14:41 ᵐ5 We Dr), 23:23; Est 3:8 ; —

1 substance, only Prov 8:21 יֵשׁ אֹהֲבַי לְהַנְחִיל to cause them that love me to inherit substance (so Sir 42:3).

2 elsewhere (properly as a substantive in the stative construct), it asserts existence, and so corresponds to the verb substantive, is (are, was, were, will be), literally the being, presence of... (so Biblical Aramaic אִתַי; Aramaic אית איתא,, [whence (self-) existent, essence, substance], with לָא contracted לֵית לַיִת,, ; Mandean לאיית עית, עת,; Arabic [with irregular ] (inflected as a verb, , , etc.; W^AG i. § 182, ii. § 42) is not ( is known only in two proverbial sayings, as a secondary form Fl^Kl. Schr. i. 146 f.); Assyrian išû, be, have Dl^HWB 310; with affix 1 singular la-a i-ša-a-ku, I have not, TP^i. 57 f.. On this word, see especially Nö^M. § 213, who exemplifies its different constructions in Semitic, and shews how it tends to pass into a verb; — (1) in Biblical Aramaic, Syriac, Babylonian ᵑ7, with possessive suffix; (2) in later Palestinian dialects, Jerusalem ᵑ7, Jerusalem Talmud, sometimes also in Babylonian ᵑ7 and Syriac, with independent personal pronoun (as הוא לית אנא, לית); (3) in Mandean, Babylonian Talmud, sometimes in Syriac and Arabic, and in Hebrew יֶשְׁנוֺ, with object. (verbal) suffix; (4) in Arabic, as a true verb. In Ethiopic literally therein, German es gibt, is similar in use (Di^Gr. § 167, l), though not of course in origin. In Hebrew the corresponding negative is אַיִן, q. v., the construction of which is quite similar) — is, are, was, were, etc., not, however, as a mere copula, but implying existence with emphasis (hence in English to be often represented by the substantive verb in italics):

a. with a predicate following, Gen 28:16 י ישׁ ׳אכן הזה במקום surely Y. is (emphatic) in this place! 44:26 אִתְּכֶם אָחִינוּ יֵשׁ אִם; Exod 17:7 אַיִן אִם בְּקִרְבֵּנוּ יהוה הֲיֵשׁ, Num 22:29 בידי חרב ישׁ לו Oh, that there were a sword in my hand! Deut 13:4 אֹהֲבִים הֲיִשְׁכֶם whether you do love, 29:17 אישׁ בכם ישׁ פן, 29:18; 1Sam 9:11f. and they said, Is the seer here? and they said יֵשׁ He is, 20:8; 23:23; Jer 27:18. Alone, in answer to a question (asked with יֵשׁ), He (it) is: 1Sam 9:12; 2Kin 10:15; Jer 37:17. But 23:26 (where ישׁ has no substantive or suffix) text must be corrupt: compare Gie.

b. absolutely, there is (es. gibt, il y a), Gen 18:24 בעיר צדיקים חמשׁים ישׁ אולי perhaps there are fifty righteous in the city, 24:23 ללון לנו מקום אביך בית הישׁ, 42:1 ׳במצ שׁבר ישׁ כי that there was corn in Egypt, Judg 4:20 אישׁ פה הישׁ, 2Sam 9:1; 2Kin 5:8 he shall know that there is (emphatic) a prophet in Israel, Ruth 3:12 there is a kinsman nearer than I, Psalm 58:12 surely there are gods judging on the earth: so in aphorisms, asserting the existence of a particular character, quality, etc., Prov 11:24 עוד ונוסף מפזר ישׁ, 12:18; 13:7, 23; 14:12; 16:25; 18:24; 20:15; Eccl 2:21; 4:8; 5:12; 6:1, 11; 7:15 (twice in verse); 8:14 (3 t. in verse); 10:5. In questions, or protestations, יֵשׁ often implies a doubt whether what is asked about is to be found or exists: 1Kin 18:10; Jer 5:1 and see אישׁ ישׁ אם if there is (emphatic) a man doing justice, etc. (compare Psa 14:2), Psalm 14:22; Isa 44:8 אלהים הישׁ is there a god beside me? Psa 7:4 בכפי עול ישׁ אם if there is iniquity in my hands! 73:11 is there knowledge in the Most High? Job 5:1; 6:30; Lam 1:2.

c. special phrases: — (a) after אִם and a participle, where an abiding intention is to be emphasized, Gen 24:42 דרכי מצליח אםיֿשׁךנֿא if thou art (really), prospering my way, 24:49; 43:4; Judg 6:36 (compare II. אין

2b). (b) לְ יֵשׁ = has (had), especially with pronouns לְךָ יֵשׁ לִי, יֵשׁ; etc., Gen 33:9 רָב יֶשׁלִֿי I have plenty, 33:11; 43:7 אח לכם הישׁ, 44:20 זקן אב לנו ישׁ, 1Sam 17:40 that they may know לישׂראל אלהים ישׁ כי that Israel has (emphatic) a god, 2Kin 4:2 מַהיֶּֿשׁלָֿכְי what hast thou ? Job 14:7 for a tree has (emphatic) hope (compare Ruth 1:12), Ruth 25:3; Ruth 28:1; Ruth 38:28 אב למטר ׃הישׁ Gen 39:5 (twice in verse); 39:8, * F f יֶשׁלֿו אשׁר כל all that had (39:4 without אשׁר, probably error). (c) with infinitive and לְ, is it possible to... ? 2Kin 4:13; לדברלֿך הישׁ can-1speak for thee to the king? 2Chr 25:9; so לְ אִשׁ 2Sam 14:19 (compare לְ אֵין, below II. אַיִן

5). (d)... אֲשֶׁר יֵשׁ (if) it was that... Num 9:20-21, there were some who... (with participle) Neh 5:2-3, ⟦N158⟧ 4 (compare Syriac for ὃ μὲν... ὃ δὲ ... Matt 13:8; PS^172). (e) יָדִי יֶשׁלְֿאֵל Gen 31:29 and elsewhere; see II. אֵל

7, p. 43. (f) 2Kin 10:15 וָיֵשׁ and (if) it be... (compare וָלֹא 5:17; 2Sam 13:26); so Judg 6:13 י ׳וְיֵשׁ אִתָּנוּ. (g) pleonastic יֵשׁ אֵין Psa 135:17. (h) יֵשׁ לֹא Job 9:33 (compare , : but ᵑ6 ᵐ5 Me and others לֻא). — As a rule, יֵשׁ precedes its substantive (from which, however, like אין, it may be separated: Gen 24:23; 43:7 אָח לָכֶם הֲיֵשׁ, 44:19-20, 1Sam 20:8 etc.); but occasionally, for greater emphasis, this is prefixed: 21:5 ישׁ קדשׁ לחם אם כי but holy bread there is! Isa 43:8 the blind people, ישׁ ועינים though it has eyes, Judg 19:19 לי ישׁ ויין לחם (compare אַיִן 2c).

=== H3427 ===
H3427. yashab

יָשַׁב verb sit, remain, dwell (Late Hebrew id.; Aramaic יְתֵיב, ; MI^10; 31 ישב, Judg 1:8, 19 וישב, 1:13 ואשב, dwell; Phoenician ישב dwell; Zinjirli ישב sit DHM^Sendsch. 58; Assyrian ašâbu, sit, dwell, Dl^HWB 244; Arabic leap, jump, Himyer. dialect sit, Lane^2919; Ethiopic II. 1 secum cohabitare facere, marry, consummate marriage, compare Hiph`il 4) —

Qal Perfect3masculine singular יָשַׁב Gen 13:12 +; 2 masculine singular יָשַׁבְתָּ Judg 5:16, נָ֑הּ וְיָשַׁבְתָּה consecutive Deut 17:14; 2masculine plural יְשַׁבְתֶּם 1:46; Lev 18:3, וִישַׁבְתֶּם consecutive 25:18 7t., etc.; Imperfect יֵשֵׁב 1Sam 5:7 +, יֵשֶׁבֿ Gen 44:33; Ezek 44:3, יֵ֫שֶׁב before monosyll. 1Kin 7:8; Job 22:8; וַיֵּ֫שֶׁב Gen 4:16 +; וַיֵּשֶׁב֑ Ruth 4:1; 1singular אֵשֵׁב Judg 6:18 +, וָאֵשֵׁב Deut 9:9 3t. + Ezek 3:15b (but Co אשׁר Kt); אֵשְׁבָה 1Sam 27:5; אֵשֵׁבָ֑ה Isa 49:20; וָאֵשְׁבָה Ezek 9:3; 3masculine plural יֵשְׁבוּ Gen 47:4 +; 3 feminine plural תישׁבנה Ezek 35:9 Kt (i.e. תֵּישַׁבְנָה Ol^§ 242 d Kö^i. 401), Qr תָּשׁוֺבְנָה (√ שׁוב), Co proposes תִּוָּשַׁבְנָה; 1 plural נֵשֵׁב Jer 42:13-14, וַנֵּ֫שֶׁב Num 20:15 5t.; Imperative masculine singular שֵׁב Gen 20:15 +, שֶׁבֿ 35:1; שְׁבָה 27:19 +; feminine singular שְׁבִי 38:11 +; masculine plural שְׁבוּ 22:5 +, etc.; Infinitive absolute יָשֹׁבֿ 1Sam 20:5; construct שֶׁ֫בֶת 7:2 +; שָׁ֑בֶת Isa 40:22 +; suffix שִׁבְתִּי 2Sam 7:5 +, etc.; Participle masculine י(וֺ)שׁב Gen 4:20; 24:3 +, etc.; feminine ישְׁבָה Nahum 3:8; י(וֺ)שֶׁבֶת Judg 4:5 +; י(וֺ)שָׁ֑בֶת Josh 2:5; 2Kin 4:13; ישַׁבְתְּ֯י Jer 22:23, י(וֺ)שֶׁבֶת֯י 10:17; Lam 4:21; Ezek 27:3 (Kt preferable in all these, see Ol^§ 123 d Ges^§ 90. 3 a); feminine plural ישְׁבוֺת 1Sam 27:8; —

1. a. sit on (עַל) a seat 1Sam 20:25, stone Exod 17:12, teraphim Gen 31:34, couch 48:2 (all E), Ezek 23:41, knees 2Kin 4:20, throne Exod 11:5; 12:29 (J), Deut 17:18; 1Sam 1:9; 4:13; 1Kin 1:13, 17, 20 +, see absolute below; of הָאֶרֶץ עַלחֿוּג הַיּשֵׁב ׳י, Isa 40:22; dust 47:1, ground (אֶרֶץ) Ezek 26:16, ashes Jonah 3:6 (these in token of humiliation); sit down by (עַל) a well Exod 2:15 or pool 2Sam 2:13; with אֶל 1Sam 28:23; sit in (ב) house, street, doorway, assembly, etc. 2Sam 7:1; Judg 19:15; Gen 38:14 (J), Jer 15:17; 26:10; Psa 1:1; Song 2:3 so Ezek 31:6, 17 (figurative); of No of Amon (personified city) בַּיְאֹרִים הַיּשְׁבָה Nahum 3:8 she who sate amid the rivers; with לְ sit on to (pregnantly) לָאָרֶץ Isa 3:26; 47:1; Lam 2:10, לְכִסֵּא Psa 9:5, at, בית לְפֶתַח Prov 9:14, לִימִינוֺ(יׅ 1Kin 2:19; Psa 110:1; לַמַּבּוּל 29:10; with לִפְנֵי Gen 43:33 (J), + שָׁם Judg 20:26; 21:2; 2Sam 7:18 = 1Chr 17:16; with עִם 1Sam 20:5; Prov 31:22; with אֶתֿ Jer 16:8; Job 2:13; with תַּחַת Judg 6:11; Micah 4:4, בַּצֵּל תַּחְתֶּיהָ וַיֵּשֶׁב Jonah 4:5; with מִנֶּגֶד Gen 21:16 (twice in verse) (E), נֶגֶד Isa 47:14, לָעִיר מִקֶּדֶם Jonah 4:5; מִצַּד 1Sam 20:25; Ruth 2:14; with accusative of congnate meaning with verb Ezek 28:2; לִשְׁמֹּט Exod 18:13; Joel 4:12 (compare Psa 9:5; Isa 28:6); hence absolute of sitting as king or judge Exod 18:14; Psa 61:8; Mal 3:3, יושׁבים Isa 10:13, perhaps Amos 1:5, 8, especially of ׳י sitting (enthroned), Psa 2:4; 9:8; 29:10; 55:20; 102:13; Lam 5:19, so in (הַ)כְּרוּבִים ישֵׁב 1Sam 4:4; 2Sam 6:2 = 1Chr 13:6; 2Kin 19:15; Psa 99:1; יִשְׂרָאֵל תְּהִלּוֺת ישֵׁב 22:4 (see with עַל, above); by meton. of thrones, for the judges sitting on them לְמִשְׁמָּט כִסְאוֺת יָָֽשְׁבוּ שָׁמָּה 122:5; הַיּשְׁבִים Ruth 4:14, i.e. in the gate (compare 4:11), those in whose presence purchase of land took place.

b. sit, sit down, absolute, Judg 19:6; Ruth 4:1-2, Neh 1:4; Jer 36:15 (followed by clause of purpose, to eat, etc.) Gen 37:25; Est 3:15 +; opposed to קוּם Exod 32:6 (JE) Psa 139:2, שֶׁבֶת מְאַחֲרֵי 127:2 (opposed to קוּם מַשְׁכִּימֵי); so Isa 37:28 = 2Kin 19:27 (read קֻמְךָ לְפָנַי at end of 19:27 = 19:26 We in Bl^Einl. 4, 257 Rs^Proph. 351, and n. 9); but also שְׁבִי קוּמִי Isa 52:2, explained by (אֶלהַֿמִּטָּה וַיֵּשֶׁב מֵהָאָרֶץ וַיָּקָם 1Sam 28:23 and) 2Sam 19:9.

c. sit down outside (חוּץ), i.e. perform a necessity of nature Deut 23:14.

d. sit = be set (as a jewel), עַלמִֿלֵּאת ישְׁבוֺתּ Song 5:12 set on a filling (i.e. in a setting, De and others), in description of eyes; (> others sitting by full streams).

2. a. remain, stay, tarry (for a limited or indefinite time), with אֶת person Gen 24:55 (J), Judg 19:4; 2Sam 16:8; with עִם person Gen 27:44 (J), עִמָּדִי 29:19 (E), Judg 17:10; with בְ local 1Sam 7:2 (of ark), 13:16; 14:2; 24:4 (23:25 read אֲשֶׁר for ᵐ5 וַיֵּשֶׁב We Dr Klo Kit Bu), 2Sam 10:5; 19:25 (where read שִׁבְתּוֺ for שִׁיבָתוֺ Dr Klo Bu, see We), Num 35:25 (P), Job 24:13; with שָׁם 1Kin 11:16, עַדעֿוֺלָם שָׁם 1Sam 1:22; with על (by) 25:13; 30:24; מֹּה 2Kin 7:4; הַחֲמוֺר עִםֿ מֹּה לָכֶם שְׁבוּ Gen 22:5 (E); with בֵּין Judg 5:16, לְ מִחוּץ Lev 14:8 (P),אֵצֶל 1Sam 20:19; with accusative בית 2Sam 6:11; 13:20; Ruth 2:7; absolute 1Sam 1:23 (twice in verse); abide, endure Micah 5:3, תֵּשֵׁב לְעוֺלָם וְיהוּדָה Joel 4:20, so of Mt. Zion Psa 121:1.

b. with special emphasis on qualifying phrase, קַשְׁתּוֺ בְּאֵיתָן וַתֵּשֶׁב Gen 49:24 and his bow abode as a firm one (poem in J); 1Kin 22:1 and they continued three years without war; of woman remaining טָהֳרָה בִּדְמֵי Lev 12:4, וגו ׳עלדֿמי 12:5.

3 dwell, have one's abode in (ב) a land, city, house, etc., Gen 4:16; 13:7, 12 (twice in verse); 19:29; 24:37, 62; Deut 1:4; 3:2; Josh 20:6; 2Sam 7:6 + often (on 21:16 see בְנֹב ישׁבו, p. 444); in tents Jer 35:7, 10; 1Chr 5:10; figurative of justice (צְדָקָה) Isa 32:16 (|| מִשְׁמָּט וְשָׁכֵן); in the midst of (בְּקֶרֶב) Gen 24:3; Josh 13:13; 16:10; Judg 1:30, 32, 33; 3:5; so with בְּתוֺךְ Gen 23:10 (P); with עַל + הארץ, of God 1Kin 8:27 2Chr 6:18; of people Lev 25:18-19, 26:35 (H); with לְ Job 15:28; with בֵּין Gen 20:1 (E); dwell with (אֶת) 34:16, 22 (P), Exod 2:21 (E), Josh 15:63 (JE), Judg 1:16, 21; 17:11; אֶתמָּֿנֶיךָ יְשָׁרִים יֵשְׁבוּ Psalm 140:14; so with עִם Josh 20:4; Deut 23:17 (+ בקרב), Psa 26:4 (i.e. assoc. with); with יַחְדָּו Gen 13:6 (twice in verse) (J), 36:7 (P), Deut 25:5; גַּםיָֿ֑חַד שֶׁבֶת Psa 133:1; dwell in their stead (תַּחְתָּם) Deut 2:12 (twice in verse); 2:22-23, 1Chr 4:41; 5:22; absolute יְצָרָהּ לָשֶׁבֶת Isa 45:18 for dwelling he formed it (the earth); (thy) dwelling-place is שִׁבְתְּךָ מְקוֺם 1Kin 8:30 2Chr 6:21, also שׁ ׳מְכוֺן 1Kin 8:39, 43, 49 2Chr 6:30, 33, 39, so Psa 33:14; לְש֔ ׳מָכוֺן Exod 15:17; 1Kin 8:13 2Chr 6:2. Participle ישֵׁב = substantive, dweller, inhabitant, very often (c. 215 t.): e.g. אהלים ישֵׁב Gen 25:27 (J); often collective אהל ישֵׁב 4:20 (J), הָאָרֶץ ישׁב 34:30; 50:11 (both J), Exod 34:12, 15 (JE); also in poetry f., (as collective; see Dr on 1Sam 17:21) שָׁפִיר יוֺשֶׁבֶת etc., MI 1:11 (twice in verse); 1:12-13, 15; צִיּוֺן יוֺשֶׁבֶת Isa 12:6; Jer 51:35; similarly 10:17; Jeremiah 21:23; 48:19 (compare Lam 4:21) Zech 2:11; with בת in app. (Da^§28 R. 6), בַּתמִֿצְרַיִם יוֺשֶׁבֶת Jer 46:19, so 48:18; more usually plural הֶעָרִים ישְׁבֵי Gen 19:25 (J), compare Deut 13:14; הָאָרֶץ ישְׁבֵי Exod 23:21 (JE), compare 15:14-15, (poem in E); תֵבֵל ישְׁבֵי Isa 18:3, חָ֑דֶל יוֺשְׁבֵי 38:11, etc.; ישְׁבֶיהָ Lev 18:25; 25:10 (H), Num 13:32 (P); absolute יוֺשֵׁב (collective) Amos 1:5, 8; Isa 49:19; הַיוֺשְׁבִים 1Chr 9:2; also (strangely) הארץ ישְׁבוֺת 1Sam 27:8 = the populations of the land We Dr (elsewhere in this sense only feminine singular and in poetry, see above); frequently in phrase without in habitant (i.e. so that there shall be no inhabitants), ישׁב מֵאֵין Isa 5:9; 6:4; Jer 4:7; 26:9; 33:10; Jeremiah 34:23; 44:22; 46:19; 51:29, 37; Zeph 2:5; ⟦N092⟧ 3:6; בָּהֶן יוֺשֵׁב מֵאֵין Jer 48:9; ישֵׁב מִבְּלִי 2:15; 9:10; בָּהּ יוֺשֵב יִהְיֶה לאֹ 50:3; יוֺשֵׁב הֱיוֺתבּֿוֺ לְבִלְתִּי 51:62.

4 of a land or city, sit, abide seated in its place, figurative for be inhabited Jer 17:6, 25; 50:13, 39; Ezek 26:20; 29:11; 36:35; Isa 13:20 (|| תִּשְׁכּוֺן לאֹ), Zech 2:8; 9:5; 14:11; תַּחְתֶּיהָ יָָֽשְׂבָה 12:6; 14:10; Ezek 35:9 Kt is תישׁבנה, Qr תָּשׁוֺבְנָה; Co תושׁבנה, i.e. Niph`al תִּוָּשַׁבְנָה (compare 36:10), or Hoph`al תּוּשַׁבְנָה (compare Isa 44:26); of palace, יֵשֵׁב עַלמִֿשְׁמָּטוֺ וְאַרְמוֺן Jer 30:18.

=== H3444 ===
H3444. yeshuah

יְשׁוּעָה noun feminine salvation; — ׳יְשׁ Psa 119:155 18t.; יְשׁוּעָ֫תָה 3:3; Jonah 2:10; יְשֻׁעָ֫תָה Psa 80:3; construct יְשׁוּעַת Exod 14:13 4t.; suffix יְשֻׁעָתִי Job 30:15; יְשׁוּעָתִי Psa 62:2 12t., + 25 t. suffixes; plural יְשׁוּעוֺת 2Sam 22:51 6t.; יְשׁוּעֹת 2Sam 42:12 3t.; יְשֻׁעוֺת 2Sam 53:7; —

1 welfare, prosperity: ישׁעתי עברה כעב Job 30:15 as a cloud my prosperity passed away.

2 deliverance: לישׁועה לי והיתה and thou wilt be to me for deliverance 2Sam 10:11 = 1Chr 19:12.

3 salvation by God, primarily from external evils, but often with added spiritual idea: Gen 49:18 (poem), Isa 33:2; 52:7, 10; 59:11; 60:18; Jonah 2:10; Psa 3:3; Psalm 3:9; 14:7 = Psalm 53:7; 22:2; ⟦N069⟧ 35:3; 62:2; 69:30; 70:5 ( = תשׁועה 40:17), 78:22; 80:3; 91:16; 96:2 = 1Chr 16:23, Psa 106:4; 140:8; Job 13:16; with verbs of rejoicing בישׁועה 1Sam 2:1; Isa 25:9; Psa 9:15; 13:6; 35:9; חוֺמוֺת יָשִׁית יְשׁועָה Isa 26:1 salvation will he set as walls; compare phrase ישׁ ׳אֱלֹהֵי Psa 88:2, ישׁ ׳אֵל Isa 12:2, ישׁ ׳צוּר Deut 32:15 (song), Psa 89:27, compare וְישׁוּעָתִי צוּרִי 62:3; 62:7; ישׁ ׳יוֺם Isa 49:8; הישׁ ׳מַעַיְנֵי 12:3 wells of salvation; plural יְשׁוּעוֺת (intensive), of ׃י מָּנַי ׳יְשׁוּעוֺת Psa 42:6; the salvation of my face (person; ᵑ0 פניו error, ᵑ6 ᵐ5 פני), Psalm 42:12; 43:5; ישׁועות כּוֺס 116:13 cup commemorating saving acts (of drink-offering); further 119:123; 119:155; 119:166; 119:174; || צְדָקָה Isa 51:6, 8; 56:1; 62:1; Psa 98:2; 98:3; הָאָרֶץ עַדקְֿצֵה יְשׁוּעָתִי Isa 49:6 my salvation unto the ends of the earth; יְשׁוּעָתֶ֑ךָ בְּכָלאגּוֺיִם Psa 67:3 among all nations thy salvation.

4 victory: with עָשָׂה work victory 1Sam 14:45; Isa 26:18: elsewhere of victories wrought by Yahweh for his people Exod 15:2 (song) Isa 12:2; Hab 3:8; Psa 20:6; 21:2; 21:6; 44:5; 68:20; 118:14; 118:15; 118:21; phrases: יְשׁוּעוֺת פעל do victories 74:12; יְשׁוּעֹת חֹסֶן Isa 33:6 store of victories; יְשׁוּעוֺת מִגְדּוֺל 2Sam 22:51 Qr tower of victories (Kt מַגְדִּיל and so || Psalm 18:51); יְשׁוּעוֺת מָעוֺז Psa 28:8 stronghold of victories; י אֶתיְֿשׁוּעַת ׳רְאוּ Exod 14:13 (J) see the victory of Yahweh, 2Chr 20:17; יְשׁוּעָה כּוֺבַע Isa 59:17 helmet of victory; בִּישׁוּעָה עֲנָוִים יְפָאֵר Psa 149:4 he will beautify the meek with victory. — compare the synonym תְּשׁוּעָה below

=== H3462 ===
H3462. yashen

[יָשֵׁן] verb sleep (Late Hebrew id.; Aramaic noun sleep; Arabic be sleepy, , sleep Assyrian šittiu, sleep, Dl^HWB 246, šunatu, šuttu, dream, Id^ib, Flood: iv. 22 Asrb^Annals,v R. 2, 97) —

Qal Perfect יָשַׁנְתִּי Job 3:13; וְיָָֽשְׁנוּ consecutive Jer 51:39 2t.; Imperfect יִישָׁ֑ן Isa 5:27; Psa 121:4; וַיִישַׁן 1Kin 19:5, etc.; Infinitive construct לִישׁוֺן Eccl 5:11; — sleep, go to sleep, and be asleep, Gen 2:21 (J), 41:5 (E), Ezek 34:25; Job 3:13; ⟦N190⟧ Psa 3:6; Psalm 4:9; Prov 4:16; Eccl 5:11, also 2Sam 4:6 ᵐ5 We Dr Kit Bu, see חֹמֶשׁ; with לֹא, of Ass. army Isa 5:27 (|| נוּם); of Baal 1Kin 19:5; of אֲדֹנָי תִישַׁן לָמָּה עוּרָה ׳י, 1Ki 44:24; יִישָׁ֑ן לֹא 12:14 (|| נוּם); with accusative of congnate meaning with verb ׳ישׁ עוֺלָם שְׁנַת Jer 51:39, 57 (see שֵׁנָה), of death, compare הַמָּוֶת אִישַׁן Psa 13:4. Niph`al only in derivatives sense of become inactive or stationary; Perfect וְנוֺשַׁנְתֶּם consecutive Deut 4:25 and ye be grown old בָּאָרֶץ, i.e. (see Di Dr) have lost freshness of first impressions; of inanimate things, be old, stale , only Participle נוֺשָׁן יָשָׁן Lev 26:10 (H), feminine נוֺשֶׁנֶת צרעת 13:11 = old leprosy (P); these possibly denominative from יָשָׁן

Pi`el causat. Imperfect וַתְּיַשְּׁנֵהוּ Judg 16:19and she made him sleep.
